### [1] [Certifying Repeatable Entry-Replay Witnesses for COBOL-to-Java Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27272)
*Andras L. Ferenczi,Mariya Bessonov,Allwyn Ponnudurai,Chandrakanth R. Mamillapalli,Krishna K. Maddula,Krishna K. Lingamneni*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 8 pages, 9 figures, 4 tables

Selection reason: 面向 agentic 代码迁移的合成测试生成与确定性验证门控，并含变异注入检验 agent 静默引入 bug 的可靠性分析，直接可迁移。

TL;DR: 提出基于 entry-replay 认证与 surrogate COBOL 执行轨迹的合成端到端测试生成方法，在 33,429 行 COBOL 上达到 78.1% 分支覆盖和 95.7% 段落覆盖；849 个用例通过 projected-agreement 验证；检测并修复 61 个缺陷；播种 450 个缺陷后门控检出 47.6%，但漏检舍入与精度缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移后 COBOL 应用的比较测试需要合成数据，而 agentic 迁移难以高效生成高覆盖且可确定性验证的测试用例。

Method: 生成合成端到端测试用例，并通过 entry-replay 测试用例认证：在 surrogate COBOL 程序上观察代码执行轨迹，该程序利用按时间顺序记录的外部操作磁带 mock 外部交互；再用 Locksmith Loop 的 projected-agreement 验证生成的 Java 代码与 COBOL surrogate 在指定观测上的一致性，并通过播种缺陷评估门控检出能力。

Result: 在一个 33,429 行 COBOL 程序上生成 849 个合成端到端测试用例，覆盖 2,973/3,806 个源分支（78.1%）和 989/1,033 个 main-AST 段落（95.7%）；849 个已认证用例均通过 projected-agreement 验证；活动中检测到 61 个缺陷并修复 Java 生成器；播种 450 个缺陷后门控检出 47.6%，可检出执行代码中的删除写操作和删除段落调用，但无法检出舍入和精度缺陷。

Conclusion: 该方法能够生成高分支与高段落覆盖的合成端到端测试，并通过确定性验证支持 COBOL 到 Java 迁移缺陷检测；但现有门控对舍入和精度类业务逻辑缺陷不敏感，需要进一步增强。

中文摘要：为迁移后的 COBOL 应用生成用于比较测试的合成数据，是当今 agentic 迁移面临的一项重大挑战。我们之前的论文《Agentic Method for Deterministic Validation of Legacy Code Migration》提出了一种确定性 oracle 以促进此类迁移，即 Locksmith Loop。我们现在提出一种新方法，通过生成的合成端到端测试用例实现高分支覆盖和高段落覆盖；该方法借助 entry-replay 测试用例认证，观察一个 surrogate COBOL 程序的代码执行轨迹，该程序通过按时间顺序记录的外部操作磁带（chronological external-operation tape）来 mock 外部交互。我们将该方法应用于一个包含 33,429 行代码的超大型 COBOL 程序，生成了 849 个合成端到端测试用例，覆盖 2,973/3,806 个源分支（78.1%）和 989/1,033 个 main-AST 段落（95.7%）。使用 Locksmith Loop 的 projected-agreement 验证，生成的 Java 代码在所有 849 个已认证合成端到端测试用例的指定观测上均与 COBOL surrogate 匹配。在本次活动中，我们检测到 61 个缺陷，并相应修复了 Java 生成器。为测试编码 agent 是否会静默引入业务逻辑缺陷，我们向 Java 代码中播种了 450 个缺陷，例如删除写操作、删除段落调用和更改舍入方式，以验证门控方法能检测出其中多少个。门控检测到 47.6%，包括执行代码中所有删除写操作和删除段落调用，但无法检测舍入和精度缺陷。

Original abstract: Producing synthetic data for comparison testing a migrated COBOL application is a major challenge that agentic migrations face today. Our prior paper, the Agentic Method for Deterministic Validation of Legacy Code Migration, presents a deterministic oracle to facilitate such migrations, the Locksmith Loop. We now propose a novel method for achieving high branch and paragraph coverage through generated synthetic end-to-end test cases, by means of entry-replay test-case certification by observing the code execution trace on a surrogate COBOL program that mocks external interactions via a chronological external-operation tape. We applied our method to a very large COBOL program with 33,429 lines of code to generate 849 synthetic end-to-end test cases covering 2,973/3,806 source branches (78.1%) and 989/1,033 main-AST paragraphs (95.7%). Using the Locksmith Loop's projected-agreement validation, the generated Java code matched the COBOL surrogate on the specified observations across all 849 certified synthetic end-to-end test cases. During the campaign we detected 61 defects and fixed the Java generator accordingly. To test whether the coding agent could silently introduce business logic bugs, we seeded 450 defects into the Java code, such as deleted writes, deleted paragraph calls, and altered rounding, to verify how many of these would be detected by the gating methodology. The gate detected 47.6%, including all deleted writes and paragraph calls in executed code, but was unable to detect the rounding and precision defects.

</details>


### [2] [A Hybrid Information Flow Analysis Framework for Android Hybrid Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27288)
*Jyoti Prakash,Mikkel Baun Kjærgaard,Abhishek Tiwari*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 跨语言静态引导动态分析框架（调用图+类型效果系统），作为程序分析通用方法有参考价值但与代码 agent 优化关联较远。

TL;DR: MifDroid 是面向 Android 混合应用的静态引导动态分析框架，通过生命周期感知的每-WebView 调用图、桥接感知类型与效应系统和模块化 JavaScript 分析引擎，提升 Java-JavaScript 跨语言信息流解析率并发现真实漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android WebView 混合应用存在 Java 与 JavaScript 之间的跨语言信息流，现有单语言分析器无法分析；现有混合应用分析或仅构建桥接感知调用图而信息流分析有限，或依赖粗粒度假设，或只关注 Web 漏洞，缺少完整的跨语言信息流分析。

Method: 提出 MifDroid 静态分析引导的动态分析框架，包含生命周期感知的按 WebView 调用图、桥接感知的类型与效应系统以及模块化 JavaScript 分析引擎，并在 F-Droid 中 338 个使用 addJavascriptInterface 的应用上进行评估。

Result: 静态分析产生 444 个条目，引导动态阶段新增 662 个条目，将解析率从 36.5% 提升至 74.5%；为 115 个静态分析结果为空的应用产生已解析的桥接相关条目；报告 14 条存在漏洞的跨语言信息流，其中 5 条已被维护者接受并修复。

Conclusion: MifDroid 能有效补充静态分析、提高混合应用跨语言信息流解析覆盖，并发现可被维护者确认和修复的真实漏洞。

中文摘要：Android（混合）应用中的 WebView 接口使原生端（如 Java）能够与 Web 端（如 JavaScript）交换数据。这产生了现有单语言分析器无法分析的跨语言信息流通道。现有的混合应用分析只解决了部分问题：一些构建了桥接感知的调用图但提供的信息流分析有限，一些依赖粗粒度假设，一些仅关注 Web 漏洞。我们提出 MifDroid，一个静态分析引导的动态分析框架，具有生命周期感知的按 WebView 调用图、桥接感知的类型与效应系统，以及模块化 JavaScript 分析引擎。我们在 F-Droid 仓库中 338 个（总共 4,126 个）使用 addJavascriptInterface 的应用程序上评估了 MifDroid，这些应用启用了 Java 与 JavaScript 之间的主动通信。我们的引导动态阶段在静态分析产生的 444 个条目基础上增加了 662 个条目，将解析率从 36.5% 提高到 74.5%，为 115 个静态分析结果为空的应用产生了已解析的桥接相关条目。MifDroid 报告了 14 条存在漏洞的跨语言信息流，其中 5 条已被维护者接受并修复。

Original abstract: The WebView interface in Android (hybrid) applications enables the native side (e.g., Java) to exchange data with the Web (e.g., JavaScript). This creates cross-language information flow channels that existing single-language analyzers cannot analyze. Existing hybrid app analyses address only part of the problem: some build bridge-aware call graphs but provide limited information-flow analysis, some rely on coarse assumptions, and some focus only on web vulnerabilities. We present MifDroid, a static-analysis- guided dynamic-analysis framework with a lifecycle-aware per-WebView call graph, a bridge-aware type-and-effect system, and a modular JavaScript analysis engine. We evaluated MifDroid on 338 apps from the F-Droid repository (out of 4,126 total) that use addJavascriptInterface, enabling active communication between Java and JavaScript. Our guided dynamic phase adds 662 entries to the 444 produced by static analysis, raising the resolution rate from 36.5% to 74.5%, produces the resolved bridge-related entries for 115 apps that static analysis leaves empty. MifDroid reported 14 vulnerable cross-language information flows, five of which have been accepted and fixed by the maintainers.

</details>


### [3] [XRust: Detecting Logical Races in Rust Linux Drivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27325)
*Qingxiao Xu,Ze Sheng,Zhicheng Chen,Arjun Manikandan,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 自动构造操作对 harness 并用串行差分 oracle 检测逻辑竞态，测试用例自动生成与判定思路可迁移到 agent 验证。

TL;DR: XRust 利用逻辑先发生分析、自动操作对测试夹具和串行差分判定，在无需手工不变量的情况下检测 Linux Rust 驱动中的逻辑竞态，并在 Linux 7.3-rc2 中发现两个新竞态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rust 在 Linux 中提升了内存安全，但无法防止单独同步的操作以违反更高层不变量的方式交错，产生逻辑竞态；现有检测通常依赖手工规范和受控执行环境。

Method: XRust 基于 Logical Happens-Before 分析识别与逻辑顺序相关的共享状态访问，自动生成操作对测试夹具，采用串行差分 oracle 检测不符合任一串行顺序的并发执行，并用轻量级用户空间抽象在可复现调度下运行驱动代码。

Result: 在 Linux 7.3-rc2 上，XRust 覆盖十个驱动的 102 个 Rust 文件，识别 17 个共享对象，构造 1,654 个可执行测试夹具，发现两个此前未知的逻辑竞态；rnull 块驱动竞态获 Linux 维护者确认，修复已进入 Linux 块子系统树。

Conclusion: XRust 表明无需手工指定不变量即可自动检测 Linux Rust 驱动中的逻辑竞态，并已在实际内核代码中发现经维护者确认的真实缺陷。

中文摘要：Rust 正越来越多地用于 Linux 以提升内存安全性，但其保证并不能防止逻辑竞态：经过单独同步的操作仍可能以违反更高层不变量的方式交错执行。检测此类缺陷通常需要手工编写的规范和精心控制的执行环境。我们提出 XRust，一种无需手工指定不变量即可检测 Linux Rust 驱动中逻辑竞态的技术。XRust 使用逻辑先发生（Logical Happens-Before, LHB）分析，识别与逻辑顺序相关的共享状态访问，自动构造操作对测试夹具，并使用串行差分判定器来检测与任一串行顺序都不一致的并发执行。XRust 还使用轻量级用户空间抽象，在可复现的调度下执行驱动代码。将其应用于 Linux 7.3-rc2，XRust 在来自十个驱动的 102 个 Rust 文件中识别出 17 个共享对象，构造了 1,654 个可执行测试夹具，并发现两个此前未知的逻辑竞态。rnull 块驱动中的竞态已得到 Linux 维护者确认，其修复已被接收入 Linux 块子系统树。

Original abstract: Rust is increasingly used in Linux to improve memory safety, but its guarantees do not prevent logical races: individually synchronized operations can still interleave in ways that violate higher-level invariants. Detecting such bugs typically requires manually written specifications and carefully controlled execution environments.
  We present XRust, a technique for detecting logical races in Linux Rust drivers without manually specified invariants. Using Logical Happens-Before (LHB) analysis, XRust identifies shared-state accesses relevant to logical ordering, automatically constructs operation-pair harnesses, and uses serial differential oracles to detect concurrent executions inconsistent with either serial order. XRust also uses lightweight userspace abstractions to execute driver code under reproducible schedules. Applied to Linux 7.3-rc2, XRust identifies 17 shared objects across 102 Rust files from ten drivers, constructs 1,654 executable harnesses, and discovers two previously unknown logical races. The rnull block-driver race has been confirmed by Linux maintainers, with a fix accepted into the Linux block subsystem tree.

</details>


### [4] [Documentation Drift in AI-Generated Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27381)
*Will De Michele,Kla Tantithamthavorn*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: Accepted at APSEC 2026 (Early Research Achievements)

Selection reason: 大规模分析 AI 生成 PR 的文档同步行为及其与后续修复的关联，为 coding agent 行为评测与工作流改进提供实证依据。

TL;DR: 分析 29,669 个 AI PR 发现 63.2% 改代码不更新文档；未更新文档的文件后续更易被修复，且文档债务在 90 天内累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画 AI 编码智能体在真实软件仓库中的文档实践，评估代码变更是否同步更新文档及其对后续维护的影响。

Method: 基于 AIDev 数据集中 2,585 个仓库的 29,669 个 AI 生成 PR，度量同一 PR 内代码文件与文档文件的协同变更，并按任务类型、后续修复率、PR 规模、合并时延和 90 天内再文档化率进行比较。

Result: 63.2% 的 AI PR 修改代码但未更新文档；功能开发为 55.2%，重构为 77.5%。未文档化文件在 30 至 90 天内被同一文件修复的概率高 8.6% 至 11.7%；原始 PR 级比较因已文档化 PR 大 3.9 倍而相反，控制规模后反转。已文档化变更合并更快（中位数 40 秒对 184 秒），但合并多为自动化。90 天内未文档化文件再文档化率为 24%，低于已文档化文件的 48%。

Conclusion: AI 生成的代码贡献普遍存在文档缺失；控制规模后，未文档化代码更易在后续被修复，文档漂移与文档债务倾向于累积而非被重新审视，需关注 AI 代码贡献中的文档同步与维护。

中文摘要：AI 编码智能体现已大规模向软件仓库贡献代码，但其文档实践尚未被刻画。我们分析 AIDev 数据集中来自 2,585 个仓库的 29,669 个 AI 生成的拉取请求（PR），衡量文档协同变更：即代码文件修改是否在同一 PR 中伴随文档文件更新。我们发现，63.2% 的 AI PR 修改了代码但未更新任何文档。这一比例随任务类型差异显著：功能开发为 55.2%，重构为 77.5%。在 30 至 90 天窗口内，未记录文档的代码文件随后被同一文件修复的概率比已记录文档的文件高 8.6% 至 11.7%。原始 PR 级比较显示相反结果，因为已记录文档的 PR 大 3.9 倍，因而吸引更多修复。控制规模暴露后，该结果反转。尽管已记录文档的变更规模更大，其合并速度也更快（中位数：40 秒对 184 秒），不过该数据集中合并在很大程度上是自动化的。文档漂移还会累积：在 90 天内，未伴随文档变更的文件后续被重新记录文档的比率约为伴随文档变更文件的一半（24% 对 48%）。这表明文档债务倾向于累积而非被重新审视。

Original abstract: AI coding agents now contribute to software repositories at scale, yet their documentation practices remain uncharacterised. We analyse 29,669 AI-generated pull requests from 2,585 repositories in the AIDev dataset, measuring documentation co-change: whether code-file modifications are accompanied by a documentation-file update in the same PR. We find that 63.2% of AI PRs change code without updating any documentation. This varies sharply by task type: 55.2% for features and 77.5% for refactoring. Undocumented code files are followed by a fix to the same file 8.6 to 11.7% more often than documented files across 30 to 90-day windows. A raw PR-level comparison shows the opposite because documented PRs are 3.9 times larger and therefore attract more fixes. Controlling for size exposure reverses this result. Documented changes also merge faster despite their larger size (median: 40 seconds versus 184 seconds), although merging is largely automated in this dataset. Documentation drift also compounds: within 90 days, a file changed without documentation is later re-documented at roughly half the rate of a file changed with documentation (24% versus 48%). This suggests that documentation debt tends to accumulate rather than be revisited.

</details>


### [5] [Standards-Guided and Dependency-Aware Code-Level Documentation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27634)
*Tingxu Han,Wei Song,Ziming Li,Yuchen Chen,Weisong Sun,Zhenpeng Chen,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 结合静态调用图/依赖感知提示与 LLM-as-judge 准则反馈迭代改进的代码文档生成框架，反思式反馈与可复现评测可迁移。

TL;DR: CODEDOC将代码文档生成视为标准引导的软件工程任务，结合依赖感知结构化提示与交互式LLM评审，在十个Python仓库上以更少token生成更高质量文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码文档生成方法缺乏显式质量规范，依赖长上下文提示处理原始代码，且难以进行可复现评估；因此需要将代码级文档生成重新定义为由权威标准引导、可复用质量要求驱动的软件工程任务。

Method: 提出CODEDOC框架：通过静态调用图和AST分析识别代码实体依赖，构建融合局部代码上下文、仓库路径和权威标准/风格指南准则的依赖感知结构化提示；并引入交互式LLM-as-a-judge协议，按细粒度标准维度提供准则对齐反馈以迭代优化文档。

Result: 在十个开源Python仓库上，标准对齐评估显示CODEDOC相较RepoAgent和长上下文基线分别取得11.6%-17.0%和21.4%-29.6%的相对提升，且每个文档实体消耗的token约为长上下文基线的十分之一；人类研究显示LLM与人类判断显著一致（Spearman's rho = 0.72），相对结论在不同评判LLM上成立。

Conclusion: 代码级文档生成应作为标准引导的软件工程任务来开展；CODEDOC通过依赖感知提示和准则对齐评审，在质量、效率与评估可复现性方面优于现有方法。

中文摘要：为软件仓库自动生成高质量文档仍然具有挑战性：现有方法大多将文档视为不受约束的文本生成，缺乏明确的质量规范，严重依赖基于原始代码的长上下文提示，并且对可复现评估的支持有限。我们认为，代码级文档生成应当是一项由标准引导的软件工程任务，其中文档依据来自权威标准的显式、可复用质量要求进行生成和评估。我们将代码文档定义为对仓库函数和类的自然语言描述，并按源代码路径组织。我们提出CODEDOC，一个由标准引导且感知依赖的框架，它通过静态调用图和AST分析识别代码实体之间的依赖关系，并构建依赖感知的结构化提示，将局部代码上下文、仓库路径以及来自权威标准和风格指南的文档准则结合起来。我们进一步引入一种交互式LLM-as-a-judge协议，提供与准则对齐的反馈，以沿正式标准和实践导向标准中的细粒度维度细化生成的文档。在十个开源Python仓库上的实验表明，在标准对齐评估下，CODEDOC生成的文档质量高于RepoAgent和长上下文基线，相对提升分别为11.6%-17.0%和21.4%-29.6%，同时每个文档实体消耗的token数量约为长上下文基线的十分之一。一项人类研究证实，基于LLM的判断与人类判断具有显著一致性（Spearman's rho = 0.72），并且相对结论在不同评判LLM上保持成立。代码可在https://github.com/ControllableModel/CodeDoc获取。

Original abstract: Automatically generating high-quality documentation for software repositories remains challenging: existing approaches largely treat documentation as unconstrained text generation, lacking explicit quality specifications, depending heavily on long-context prompts over raw code, and offering limited support for reproducible evaluation. We argue that code-level documentation generation should instead be a standards-guided software engineering task, in which documentation is generated and assessed against explicit, reusable quality requirements derived from authoritative standards. We define code documentation as natural-language descriptions of repository functions and classes, organized by source-code path. We present CODEDOC, a standards-guided and dependency-aware framework that identifies dependencies between code entities via static call-graph and AST analysis and constructs dependency-aware structured prompts combining local code context, repository paths, and documentation criteria drawn from authoritative standards and style guides. We further introduce an interactive LLM-as-a-judge protocol that supplies criterion-aligned feedback to refine generated documentation along fine-grained dimensions from formal and practice-oriented standards. Experiments on ten open-source Python repositories show that CODEDOC produces higher-quality documentation than RepoAgent and a long-context baseline under standards-aligned evaluation, with relative improvements of 11.6%-17.0% and 21.4%-29.6%, respectively, while consuming roughly an order of magnitude fewer tokens per documentation entity than the long-context baseline. A human study confirms substantial alignment between LLM-based and human judgments (Spearman's rho = 0.72), and the relative findings hold across different judge LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/ControllableModel/CodeDoc.

</details>


### [6] [Enhancing Bug-Exposing Test Generation via Step-Level Credit Assignment and Self-Distillation from Valuable Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27694)
*Wenjie Gan,Zhaoxi Zhang,Jiahui Liang,Weikang Li,Wanyuan Wang,Li Zhang,Jian Dong*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 用步骤级信用分配与从“有价值失败轨迹”自蒸馏的 RL 测试生成，直接涉及 agent 训练信号与执行反馈学习机制。

TL;DR: SCALF 是一种过程监督强化学习方法，通过步骤级信用分配和从有价值失败轨迹中学习，提升大语言模型生成可暴露代码缺陷的测试的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的测试生成方法对所有推理步骤使用相同结果奖励，可能抑制有用推理并强化错误步骤，且未能充分利用找到触发缺陷输入但输出预测错误的失败轨迹。

Method: 提出 SCALF：从过程奖励推导步骤特定优势进行步骤级信用分配；并将暴露缺陷但预测错误输出的轨迹用于同策略自蒸馏，借助执行反馈学习其有价值的触发缺陷进展。

Result: 在三个基准上，SCALF 相比强提示和后训练基线，在联合成功率、输出正确率和触发缺陷率上分别最高提升 2.97、5.26 和 3.53 个百分点。

Conclusion: SCALF 通过过程监督强化学习有效改进测试生成，能更好利用步骤级信号和失败轨迹，提升触发缺陷与输出正确性的综合表现。

中文摘要：大型语言模型已展现出强大的代码生成能力，但其输出往往包含细微缺陷。自动修复此类代码需要信息丰富的测试：能够暴露错误行为的输入，以及与之配对的正确预期输出。近期基于强化学习的测试生成方法对所有推理步骤施加相同的结果奖励，这可能抑制有用的推理并强化有缺陷的步骤。它们也无法有效利用那些找到了触发缺陷的输入但预测了错误输出的轨迹，导致其已验证的触发缺陷进展未被充分挖掘。为解决这些局限，我们提出 SCALF，一种过程监督的强化学习方法，将步骤级信用分配与对有价值失败轨迹的定向学习相结合。SCALF 从过程奖励中推导步骤特定的优势，使每个推理阶段获得各自的学习信号。它进一步将暴露缺陷但预测错误输出的轨迹路由至同策略自蒸馏，利用执行反馈从其有价值的触发缺陷进展中学习。在三个基准上，SCALF 在联合成功率、输出正确率和触发缺陷率上分别比强提示与后训练基线高出最多 2.97、5.26 和 3.53 个百分点。我们的代码和数据集可在 https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SCALF-F4D1 获取。

Original abstract: Large Language Models have shown strong code generation capabilities, yet their outputs often contain subtle bugs. Automatically repairing such code requires informative tests: inputs that expose faulty behavior, paired with correct expected outputs. Recent RL-based test generation methods apply the same outcome reward to all reasoning steps, which can discourage useful reasoning and reinforce flawed steps. They also fail to effectively learn from trajectories that find bug-triggering inputs but predict incorrect outputs, leaving their verified bug-triggering progress insufficiently exploited. To address these limitations, we propose SCALF, a process-supervised RL approach that combines Step-level Credit Assignment with targeted Learning from valuable Failed trajectories. SCALF derives step-specific advantages from process rewards, allowing each reasoning stage to receive its own learning signal. It further routes trajectories that expose bugs but predict incorrect outputs to on-policy self-distillation, using execution feedback to learn from their valuable bug-triggering progress. Across three benchmarks, SCALF outperforms strong prompting and post-training baselines by up to 2.97, 5.26, and 3.53 percentage points in joint success rate, output correctness rate, and bug-triggering rate, respectively. Our code and datasets are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SCALF-F4D1.

</details>


### [7] [RELAY: Scaling Historical Build Restoration through Rule Reuse and Agentic Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27784)
*Olivier Nourry,Yoshiki Higo*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 规则复用加逐案 agent 升级的混合修复框架，规则优先、agent 兜底的调度策略与成本对比对 agent 工作流设计很有迁移价值。

TL;DR: 研究 OSS-Fuzz 历史构建的环境衰减，提出 Relay：LLM 生成可复用修复规则，未解决案例再交给智能体；规则优先的混合方法恢复更多构建且成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史软件重建依赖会独立变化的外部资源，导致环境衰减并影响漏洞基准与软件工程研究的可复现性。

Method: 分析 14,159 个 OSS-Fuzz 案例；提出 Relay，用 LLM 从代表性失败中推导并验证确定性修复规则，复用规则，并将未解决案例升级给逐案例智能体；在人工引导与全自动规则推导中评估，并与逐案例智能体比较。

Result: 69.7% 案例无法完成重建与验证，多数失败为少量复发症状；规则推导可全自动，复用规则以更少构建执行恢复与逐案例智能体相同数量的构建，但智能体可恢复规则无法处理的案例；先规则后智能体的混合方法恢复最多且成本低于单独智能体。

Conclusion: 复发失败可一次诊断并复用修复；规则优先、智能体兜底的混合框架在恢复构建数量和成本上优于单一方法。

中文摘要：漏洞基准和软件工程研究依赖于重建历史软件。然而，构建依赖外部资源（如基础镜像和软件包），这些资源会独立于源代码发生变化。在本研究中，我们在 14,159 个 OSS-Fuzz 案例上考察这种环境衰减。我们发现，69.7% 的案例不再能完成重建与验证，且大多数失败共享少量反复出现的失败症状。因此，一种反复出现的失败可以被诊断一次，并复用其修复。我们提出 Relay，一种混合框架：它使用 LLM 从代表性失败中推导并验证确定性修复规则，在无需模型调用的情况下复用这些规则，并将规则未能解决的案例升级给逐案例智能体。我们在人工引导和全自动的规则推导活动中评估 Relay，并将其与逐案例智能体方法进行比较。我们发现，规则推导可以完全自动化；可复用规则能以更少的构建执行次数恢复与逐案例智能体同样多的构建。然而，智能体能恢复规则无法恢复的案例。我们的结果表明，一种先应用规则、再将剩余案例升级给智能体的混合方法，比任一单独方法恢复更多构建，且成本低于单独使用智能体。

Original abstract: Vulnerability benchmarks and software engineering research depend on rebuilding historical software. Yet builds rely on external resources, such as base images and packages, that change independently of the source code. In this study, we examine this environment decay on 14,159 OSS-Fuzz cases. We find that 69.7% no longer complete reconstruction and verification, and that most failures share a small number of recurring failure symptoms. A recurring failure can therefore be diagnosed once and its repair reused. We present Relay, a hybrid framework that uses an LLM to derive and validate deterministic repair rules from representative failures, reuses them without model calls, and escalates cases unresolved by rules to a per-case agent. We evaluate Relay in human-guided and fully automated rule-derivation campaigns and compare it with a per-case agent approach. We find that rule derivation can be fully automated, and that reusable rules restore as many builds as per-case agents with fewer build executions. However, agents restore cases that the rules cannot. Our results show that a hybrid approach that applies rules first and escalates the remaining cases to an agent restores more builds than either approach alone and at a lower cost than using agents alone.

</details>


### [8] [Raise or Check? Predicting the Necessity of Additional Fuzzing for Continuous Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27828)
*Riku Kato,Tatsuya Shirai,Olivier Nourry,Yutaro Kashiwa,Kenji Fujiwara,Yasutaka Kamei,Hajimu Iida*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 用覆盖率与变更特征预测追加 fuzzing 的必要性以优化资源分配，风险感知的资源调度思路可迁移到测试/验证预算分配。

TL;DR: 提出RAISE框架，利用项目日粒度的漏洞风险预测优先对高风险项目日进行额外模糊测试，从而以更低成本检测漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续模糊测试存在漏洞暴露窗口长、计算成本高的问题，需要基于漏洞风险估计高效分配模糊测试资源。

Method: 构建结合OSV漏洞引入提交与OSS-Fuzz每日覆盖率及变更指标的数据集，以项目日为粒度进行二分类；采用随机森林等模型，并在968个漏洞上比较五种资源分配策略。

Result: 最佳模型（随机森林结合VCCFinder静态特征与动态模糊测试特征）ROC-AUC为0.74，显著优于随机基线；PatchCoverage等覆盖率指标最重要；Simple-Regression每检测漏洞花费91.60次额外模糊测试会话，约为Median启发式（171.89）的一半。

Conclusion: JIT漏洞预测结合覆盖率感知模糊测试特征能有效指导持续模糊测试资源的风险感知分配。

中文摘要：持续模糊测试被用于开源软件（OSS）中的漏洞检测。诸如Google的OSS-Fuzz等大规模服务对超过1,300个项目进行模糊测试，并已发现超过10,000个漏洞。尽管如此，从漏洞被引入到被检测到之间通常存在相当大的时间滞后（即暴露窗口）。鉴于额外模糊测试的计算成本，我们旨在利用漏洞风险估计来高效分配模糊测试资源。我们提出RAISE（风险感知密集型补充执行框架），这是一个即时（JIT）框架，优先对高风险项目日进行额外模糊测试。RAISE在项目日粒度上执行二分类，以估计某一天是否引入了漏洞。我们构建了一个数据集，将OSV数据库中的漏洞引入提交与来自OSS-Fuzz的每日覆盖率和变更指标相结合，涵盖15个C/C++项目和15,210个项目日，其中347个（2.28%）是漏洞引入日。在跨项目验证设置中，我们最佳模型——结合基于VCCFinder的静态特征与动态模糊测试特征的随机森林——实现了0.74的ROC-AUC，显著优于随机基线；以PatchCoverage为首的覆盖率指标是最重要的特征。在对968个漏洞比较五种分配策略的模拟中，Simple-Regression比不依赖预测的启发式方法显著更具成本效益，每个检测到的漏洞花费91.60次额外模糊测试会话，约为Median启发式方法（171.89）成本的一半。RAISE表明，具有覆盖率感知模糊测试特征的JIT漏洞预测可以有效指导持续模糊测试资源的风险感知分配。

Original abstract: Continuous fuzzing is used for vulnerability detection in Open Source Software (OSS). Large-scale services such as Google's OSS-Fuzz fuzz over 1,300 projects and have revealed more than 10,000 vulnerabilities. Despite this, a considerable time lag often exists between when a vulnerability is introduced and when it is detected (i.e., the window of exposure). Given the computational cost of additional fuzzing, we aim to allocate fuzzing resources efficiently using vulnerability-risk estimates. We propose RAISE (Risk-Aware Intensive Supplemental Execution Framework), a Just-In-Time (JIT) framework that prioritizes additional fuzzing for high-risk project-days. RAISE performs binary classification at the project-day granularity to estimate whether a vulnerability is introduced on a given day. We construct a dataset combining vulnerability-introducing commits from the OSV database with daily coverage and change metrics from OSS-Fuzz, covering 15 C/C++ projects and 15,210 project-days, of which 347 (2.28%) are vulnerability-introducing. In a cross-project validation setting, our best model, a Random Forest combining VCCFinder-based static features with dynamic fuzzing features, achieves a ROC-AUC of 0.74, substantially outperforming a random baseline; coverage metrics, led by PatchCoverage, are the most important features. In a simulation over 968 vulnerabilities comparing five allocation strategies, Simple-Regression was significantly more cost-efficient than the prediction-agnostic heuristics, spending 91.60 additional fuzzing sessions per detected vulnerability, roughly half the cost of the Median heuristic (171.89). RAISE shows that JIT vulnerability prediction with coverage-aware fuzzing features can effectively guide risk-aware allocation of continuous fuzzing resources.

</details>


### [9] [From Tokens to Files: Multi-Granularity Detection and Localization of AI-Involved Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27863)
*Yue Xu,Xiyang Wang,Hui Liu,Bolei He*

Main category: cs.SE

Comments: 29 pages

Selection reason: token 级适配器打分并聚合到行/块/文件的多粒度 AI 代码溯源检测，可用于 agent 生成代码的评测与归因。

TL;DR: 提出token级AI作者身份建模框架，用双低秩适配器似然比和轻量聚合头实现行、块、文件级AI代码定位，并在内部与外部基准及真实人类代码上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将文件压缩为单一分数，在仅少量AI块参与时会稀释局部证据，因此需要既能标记AI参与文件又能定位AI生成或修改行/块的方法。

Method: 冻结代码语言模型并配备两个低秩适配器，分别在人写与机器写文件上训练，以逐token对数似然比作为局部证据；轻量头结合上下文将证据聚合到行和块，再聚合为文件级决策；训练数据为人类原始文件及其AI生成或AI修改版本构成的混合作者文件，涵盖续写与中间填充场景。

Result: 在涵盖六种生成模式的11,773个AI参与文件和2,541个人类文件的内部测试集上，1% FPR操作点达到72.4 macro F1；无适配时在HybridCodeAuthorship上达到62.5行级F1，高于其论文报告的最佳结果56；在2022年前仓库的19,445个人类编写文件上，迁移1%阈值下假阳性率为0.55%至0.81%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在token级证据基础上实现行、块和文件级AI作者身份检测与定位，并在外部人机共写基准和真实人类代码上保持有效且稳定的低假阳性操作点。

中文摘要：大型语言模型在代码生成中的日益使用，需要能够区分纯人类编写代码与AI参与代码的方法：即文件中任何部分，无论多小，由模型生成或被模型大幅修改。除了标记此类文件外，我们旨在定位可归因于AI的行或块。现有方法将文件压缩为单一分数，基于表面文体特征或全局代码表示，当仅少数块涉及AI时，这会平均掉证据。我们提出一个在token级别建模AI作者身份，并在行、块和文件级别进行预测的框架。一个冻结的代码语言模型配备两个低秩适配器，分别在人写文件和机器写文件上训练；它们逐token的对数似然比提供AI作者身份的局部证据。轻量级头利用周围代码上下文在一行和块内聚合该证据，其局部预测被聚合为文件级决策。我们在由人类原始文件及其AI生成或AI修改对应文件构建的混合作者文件中训练，包括续写和中间填充场景。在内部测试集上，涵盖六种生成模式的11,773个AI参与文件与2,541个人类文件，我们的方法在1% FPR操作点达到72.4 macro F1。在无适配的情况下，它在HybridCodeAuthorship上达到62.5行级F1，这是一个没有训练拆分的外部人机共写基准，高于其论文报告的最佳行级结果56。在来自2022年前创建仓库的19,445个人类编写文件上，在迁移的1%阈值下，假阳性率保持在0.55%至0.81%之间，因此该操作点在真实人类代码上成立。

Original abstract: The increasing use of large language models for code generation calls for methods that distinguish purely human-written code from AI-involved code: files in which any part, however small, was generated or substantially modified by a model. Beyond flagging such files, we aim to localize the lines or blocks attributable to AI. Existing approaches compress a file into a single score, from surface stylistic features or a global code representation, which averages away the evidence when only a few blocks are AI-involved. We propose a framework that models AI authorship at the token level and predicts at the line, block, and file levels. A frozen code language model is equipped with two low-rank adapters, trained separately on machine-written and human-written files; their per-token log-likelihood ratio provides local evidence of AI authorship. Lightweight heads aggregate this evidence within lines and blocks, using the surrounding code context, and their local predictions are aggregated into a file-level decision. We train on mixed-authorship files built from human originals and their AI-generated or AI-modified counterparts, including continuation and fill-in-the-middle scenarios. On an internal test set of 11,773 AI-involved files across six generation regimes against 2,541 human files, our method reaches 72.4 macro F1 at a 1% FPR operating point. With no adaptation, it reaches 62.5 line-level F1 on HybridCodeAuthorship, an external human-AI co-writing benchmark with no training split, above the 56 its own paper reports as the best line-level result. On 19,445 human-written files from repositories created before 2022, the false-positive rate stays between 0.55% and 0.81% under the transported 1% threshold, so the operating point holds on real human code.

</details>


### [10] [Hallucinations of Large Language Models in Cross-Language Code Translation: Phenomena and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27868)
*Yali Du,Yunji Zhang,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 系统刻画跨语言代码翻译幻觉并给出检索增强+分类感知反思式修正框架，反思与可靠性改进直接可迁移。

TL;DR: 系统实证研究LLM代码翻译幻觉，构建三类分类体系，人工标注13,919个案例，并提出HaluCodeTran缓解框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉研究多集中于自然语言生成或代码生成，跨语言代码翻译中幻觉的形式、分布与缓解仍缺乏充分刻画；翻译中的微小语义偏差可能静默改变程序行为，危害较大。

Method: 在三个基准数据集、六种语言对和七个LLM上开展实证研究，构建语义一致性冲突、事实知识冲突和项目上下文偏离三类分类体系，人工标注13,919个幻觉案例并分析其在不同模型、数据集和语言方向上的变化；提出结合BM25相关翻译示例检索与分类体系感知反思式细化的HaluCodeTran框架。

Result: 形成了代码翻译幻觉的三类分类体系，完成了13,919个案例的人工标注，揭示了幻觉类型随模型、数据集和语言方向的变化，并提出HaluCodeTran缓解框架。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了经过执行测试的代码翻译幻觉刻画，并为构建更可靠的基于LLM的代码迁移工具提供了实用基线。

中文摘要：代码翻译将源代码从一种编程语言转换为另一种编程语言，同时保持其功能。尽管大语言模型（LLMs）改进了代码翻译，但其输出仍包含幻觉：生成的代码与源程序冲突、违反目标语言事实，或引入上下文不一致的依赖。这些失败在翻译中尤其有害，因为微小的语义偏差可能悄然改变程序行为。现有的幻觉研究主要集中于自然语言生成或代码生成，尚未充分刻画跨语言代码翻译中幻觉的形式、分布和缓解方法。本文对代码翻译中的LLM幻觉进行了系统的实证研究。在三个基准数据集、六种语言对和七个LLM上，我们构建了一个包含三大类别的分类体系：语义一致性冲突、事实知识冲突和项目上下文偏离。我们人工标注了13,919个幻觉案例，并分析了幻觉类型如何在模型、数据集和语言方向上变化。在该分类体系的指导下，我们进一步提出HaluCodeTran，一种结合基于BM25的相关翻译示例检索与分类体系感知反思式细化的缓解框架。该研究提供了经过执行测试的代码翻译幻觉刻画，并为构建更可靠的基于LLM的代码迁移工具提供了实用基线。

Original abstract: Code translation converts source code from one programming language to another while preserving its functionality. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have improved code translation, their outputs still contain hallucinations: generated code that conflicts with the source program, violates target-language facts, or introduces contextually inconsistent dependencies. These failures are especially harmful in translation because small semantic deviations can silently change program behavior. Existing hallucination studies mainly focus on natural-language generation or code generation, leaving the forms, distribution, and mitigation of hallucinations in cross-language code translation insufficiently characterized. This paper presents a systematic empirical study of LLM hallucinations in code translation. Across three benchmark datasets, six language pairs, and seven LLMs, we construct a taxonomy with three major categories: Semantic Consistency Conflicts, Factual Knowledge Conflicts, and Project Context Deviations. We manually annotate 13,919 hallucination cases and analyze how hallucination types vary across models, datasets, and language directions. Guided by the taxonomy, we further propose HaluCodeTran, a mitigation framework that combines BM25-based retrieval of relevant translation examples with taxonomy-aware reflective refinement. The study provides an execution-tested characterization of code-translation hallucinations and a practical baseline for building more reliable LLM-based code migration tools.

</details>


### [11] [A Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning Engine for Compilation-Free Patch Presence Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27871)
*Siyuan Li,Jiayi Zhou,Zhiyuan Xia,Weijie Wang,Yaowen Zheng,Weiran Tang,Hong Li,Hongsong Zhu,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 神经感知、符号判定的耦合设计（LLM 生成 SMT 规格，Z3 做确定性裁决），为 agent 验证环节提供可迁移的可靠判定范式。

TL;DR: SyLares 将免编译补丁存在性检测重构为神经符号推理：LLM 仅提取特征并生成 SMT-LIB，Z3 做确定性判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码复用导致 1-day 漏洞扩散；传统补丁存在性检测可用性和准确性受限且依赖脆弱编译；先前 Lares 的 LLM 与 Z3 并行双通道中 Z3 仅覆盖 20% 代码案例，回退 LLM 导致非形式化、非确定性和幻觉风险，且通道间不互相增强。

Method: SyLares 不让 LLM 输出最终判决，而将其作为神经特征提取器，感知异构源代码和反编译伪代码，生成符合规范的 SMT-LIB 输入；由 Code2SMT 推理引擎结合确定性语法解析与 LLM 驱动的代码归一化、符号对齐，把最终确定性判断交给 Z3。

Result: 提出 Code2SMT 推理引擎与神经感知/符号决策耦合，使每个案例转化为形式化等价性查询，而非不透明的 LLM 猜测；摘要未报告定量实验指标。

Conclusion: SyLares 以神经符号推理克服并行双通道的局限，降低幻觉和非确定性，实现免编译补丁存在性检测的形式化判定。

中文摘要：现代软件生态系统中的代码复用促进了 1-day 漏洞的传播，带来显著安全风险。确定补丁是否存在至关重要，但传统方法可用性和准确性有限，且往往依赖脆弱的编译过程。在我们先前的工作中，我们提出了 Lares，这是首个将大语言模型（LLM）与 SMT 求解器相结合的、用于补丁存在性检测的免编译框架。然而，Lares 将 LLM 和 SMT 求解器视为两个独立、并行的决策通道：符号通道（Z3）仅在 20% 的代码案例中成功，其余案例回退到神经通道（LLM）进行最终决策。让 LLM 充当最终裁决者会使验证变成一个非形式化、非确定性的过程，容易产生幻觉错误，而两个通道从未相互增强。为克服这些局限，我们提出 SyLares，它将免编译补丁存在性检测重新表述为神经符号推理。SyLares 不让 LLM 输出最终判决，而是将 LLM 用作神经特征提取器，感知异构源代码和反编译伪代码，并生成符合规范的 SMT-LIB 输入，同时将最终、确定性的判断委托给符号 Z3 求解器。这种“神经感知、符号决策”的耦合由 Code2SMT 推理引擎实现，该引擎将确定性语法解析与 LLM 驱动的代码归一化和符号对齐相结合，从而使每个案例都被归约为形式化等价性查询，而不是不透明的 LLM 猜测。

Original abstract: Code reuse in modern software ecosystems facilitates the spread of 1-day vulnerabilities, posing significant security risks. Determining if patches are present is crucial, but traditional methods suffer from limited usability and accuracy, often depending on a fragile compilation process. In our prior work, we introduced Lares, the first compilation-free framework for patch presence testing that combines large language models (LLMs) with SMT solvers. However, Lares treats the LLM and the SMT solver as two separate, parallel decision channels: the symbolic channel (Z3) succeeded in only 20% of code cases, and the remaining cases fell back to the neural channel (the LLM) for the final decision. Letting the LLM act as the final arbiter turns verification into an informal, non-deterministic process that is prone to hallucination errors, while the two channels never reinforce each other. To overcome these limitations, we propose SyLares, which reformulates compilation-free patch presence testing as neuro-symbolic reasoning. Instead of letting the LLM emit a final verdict, SyLares uses the LLM as a neural feature extractor that perceives heterogeneous source code and decompiled pseudocode and produces specification-compliant SMT-LIB inputs, and delegates the final, deterministic judgment to the symbolic Z3 solver. This neural-perceives, symbolic-decides coupling is realized by a Code2SMT reasoning engine that combines deterministic syntactic parsing with LLM-driven code normalization and symbol alignment, so that every case is reduced to a formal equivalence query rather than an opaque LLM guess.

</details>


### [12] [LiveUIBench: Evaluating Agent as App Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27977)
*Tong Zhang,Yuxuan Cheng,Ruimin Zhang,Yun Peng,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 提出 Agent as App 任务与 LiveUIBench 基准，含共享 agent 运行时与编排更新机制，对 agent 长程工作流与评测有参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出 Agent as App 任务、LiveUIAgent 实现与 LiveUIBench 基准，以共享运行时支持自然语言个性化应用，但评测模型难以可靠完成完整工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一应用的不同用户需求可能不同甚至冲突，同一用户需求也会随时间变化；对 agent-to-app 或 UI-to-code 生成的应用做个性化往往需要改动生成实现。

Method: 提出 Agent as App 任务，让用户用自然语言表达需求并由智能体创建个性化应用；实现 LiveUIAgent，生成连接用户界面、已保存信息和服务动作的任务特定编排，由共享智能体运行时渲染和更新界面并执行操作；复用运行时修改编排与界面；构建 LiveUIBench 基准。

Result: 所评估模型难以可靠完成这些工作流；应用常满足许多局部检查但整体任务未完成，且更高的部分覆盖率可能对应更少完成的工作流。

Conclusion: 复用共享运行时可为需求演化提供轻量级个性化基础，但评测显示端到端工作流可靠性仍需提升。

中文摘要：应用程序为用户完成日常任务提供便捷功能。然而，不同用户可能对同一应用有不同甚至冲突的需求，而同一用户的需求也可能随时间变化。对由 agent-to-app 或 UI-to-code 方法生成的应用进行个性化，可能需要修改生成的实现。我们提出 Agent as App 任务：用户用自然语言表达需求，智能体创建个性化应用，并在使用期间持续提供其行为。我们实现了 LiveUIAgent，它生成连接用户界面、已保存信息和服务动作的任务特定编排。共享智能体运行时负责渲染和更新界面，同时执行所请求的操作。在复用该运行时的同时修改编排和界面，为需求演变时的个性化提供了轻量级基础。我们引入了 LiveUIBench，这是一个包含跨个人服务的多样需求和有序交互的基准。实证结果表明，所评估的模型难以可靠地完成这些工作流。应用可以满足许多局部检查，却使整体任务未完成；更高的部分覆盖率可能与更少完成的工作流同时出现。

Original abstract: Applications provide convenient features for users to complete daily tasks. However, different users may have different or even conflicting requirements for the same application, while the requirements of the same user may change over time. Personalizing applications produced by agent-to-app or UI-to-code approaches can require changes to the generated implementation. We propose Agent as App, a task in which users express their requirements in natural language and an agent creates a personalized application while continuing to supply its behavior during use. We implement LiveUIAgent, which generates a task-specific orchestration connecting the user interface, saved information, and service actions. A shared agent runtime renders and updates the interface while carrying out the requested operations. Revising the orchestration and interface while reusing that runtime provides a lightweight basis for personalization as requirements evolve. We introduce LiveUIBench, a benchmark with diverse requirements and ordered interactions across personal services. The empirical findings show that the evaluated models struggle to complete these workflows reliably. Applications can satisfy many local checks while leaving the overall task unfinished, and greater partial coverage can coincide with fewer completed workflows.

</details>


### [13] [Training the Whole Agent, Not Just the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28069)
*Lingxiao Tang,Wei Tao,Yiwen Guo,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 在 RL 中同时训练策略并自适应 harness（技能生成与工具/钩子演化），直接面向 coding agent 训练与工具反馈机制，价值高。

TL;DR: HarnessAdapt-RL在强化学习中联合训练编码智能体策略并自适应演化其harness，在多个SWE基准上显著优于GRPO和人工设计harness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练编码智能体时通常固定harness，但轨迹显示有缺陷的修复策略和交互问题会随任务及策略学习而变化，固定harness难以适配这些动态问题。

Method: 提出HarnessAdapt-RL：对任务特定的修复缺陷，用冻结教师生成机制级技能；对跨任务交互问题，用独立教师模型演化工具和钩子，仅保留能提升通过率或可训练率且不损害另一指标的更改；策略在适配后harness下训练，新轨迹继续指导适配。

Result: 在9B基础模型上，HarnessAdapt-RL在SWE-bench Verified、Multilingual和Pro上相比GRPO分别提升pass@1 9.8、12.6和6.4个百分点，并优于使用人工设计OpenHands harness的GRPO；机制级技能移除后增益仍持续；随策略变化重新演化工具和钩子优于冻结早期版本，而部署时移除工具和钩子最多损失7.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 训练应产生由训练后策略与演化出的工具和钩子组成的模型-harness对，机制级技能可在部署时移除，但演化出的工具和钩子应保留。

中文摘要：训练编码智能体通常更新策略而保持执行框架（harness）固定。在训练过程中，轨迹会揭示有缺陷的修复策略和交互问题，这些问题随任务而变化，并随策略学习而改变。我们提出HarnessAdapt-RL，在强化学习过程中同时训练策略并适配其harness。针对有缺陷的修复策略，冻结教师生成任务特定的机制级技能。针对跨任务反复出现的交互问题，独立教师模型演化工具和钩子，仅当每次更改能提高观测到的通过率或观测到的可训练率且不降低另一者时才保留。策略在适配后的harness下训练，其新轨迹指导进一步适配。在9B基础模型上，HarnessAdapt-RL在SWE-bench Verified、Multilingual和Pro上相比GRPO分别提升pass@1 9.8、12.6和6.4个百分点，并优于使用人工设计OpenHands harness的GRPO。机制级技能带来的增益在其评估时移除后仍持续。随策略变化重新演化工具和钩子优于冻结早期版本。相反，在部署时移除它们会损失高达7.7个百分点。因此，训练产生一个模型-harness对：训练后的策略与演化出的工具和钩子，部署时移除技能。

Original abstract: Training coding agents typically updates the policy while keeping the harness fixed. During training, trajectories reveal flawed repair strategies and interaction problems that vary across tasks and change as the policy learns. We introduce HarnessAdapt-RL, which trains the policy and adapts its harness during reinforcement learning. For flawed repair strategies, a frozen teacher generates task-specific mechanism-level skills. For interaction problems recurring across tasks, a separate teacher model evolves tools and hooks, keeping each change only if it improves the observed pass rate or the observed trainable rate without lowering either. The policy trains with the adapted harness, and its new trajectories guide further adaptation. With a 9B base model, HarnessAdapt-RL improves pass@1 over GRPO by 9.8, 12.6, and 6.4 percentage points on SWE-bench Verified, Multilingual, and Pro, and outperforms GRPO with the human-designed OpenHands harness. Gains from mechanism-level skills persist after their removal at evaluation. Re-evolving tools and hooks as the policy changes outperforms freezing early versions. Removing them at deployment, in contrast, costs up to 7.7 percentage points. Training therefore produces a model-harness pair: the trained policy and the evolved tools and hooks, with skills removed at deployment.

</details>


### [14] [AgenTest: Mining Business Rules to Test LLM Agents for Business Logic Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28116)
*Lili Quan,Longtian Wang,Yuelin Wang,Mingfei Cheng,Chao Shen,Sen Chen,Xiaofei Xie*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 从系统提示与工具定义挖掘业务规则并转为 Given-When-Then 规格做多轮交互测试，混合 oracle 检查全轨迹，对 agent 测试与验证高度可迁移。

TL;DR: AgenTest 从系统提示、工具定义和领域知识中挖掘并细化业务规则为 Given-When-Then 规范，通过多轮交互和混合预言机系统测试 LLM 智能体，在 tau2-bench 上显著优于基线并发现跨用户数据违规等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体通过工具执行业务操作时必须遵守领域业务规则，但规则分散于系统提示、工具定义和隐含领域知识中，缺乏显式可测试规范；现有测试多关注任务成功、预定义策略或已知攻击，难以系统发现未明确的业务规则违反。

Method: AgenTest 从系统提示、工具定义和领域知识中挖掘业务规则，细化为 Given-When-Then 规范；对每个规范构造满足 Given 的环境状态和模拟用户，通过多轮交互驱动智能体，并用混合预言机在消息、工具调用和状态变化上检查 Then 条件。

Result: 在 tau2-bench 的航空、零售和电信智能体上，使用两个底层模型，AgenTest 生成 551 个细化规范，分别检测到 89 和 121 个不同规范的违反，为所有基线合计的 3.4 倍和 2.5 倍；仅 AgenTest 检测到不当访问或修改另一用户数据的情况；约 60% 的触发缺陷运行无法仅从最终状态检测。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于显式业务规则对完整交互进行系统测试，能更有效地发现 LLM 智能体的业务逻辑缺陷，尤其是仅检查最终状态或任务成功无法覆盖的违规行为。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）智能体越来越多地通过工具执行业务操作，并直接与应用数据和服务交互。除了完成用户请求外，这些智能体还必须遵守领域特定的业务规则，这些规则约束何时允许执行动作以及可能影响哪些数据或资源。违反此类规则可能导致业务逻辑缺陷，即使任务表面上成功。检测这些缺陷很困难，因为规则很少以显式、可测试的规范形式提供。它们分布在系统提示和工具定义中，而其他规则仍隐含在领域知识中。现有的智能体测试方法通常评估任务成功、预定义策略或已知攻击场景。这限制了它们系统性地发现先前未明确的业务规则违反的能力。我们提出 AgenTest，它从系统提示、工具定义和领域知识中挖掘业务规则，将其细化为 Given-When-Then 规范，并系统性地测试每个规范。对于每个提取的规范，AgenTest 通过构造满足条件的环境状态和模拟用户来实例化其 Given 条件，通过多轮交互驱动智能体，并使用混合预言机检查其在消息、工具调用和状态变化上的 Then 条件。在 tau2-bench 的航空、零售和电信智能体上，使用两个底层模型，AgenTest 生成 551 个细化规范，并分别检测到对 89 和 121 个不同规范的违反——是所有基线合计的 3.4 倍和 2.5 倍。只有 AgenTest 检测到智能体不当访问或修改另一用户数据的情况。此外，约 60% 的触发缺陷的运行无法仅从最终状态检测到，这突显了根据显式业务规则检查完整交互的必要性。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly execute business operations through tools and interact directly with application data and services. Beyond completing user requests, these agents must obey domain-specific business rules that constrain when actions are permitted and what data or resources may be affected. Violating such rules can lead to business logic bugs, even when a task appears successful. Detecting these bugs is difficult because the rules are rarely available as explicit, testable specifications. They are distributed across system prompts and tool definitions, while others remain implicit in domain knowledge. Existing agent testing approaches typically evaluate task success, predefined policies, or known attack scenarios. This limits their ability to systematically uncover previously unspecified business-rule violations.
  We present AgenTest, which mines business rules from system prompts, tool definitions, and domain knowledge, refines them into Given-When-Then specifications, and systematically tests each specification. For each extracted specification, AgenTest instantiates its Given conditions by constructing a satisfying environment state and simulated user, exercises the agent through multi-turn interactions, and checks its Then conditions over messages, tool calls, and state changes using a hybrid oracle. On the airline, retail, and telecom agents of tau2-bench with two underlying models, AgenTest produces 551 refined specifications, and detects violations of 89 and 121 distinct specifications, respectively -- 3.4X and 2.5X as many as all baselines combined. Only AgenTest detects cases where an agent improperly accesses or modifies another user's data. Moreover, about 60% of bug-triggering runs cannot be detected from the final state alone, highlighting the need to check the full interaction against explicit business rules.

</details>


### [15] [From Code Generator to Engineering Companion: A Plant-aware Agent for PLC Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28160)
*Joschka Kersting,Gesa Benndorf*

Main category: cs.SE

Journal reference: 2026 IEEE 31st International Conference on Emerging Technologies and Factory Automation (ETFA), Västerås, Sweden, 2026

DOI: [10.1109/ETFA68377.2026.11725227](https://doi.org/10.1109/ETFA68377.2026.11725227)

Selection reason: PLC 编程 copilot：持久化工厂上下文摄取、chat-first 调试交互与分级工具权限梯子，对代码 agent 的工具反馈与部署安全控制有可迁移设计。

TL;DR: 提出工厂感知的对话式PLC编程副驾驶，支持工厂上下文摄取、聊天优先的解释与调试、分级工具权限，并由工程师掌控实际部署步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PLC编程仍依赖IDE和人工操作，需频繁切换文档、项目文件与代码编辑器；现有检索增强LLM虽能生成合规结构化文本，但一次性生成且缺乏特定工厂的持久知识。

Method: 设计并呈现一个工厂感知的对话式副驾驶，引入三类扩展：工厂特定上下文摄取；面向解释与调试的聊天优先交互；从聊天到部署的渐进式工具访问阶梯。

Result: 提出系统方案，面向真实工厂基础设施，强调每个产生实际影响的步骤由工程师控制；摘要未报告定量评估结果。

Conclusion: 将PLC代码生成从一次性流水线推进为具备工厂上下文和对话交互的副驾驶范式，同时以分级工具访问保障工程控制。

中文摘要：可编程逻辑控制器（PLC）编程仍然是一项以集成开发环境（IDE）为中心的手动任务，需要在文档、项目文件和代码编辑器之间不断切换上下文。近期研究已证明，检索增强型大语言模型能够以较高的编译通过率生成符合供应商规范的结构化文本\cite{pred}，但这些系统将代码生成视为一次性流水线：工程师提交查询并接收代码，系统不具备特定工厂的持久知识。我们提出了一种具备工厂感知能力的对话式副驾驶，从三个方向扩展了该范式：工厂特定上下文摄取、用于解释和调试的聊天优先交互模型，以及从聊天到部署的渐进式工具访问阶梯。该系统面向真实工厂基础设施设计，并使工程师对每个产生现实影响的步骤保持控制。

Original abstract: Programmable Logic Controller (PLC) coding remains a manual, IDE-centric task that demands constant context switching between documentation, project files, and code editors. Recent work has demonstrated that retrieval-augmented large language models can generate vendor-compliant Structured Text at high compilation rates \cite{pred}, yet these systems treat code generation as a one-shot pipeline: the engineer submits a query and receives code, with no persistent knowledge of the specific plant. We present a plant-aware conversational copilot that extends this paradigm in three directions: plant-specific context ingestion, a chat-first interaction model for explanation and debugging, and a graduated tool-access ladder from chat to deployment. The system is designed for real plant infrastructure and keeps the engineer in control of each step that produces real-world effects.

</details>


### [16] [Foreword: Pre-Generation Prediction and Characterisation of LLM Test Failures using the Jacobian Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28187)
*Somin Kim,Robert Feldt,Shin Yoo*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 用内部激活/Jacobian 视角在生成前预测 LLM 测试失败并做测试优先级排序，属可迁移的可靠性预测与测试成本削减机制。

TL;DR: Foreword通过同时学习通过/失败参考并用J-lens表示输入，在不生成输出的情况下实现更优的LLM软件失败预测、测试优先级排序和失败语义刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的软件测试因多次采样和昂贵oracle而成本高，且提示词或模型变更需重测；现有预生成充分性方法Clotho仅建模通过参考，隐藏状态表示不能解释失败原因。

Method: 提出Foreword框架：从通过和失败参考中学习；用Jacobian透镜（J-lens）将中间激活转为词表token分数；搭配稀疏逻辑回归探针或双高斯混合模型（dual GMMs），并提供参考密度与失败类组件。

Result: 在十个任务和三个开放权重LLM上，四种表示-估计器组合平均优于Clotho；失败预测ROC-AUC平均提升0.14、最高0.31；前100个排序输入中平均多发现15%失败、最高33%；小模型优先级排序迁移到专有模型时前四分之一平均多发现22%失败、最高41%；定性案例显示可刻画标签或任务规范歧义导致的失败。

Conclusion: Foreword可在不生成输出的情况下支持测试人员优先排序输入、检查预测支持情况并刻画特定失败，且优于现有方法。

中文摘要：测试基于LLM的软件成本高昂：每个输入需要多个采样响应，每个响应都要由昂贵的oracle判定。此外，每次提示词或模型变更都需要重新测试。预生成充分性度量通过基于模型内部状态按失败风险对输入排序来降低这一成本。然而，当前最先进方法Clotho仅对通过的参考输入建模，其隐藏状态表示也无法说明输入为何失败。我们提出Foreword，一个基于两个思想的框架：同时从通过和失败的参考中学习，并通过Jacobian透镜（J-lens）表示输入；J-lens是一种可解释性技术，可将中间激活读取为词表token上的分数。Foreword将这些表示与两种估计器配对：稀疏逻辑回归探针或双高斯混合模型（dual GMMs），后者还可产生参考密度（用于标记参考未覆盖的输入）和失败类组件（用于对相似失败分组）。在十个任务和三个开放权重LLM上，表示与估计器的全部四种组合平均均优于Clotho，无论是在失败预测（ROC-AUC平均提升0.14，最高提升0.31）还是测试优先级排序（平均在前100个排序输入中多发现15%的失败，最高33%）。使用小模型获得的优先级排序也能更好地迁移到专有模型（平均在排序输入的前四分之一中多发现22%的失败，最高41%）。重要的是，我们的定性案例研究表明，Foreword通过指出标签或任务规范中的歧义，支持对失败进行语义刻画。综合来看，Foreword可帮助测试人员优先排序输入、检查预测的支持情况并刻画特定失败，而无需生成输出。

Original abstract: Testing LLM-based software is expensive: each input needs several sampled responses, each judged by a costly oracle. Further, every prompt or model change calls for re-testing. Pre-generation adequacy measures cut this cost by ranking inputs by failure risk based on the model's internal state. However, the state of the art, Clotho, models only passing reference inputs, and its hidden-state representation says nothing about why inputs fail. We present Foreword, a framework built on two ideas: learn from both passing and failing references, and represent inputs through the Jacobian lens (J-lens), an interpretability technique that reads intermediate activations out as scores over vocabulary tokens. Foreword pairs these representations with two estimators: a sparse logistic-regression probe or dual Gaussian Mixture Models (dual GMMs), which also yield a reference density (to flag inputs the references do not cover) and failing-class components (to group similar failures). On ten tasks and three open-weight LLMs, all four combinations of representations and estimators outperform Clotho on average, both for prediction of failure (ROC-AUC improves by 0.14 on average and by up to 0.31) and test prioritisation (15% more failures among the 100 top-ranked inputs on average, up to 33%). Prioritisation obtained using small models also transfers better to proprietary models (on average 22% and up to 41% more failures in the first quarter of prioritised inputs). Importantly, our qualitative case study shows that Foreword supports semantic characterisation of failures by pointing to ambiguities in labels or task specifications. Combined, Foreword can support testers for prioritising inputs, checking support for a prediction, and characterising specific failures, all without generating output.

</details>


### [17] [ExeBench-FHD: Addressing Fidelity Issues in Decompiler Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28212)
*Akarsh Ashok,Dinil Mon Divakaran,Vivek Balachandran*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 系统分析 ExeBench 反编译评测的九类保真度缺陷并重构 benchmark，对构建代码/编译 agent 的评测基准与可复现验证有直接参考。

TL;DR: 分析 ExeBench 的九类缺陷，提出向后兼容的 ExeBench-FHD，通过新增汇编、数据段、包装器和警告标志等字段提升反编译评估有效性，并在 -O2 四架构上使 GPT 6 Sol 重执行率最高提升 26.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ExeBench 广泛用于评估 LLM 反编译器，但其测试忠实性和有效性存在问题，需要识别并修正。

Method: 分析 ExeBench，归纳九个影响测试忠实性和有效性的问题；构建向后兼容的 ExeBench-FHD，新增 16 个字段和五个警告标志，并研究记录过滤；在四种架构上以 -O2 评估。

Result: ExeBench 中 38.9% 的函数至少有一个警告标志，过滤四个警告标志后保留 63.9% 语料；GPT 6 Sol 在读取数据段、混合参数类型和具有未使用参数的函数上，重执行率分别最多提升 26.5%、19.1% 和 21.0%。

Conclusion: ExeBench-FHD 以向后兼容方式提升基准忠实性和测试有效性，改善 LLM 反编译评估。

中文摘要：ExeBench 是用于评估基于 LLM 的反编译器最广泛使用的基准之一。我们分析了 ExeBench，并识别出九个问题，这些问题会影响测试对反编译器评估的忠实程度。其中三个问题会阻止 LLM 反编译器从 ExeBench 的函数中恢复 C 代码。这些问题包括：整数和浮点类型混合时参数顺序有歧义、优化后的汇编可能丢弃未使用参数，以及常量存储在函数汇编代码之外。另有六个问题影响测试有效性：非 void 函数没有 return、占位符类型、被优化器删除的 static 或 inline 函数、名为 main 的函数、平凡函数体以及被截断的定义。我们提出 ExeBench-FHD，这是 ExeBench 的向后兼容修订版，新增 16 个字段：调用点汇编、数据段、测试包装器、链接二进制反汇编以及五个警告标志。在 ExeBench 中，38.9% 的函数至少具有五个标志之一；删除带有四个警告标志中任意一个的记录（除了 unused_param，该标志标记的函数可由新包装器进行评估）后，保留 63.9% 的语料库。在四种架构上以 -O2 进行的评估中，新字段将 GPT 6 Sol 的重新执行率分别最多提高 26.5%、19.1% 和 21.0%，对应读取数据段、混合参数类型和具有未使用参数的函数。

Original abstract: ExeBench is one of the most widely used benchmarks for evaluating LLM-based decompilers. We analyze ExeBench and identify nine issues that affect how faithfully a test measures a decompiler. Three of them prevent LLM decompilers from recovering C code from ExeBench's function. These are ambiguous parameter order when integer and floating-point types are mixed, unused parameters that optimized assembly may drop, and constants stored outside the function's assembly code. Six more affect test validity: non-void functions without a return, placeholder types, static or inline functions removed by the optimizer, functions named main, trivial bodies and truncated definitions. We present ExeBench-FHD, a backward-compatible revision of ExeBench that adds 16 new fields: call-site assembly, data sections, test wrappers, linked-binary disassembly and five warning flags. In ExeBench, 38.9% of functions have at least one of the five flags; dropping records with any of the four warning flags (all except unused_param, which marks functions that the new wrappers make evaluable) keeps 63.9% of the corpus. In an evaluation at -O2 on four architectures, the new fields raise GPT 6 Sol's re-execution rate by up to 26.5%, 19.1% and 21.0% on functions that read data sections, mix parameter types and have unused parameters, respectively.

</details>


### [18] [InterWebCoder: Learning Interactive Webpage Generation from Executable Tests via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28610)
*Jingyu Xiao,Dasen Dai,Yuxuan Wan,Jen-tse Huang,Yintong Huo,Hongyu Zhang,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: 以可执行测试为反馈的网页代码生成 RL 框架，含失败归因修复与测试通过率奖励，验证驱动的代码生成闭环可迁移到 SemOpt。

TL;DR: InterWebCoder通过测试规范引导的数据合成、失败归因修复以及测试ID加权SFT与GRPO，实现交互网页代码生成；9B模型在IWR-Bench和Vision2Web上超过30B级模型，并显著降低验证延迟和训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互网页需同时复现视觉外观与多步行为，MLLM难以胜任；训练数据需覆盖用户动作、状态转移和预期结果，真实网页代码含无关脚本与外部依赖，且基于GUI智能体的验证慢且可能不稳定。

Method: 提出InterWebCoder框架：记录真实交互轨迹，合成带可执行测试脚本的自包含网页代码；利用运行时证据进行失败归因，迭代修复网页代码或测试脚本；结合测试ID加权监督微调与组相对策略优化，以测试通过率和状态级视觉相似度优化功能正确性与视觉保真度。

Result: 流水线从2,058个任务生成1,736个验证样本，合成成功率84.35%；在IWR-Bench和Vision2Web上以9B主干分别取得48.54和48.42，超过30B级模型和Gemini-3.7-Flash，并与Claude-Sonnet-5保持竞争力；奖励计算延迟降低超97%，消除外部MLLM验证成本，端到端训练最高加速1.4倍。

Conclusion: InterWebCoder以快速、可复现的可执行反馈替代昂贵的GUI智能体验证，在提升交互网页代码生成质量的同时显著提高验证与训练效率。

中文摘要：交互式网页支撑着现代应用，但复现其视觉外观和多步行为对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）而言仍具挑战。可扩展的训练数据必须捕获用户动作、状态转移和预期结果，而真实网页代码常与无关脚本和外部依赖纠缠在一起。此外，现有基于GUI智能体的验证提供缓慢且可能不稳定的功能反馈。为应对这些挑战，我们提出InterWebCoder，一个用于交互式网页代码生成的高效框架。InterWebCoder引入测试规范引导与执行验证的数据合成流水线，记录真实交互轨迹并合成带有可执行测试脚本的自包含网页代码。失败归因修复机制利用运行时证据定位并迭代修复网页代码或测试脚本中的错误。随后，我们将测试ID加权监督微调与组相对策略优化相结合，利用测试通过率和状态级视觉相似度直接优化功能正确性与视觉保真度。这以快速、可复现的可执行反馈取代昂贵的GUI智能体验证。我们的流水线从2,058个任务中产生1,736个已验证样本，合成成功率达到84.35%。在IWR-Bench和Vision2Web上，InterWebCoder以9B主干模型分别取得48.54和48.42的最终得分，超过30B级模型和Gemini-3.7-Flash，同时与Claude-Sonnet-5保持竞争力。它将奖励计算延迟降低超过97%，消除外部MLLM验证成本，并将端到端训练加速最高1.4倍。

Original abstract: Interactive webpages underpin modern applications, but reproducing both their visual appearance and multi-step behavior remains challenging for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Scalable training data must capture user actions, state transitions, and expected outcomes, while real-world webpage code is often entangled with irrelevant scripts and external dependencies. Moreover, existing GUI-agent-based verification provides slow and potentially unstable functional feedback. To address these challenges, we propose InterWebCoder, an efficient framework for interactive webpage code generation. InterWebCoder introduces a Test-Specification-Guided and Execution-Verified Data Synthesis pipeline that records real-world interaction trajectories and synthesizes self-contained webpage code with executable test scripts. A failureattributed repair mechanism uses runtime evidence to locate and iteratively fix errors in either the webpage code or test script. We then combine test-ID-weighted supervised fine-tuning with group-relative policy optimization, directly optimizing functional correctness and visual fidelity using test pass rates and state-level visual similarity. This replaces costly GUI-agent verification with fast, reproducible executable feedback. Our pipeline produces 1,736 verified samples from 2,058 tasks, achieving an 84.35% synthesis success rate. On IWR-Bench and Vision2Web, InterWebCoder achieves final scores of 48.54 and 48.42 with a 9B backbone, outperforming 30B-level models and Gemini-3.7-Flash while remaining competitive with Claude-Sonnet-5. It reduces reward-computation latency by over 97%, eliminates external MLLM verification costs, and accelerates end-to-end training by up to 1.4x.

</details>


### [19] [TraceLite: Efficient Trace Reduction for SWE Agents via Optical Compression and Redundancy Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28612)
*Jingyu Xiao,Qianxi Liu,Yintong Huo,Yuan Jiang,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE



Selection reason: SWE agent 轨迹压缩：历史观测图像化+去冗余+阶段过期丢弃，训练无关且降低 33% token 成本，直接关系到长程代码 agent 上下文与效率优化。

TL;DR: TraceLite 是一个无需训练的 SWE-agent 观察压缩框架，通过近期观察保留文本、历史观察渲染为图像、去重和阶段过期丢弃，在 SWE-bench Verified 上达到 78.25% 解决率，并降低 token 与 API 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-agent 的交互轨迹持续增长，导致显著的 token 消耗和推理开销；现有压缩方法常依赖模型训练、辅助 LLM 调用或对源代码等内容作假设，缺乏通用且低开销的方案。

Method: TraceLite 无需训练，压缩异构 SWE-agent 观察：近期观察保留为高保真文本，历史观察渲染为紧凑图像，移除重叠内容，并在代码编辑后丢弃阶段过期的观察载荷；不需要离线训练或辅助 LLM 推理。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 和 DeepSWE 上，以 GPT-5.6-Luna 和 Gemini-3.8Flash 为骨干模型评估。SWE-bench Verified 上解决率达 78.25%，优于所评估的压缩基线，平均输入 token 消耗和 API 成本分别降低 33.05% 和 21.30%，每个观察仅增加 0.438 秒压缩延迟。

Conclusion: TraceLite 以较低处理开销提升 SWE-agent 效率，同时不损害问题解决能力，是一种简单有效且无需训练的观察压缩框架。

中文摘要：软件工程智能体通过迭代式工具使用来解决仓库级任务，但其不断增长的交互轨迹会带来大量 token 消耗和推理开销。现有压缩方法通常需要模型训练、辅助 LLM 调用，或对源代码等特定内容作出假设。我们提出 TraceLite，一个简单而有效、无需训练的框架，用于压缩异构 SWE-agent 观察。我们的初步研究表明，在适度压缩下，光学渲染可保留大量文本和语义信息，同时智能体轨迹表现出跨步和阶段级冗余。受这些发现启发，TraceLite 将近期观察保留为高保真文本，将历史观察渲染为紧凑图像，移除重叠内容，并在代码编辑后丢弃阶段过期的观察载荷。它既不需要离线训练，也不需要辅助 LLM 推理。我们在 SWE-bench Verified 和 DeepSWE 上使用 GPT-5.6-Luna 和 Gemini-3.8Flash 作为骨干模型评估 TraceLite。在 SWE-bench Verified 上，TraceLite 达到 78.25% 的解决率，优于所评估的压缩基线，同时将平均输入 token 消耗和 API 成本分别降低 33.05% 和 21.30%。此外，它每个观察仅引入 0.438 秒的压缩延迟。这些结果表明，TraceLite 以较低处理开销提升 SWE-agent 效率，且不损害问题解决能力。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/xjywhu/TraceLite 获取。

Original abstract: Software engineering agents solve repository-level tasks through iterative tool use, but their continuously growing interaction traces incur substantial token consumption and inference overhead. Existing compression approaches often require model training, auxiliary LLM calls, or assumptions about specific content such as source code. We present TraceLite, a simple yet effective, training-free framework for compressing heterogeneous SWE-agent observations. Our preliminary study shows that optical rendering preserves substantial textual and semantic information under moderate compression, while agent trajectories exhibit both cross-step and phase-level redundancy. Motivated by these findings, TraceLite preserves recent observations as high-fidelity text, renders historical observations as compact images, removes overlapping content, and discards phase-expired observation payloads after code editing. It requires neither offline training nor auxiliary LLM inference. We evaluate TraceLite on SWE-bench Verified and DeepSWE using GPT-5.6-Luna and Gemini-3.8Flash as backbone models. On SWE-bench Verified, TraceLite achieves 78.25% resolve rate, outperforming the evaluated compression baselines while reducing average input-token consumption and API cost by 33.05% and 21.30%, respectively. Moreover, it introduces only 0.438 seconds of compression latency per observation. These results demonstrate that TraceLite improves SWE-agent efficiency with low processing overhead and without compromising issue-resolution capability. Our code is available at https://github.com/xjywhu/TraceLite.

</details>

### [20] [Navalia: Advancing Cowork Agents through Verifiable Task Synthesis and Long-Horizon Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26887)
*Hengqi Liu,Ziyuan Liu,Yang Qin,Wanting Zhou,Zichuan Wang,Jiachen Liang,Xu Chu,Shaowei Chen,Zhaokai Luo,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 13 pages, 2 figures

Selection reason: 提出可验证任务合成（证据选择+可执行计算链+程序化校验）与长程后训练流程，任务合成与验证机制可迁移到代码优化 agent 的可靠数据构造。

TL;DR: Navalia 以企业财报为数据源，通过可验证的任务合成（语义选证、可执行计算链、独立 LLM 评审与程序化校验）生成带可追溯证据与步骤间真实数据依赖的金融分析任务，并结合轨迹采集、冷启动 SFT 与 RL 进行长时程后训练，在五个基准上均优于基座模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融分析需要智能体整合跨文档证据并完成多步数值推理，但专家人工编写任务成本高，直接由 LLM 合成又难以保证任务难度与答案可靠性，因此需要可验证、可扩展的任务合成方法，并配合长时程后训练提升执行能力。

Method: 提出 Navalia 框架：以企业财务报告为数据源，从完整事实集中语义筛选支撑证据，构建可执行计算链，生成步骤间具有真实数据依赖且证据可追溯的分析任务；再用独立 LLM 评审与程序化校验验证证据一致性和答案正确性；将校验后的任务用于轨迹采集、冷启动 SFT 和 RL 后训练。

Result: 以 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 初始化，Navalia 在 OfficeQA Pro、FinSearchComp、Finance Agent Benchmark、APEX-Agents-AA 与 Workspace-Bench-Lite 五个基准上均较基座模型取得一致提升。

Conclusion: Navalia 有效增强了证据整合、工具使用与长时程任务执行能力，并能泛化到更广泛的 Cowork 场景，包括通用办公任务。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）智能体正从对话式助手演进为能够自主规划、使用工具并执行复杂任务的协作智能体（Cowork Agents）。金融分析要求这些智能体整合跨文档证据并进行多步数值推理，但由专家编写任务成本高昂，而直接用 LLM 合成则难以生成具有可靠答案的高难度任务。我们提出 Navalia，一个面向 Cowork Agents 的框架，将可验证的任务合成与长时程后训练相结合。Navalia 以企业财务报告为数据源，从完整事实集中语义筛选支撑证据，并构建可执行的计算链，从而生成具有可追溯证据且步骤之间存在真实数据依赖的分析任务。我们进一步使用独立的 LLM 评审与程序化校验来验证证据一致性与答案正确性。经校验的任务随后用于轨迹采集、冷启动监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL），以提升长时程任务执行能力。Navalia 基于 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 初始化，在全部五个基准——OfficeQA Pro、FinSearchComp、Finance Agent Benchmark、APEX-Agents-AA 与 Workspace-Bench-Lite——上均较基座模型取得一致提升。这些结果表明，Navalia 提升了证据整合、工具使用与长时程任务执行能力，并可泛化到更广泛的 Cowork 场景，包括通用办公任务。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are evolving from conversational assistants into Cowork Agents capable of autonomous planning, tool use, and complex task execution. Financial analysis requires these agents to integrate cross-document evidence and perform multistep numerical reasoning, but expert task authoring is costly, while direct LLM synthesis struggles to produce challenging tasks with reliable answers. We introduce Navalia, a framework for Cowork Agents that combines verifiable task synthesis with long-horizon post-training. Using corporate financial reports as its data source, Navalia semantically selects supporting evidence from complete fact sets and constructs executable computation chains to generate analytical tasks with traceable evidence and actual data dependencies between steps. We further use independent LLM review and programmatic checks to verify evidence consistency and answer correctness. The verified tasks are then used for trajectory collection, cold-start supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL) to improve long-horizon task execution. Navalia, initialized from Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, achieves consistent gains over the base model across all five benchmarks: OfficeQA Pro, FinSearchComp, Finance Agent Benchmark, APEX-Agents-AA, and Workspace-Bench-Lite. These results demonstrate that Navalia improves evidence integration, tool use, and long-horizon task execution capabilities and generalizes to broader Cowork settings, including general office tasks.

</details>


### [21] [Pareto-Optimal RTL Code Generation via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26904)
*Koki Takeshita,Kenji Uebayashi,Hikaru Tomonari,Masaya Tsunokake,Koichiro Yawata,Tatsuya Sasaki,Yusuke Ohtsubo,Kota Dohi,Yasuhiro Sogawa*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at COLM 2026

Selection reason: 用多目标 RL 让 LLM 生成 PPA 帕累托最优 RTL 代码，属 LLM 自动代码（硬件）性能优化，奖励设计与集合级优化思路可迁移到 SemOpt。

TL;DR: 提出基于RLVR的LLM电路生成方法，以超体积贡献奖励优化多次生成所得电路集合的PPA质量与多样性；在RTLLM v2上相对超体积较无PPA优化基线提升13.3个百分点、较单目标强化学习提升14.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将PPA多目标聚合为单一标量奖励，难以同时产生低功耗、低延迟、小面积等不同权衡下的多样化帕累托最优解；实际设计中各目标重要性随需求和约束变化，因此需要可事后选择的多样化高质量候选集。

Method: 提出面向LLM电路生成的学习方法，优化多次生成所得整个电路集合在PPA目标空间中的质量与多样性；包括基于生成的训练流程，将帕累托解集覆盖率提升重构为序列化电路生成问题，以及基于超体积贡献（HVC）的奖励函数来衡量生成电路对帕累托前沿的贡献。

Result: 在RTLLM v2基准上，相对超体积较未进行PPA优化的基线提高13.3个百分点，较单目标强化学习方法提高14.4个百分点，并在电路多样性上表现更优。

Conclusion: 该工作首次针对LLM电路生成优化整个生成电路集合的质量与多样性，而非单一电路质量；实验表明其在PPA权衡覆盖和电路多样性方面均优于相关基线。

中文摘要：近期研究提出了带有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）方法，使LLM能够根据自然语言规格生成高质量RTL电路。现有方法通过将多个目标聚合为单一标量奖励来优化电路PPA（功耗、性能、面积），这使得难以同时生成在不同权衡（如低功耗、低延迟和小面积）下的多样化帕累托最优解。然而在实际设计中，各目标的相对重要性随产品需求和设计约束而变化；因此，获得多样化且高质量的候选集是有益的，设计者可以在事后选择偏好的权衡。在本工作中，我们首次提出一种用于基于LLM的电路生成的学习方法，该方法优化的不是单个电路的质量，而是通过多次生成在PPA目标空间中获得的整个电路集合的质量与多样性。所提方法包括：（1）基于生成的训练流程，将整个帕累托解集覆盖率的提升重构为序列化电路生成问题；（2）基于超体积贡献（HVC）的奖励函数，用于衡量生成电路对帕累托前沿的贡献程度。在RTLLM v2基准上的评估表明，所提方法相对于未进行PPA优化的基线在相对超体积上提高了13.3个百分点，相对于单目标强化学习方法提高了14.4个百分点，同时在电路多样性方面也表现出优越性。

Original abstract: Recent studies have proposed reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) methods that enable LLMs to generate high-quality RTL circuits from natural-language specifications. Existing approaches optimize circuit PPA (Power, Performance, Area) by aggregating multiple objectives into a single scalar reward, making it difficult to simultaneously generate diverse Pareto-optimal solutions across different trade-offs such as low power, low latency, and small area. However, in practical design, the relative importance of each objective varies depending on product requirements and design constraints; thus, obtaining a diverse and high-quality candidate set is beneficial in that designers can choose a preferred trade-off a posteriori. In this work, we are the first to propose a learning method for LLM-based circuit generation that optimizes, not the quality of a single circuit, but the quality and diversity of the entire circuit set obtained through multiple generations in the PPA objective space. The proposed method consists of (1) a generation-based training pipeline that reformulates coverage improvement of the entire Pareto solution set into a sequential circuit generation problem, and (2) a reward function based on Hypervolume Contribution (HVC) that measures how much a generated circuit contributes to the Pareto front. Evaluation on the RTLLM v2 benchmark shows that the proposed method achieves +13.3 percentage points in relative Hypervolume over a baseline without PPA optimization and +14.4 percentage points over a single-objective reinforcement learning method, while also demonstrating superiority in circuit diversity.

</details>


### [22] [DataPraxis: Scaling Analytics Tasks via Verified Workspace Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26924)
*Nan Jia,Wenxuan Shi,Jingqing Ruan,Zhizhao Zeng,Tianyi Zhai,Zhongda Su,Feng Hong,Zhengyu Chen,Wenhao Yan,Haojin Yang,Siqi Dai,Weipeng Zhang,Ke Zeng,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 通过“已验证工作区演化”合成分析任务并校验结果依赖，其可验证任务合成与轨迹训练范式对 agent 数据构建有参考价值。

TL;DR: 提出基于已验证工作区演化的分析任务合成框架 DataPraxis，构建 5,000 条验证轨迹并训练模型，在 DABStep/JobBench 上达到 55.11/51.9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业分析任务需要持续推理、迭代调查与可靠证据；直接从原始数据生成任务会忽略深层问题且验证证据不足。

Method: DataPraxis 通过验证发现和产物逐步演化工作区，继承并组合工作区以发现深层问题，并依据累积发现及依赖构建任务，在演化和组装后验证结果；据此构建 DataPraxis-5K 训练 DataPraxis-27B 与 DataPraxis-35B-A3B。

Result: DataPraxis-27B 在 DABStep 获 55.11、JobBench 获 51.9，匹配或超过 GLM-5.2 等前沿模型；DataPraxis-5K 含 5,000 条已验证轨迹，覆盖多领域与输出格式并公开。

Conclusion: 验证工作区演化可合成更深入且可验证的分析任务，并有效训练通用智能体的专业分析能力。

中文摘要：通用智能体正日益被期望执行专业分析工作，这要求持续推理、迭代调查和可靠证据。训练这些能力需要多样且具有挑战性的任务。然而，直接从原始数据生成任务往往会忽视通过分析才浮现的更深层问题，并且为验证提供的证据有限。为解决这些局限，我们提出 DataPraxis，一个基于已验证工作区演化的分析任务合成框架。该框架用经过验证的发现和产物逐步丰富工作区，然后继承并组合这些工作区以揭示更深层的问题。它根据累积的发现及其依赖关系构建任务，并在工作区演化过程中以及任务组装之后验证分析结果。利用该框架，我们构建了 DataPraxis-5K，这是一个包含 5,000 条已验证轨迹的数据集，涵盖多样领域和输出格式，用于训练 DataPraxis-27B 和 DataPraxis-35B-A3B。DataPraxis-27B 在 DABStep 上达到 55.11，在 JobBench 上达到 51.9，匹配或超越 GLM-5.2 等前沿模型。DataPraxis-5K 可在 huggingface.co/datasets/stargazerhust/DataPraxis-5k 获取。

Original abstract: General-purpose agents are increasingly expected to perform professional analytics work, which requires sustained reasoning, iterative investigation, and reliable evidence. Training these capabilities requires diverse and challenging tasks. However, generating tasks directly from raw data often overlooks deeper questions that emerge through analysis and provides limited evidence for verification. To address these limitations, we introduce DataPraxis, an analytics task synthesis framework based on verified workspace evolution. The framework progressively enriches workspaces with validated findings and artifacts, then inherits and combines these workspaces to uncover deeper questions. It constructs tasks from accumulated findings and their dependencies, verifying analytical results both during evolution and after task assembly. Using this framework, we construct DataPraxis-5K, a dataset of 5,000 verified trajectories spanning diverse domains and output formats, to train DataPraxis-27B and DataPraxis-35B-A3B. DataPraxis-27B achieves 55.11 on DABStep and 51.9 on JobBench, matching or surpassing frontier models such as GLM-5.2. DataPraxis-5K is available at huggingface.co/datasets/stargazerhust/DataPraxis-5k

</details>


### [23] [Fix, Not Just Resample: Bounded Compiler-Guided Repair in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26942)
*Haotian Deng,Chris Farber,Sonal Narasimha,Samskrithi Sivakumar,Yexiang Xue*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 利用编译器精确报错对失败步骤做有界在线修复，替代纯采样重试，直接对应代码优化 agent 的编译反馈修复回路。

TL;DR: 有界在线步骤级修复利用 Lean 编译器错误信息就地修正失败 tactic，在多个候选预算下提升求解率，且增益主要来自浅层执行错误并具有可预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯采样会丢弃证明助手对失败步骤给出的精确错误信息，因此希望利用编译器反馈改进自动定理证明的搜索效率。

Method: 提出有界在线步骤级修复模块：每步采样 K 个候选 tactic，对失败 tactic 最多修复 R 次，并将编译器错误消息输入预训练修复模型以就地修正被拒步骤。

Result: 在所有测试 K∈{1,3,5,8} 上优于仅采样基线，最高相对提升 32%，在 LeanDojo 的 1,990 定理子集上额外求解 82 个定理；修复以额外运行时间换取证明，采样受限时收益最大，首次修复捕获 84–96% 总增益，增益主要来自浅层 tactic 执行失败而非类型不匹配等结构错误；在 Lean Workbook 上几乎翻倍且零回退。

Conclusion: 定向步骤级修复能有效且可预测地提升定理证明求解率，尤其适用于采样受限场景，并可在第二个基准上复现。

中文摘要：自动定理证明器通常依赖对语言模型采样，但 Lean 等证明助手会用精确错误消息拒绝每个失败步骤——纯采样会丢弃这些信息。为克服这一点，我们引入一个有界在线步骤级修复模块。我们的搜索在每一步采样 K 个候选策略（tactics），并对失败策略最多修复 R 次。我们不是丢弃失败策略，而是将编译器错误消息输入预训练修复模型，以就地修复单个被拒步骤。该方法在所有测试候选预算（K∈{1,3,5,8}）上均优于仅采样基线，最高取得 32% 的相对提升，并在 LeanDojo 基准的 1,990 定理子集上额外求解 82 个定理。修复并非免费：它用额外运行时间换取可达证明。但这种权衡是可预测的：采样受限时修复收益最大；其回报前置，第一次修复尝试捕获总增益的 84–96%；增益几乎完全来自浅层策略执行失败，而非类型不匹配等结构性错误。这种定向修复在第二个基准（Lean Workbook）上成功复现，求解率几乎翻倍且零回退。

Original abstract: Automated theorem provers often rely on sampling language models, but proof assistants like Lean reject each failed step with a precise error message --- information that pure sampling discards. To overcome this, we introduce a bounded, online step-level repair module. Our search samples $K$ candidate tactics at each step and repairs a failed tactic at most $R$ times. Instead of discarding failed tactics, we feed the compiler's error message to a pretrained repair model to fix individual rejected steps in place. This approach improves the solve rate over a sampling-only baseline across all tested candidate budgets ($K \in \{1,3,5,8\}$), achieving up to a $32\%$ relative gain and solving $82$ additional theorems on a $1{,}990$-theorem subset of the LeanDojo benchmark. Repair is not free: it trades extra runtime for reachable proofs. But the trade is predictable: repair pays most when sampling is constrained; its return is front-loaded, with the first repair attempt capturing $84\text{--}96\%$ of the total gain; and the gain comes almost entirely from shallow tactic-execution failures rather than structural errors such as type mismatches. This targeted repair successfully replicates on a second benchmark (Lean Workbook), nearly doubling the solve rate with zero regressions.

</details>


### [24] [MAMath-Bench: Process-Aware and Robust Evaluation of Multimodal Multi-Agent Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26943)
*Xinping Wang,Jie Ma,Rui Xing,Wensong Guo,Xiayi Zhang,Jianhao Deng,Zihao Zhou,Boqian Yang,Feijuan He*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 多 agent 数学推理的过程感知评测，给出“协作不必然提升正确性、且对推理扰动脆弱”的一般性结论，可迁移到 MAS 可靠性评估。

TL;DR: 该文提出过程感知的多模态数学推理基准MAMath-Bench，从答案正确性、推理过程可靠性和对抗扰动鲁棒性评估多智能体系统，并发现协作未必提升最终准确率且系统仍易受细微推理错误影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM数学推理评估多聚焦纯文本最终答案正确性，难以揭示多模态推理能力、中间步骤可靠性以及对推理错误的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建MAMath-Bench，包含1,003道题、11个数学类别、多难度与文本/图像/音频模态，并提供细粒度里程碑标注，从问题求解正确性、推理过程可靠性和对抗性推理链扰动鲁棒性三个维度评估多智能体系统。

Result: 评估发现：多智能体协作不总能提升最终答案准确率；协作可提升中间推理覆盖度但可能增加推理冗余；当前系统对对抗性推理错误仍脆弱，尤其是细微结构扰动。

Conclusion: MAMath-Bench提供了过程感知的多模态数学推理评估框架，揭示多智能体协作的收益与局限，并强调需提升推理过程可靠性和对抗鲁棒性；数据集已公开。

中文摘要：数学推理是大型语言模型（LLMs）在教育应用中的一项基础能力，其中可靠的问题求解不仅需要正确的答案，还需要连贯且可验证的推理过程。多智能体系统（MASs）通过多个LLM智能体之间的协作与验证，在这一领域展现出日益增长的潜力。然而，现有评估主要集中于纯文本设置下最终答案的正确性，对多模态推理能力、中间推理步骤的可靠性以及对推理错误的鲁棒性所提供的洞见有限。为解决这些局限，我们引入MAMath-Bench，一个过程感知的基准，旨在评估MASs的多模态数学推理能力。MAMath-Bench包含1,003道问题，涵盖11个数学类别、多个难度级别以及多种模态，包括文本、图像和音频。它具有细粒度的里程碑标注，可从三个维度评估系统：问题求解正确性、推理过程可靠性以及对对抗性推理链扰动的鲁棒性。我们的评估得出三个关键发现。第一，多智能体协作相较于强单一LLM并不能持续提升最终答案准确率。第二，协作可以显著提高中间推理的覆盖度，但这种收益可能以推理冗余增加为代价。第三，当前系统仍然容易受到对抗性推理错误的影响，尤其是涉及细微结构扰动的错误。数据集可在https://github.com/mira-ai-lab/AgentMath获取。

Original abstract: Mathematical reasoning is a fundamental capability for educational applications of Large Language Models (LLMs), where reliable problem solving requires not only correct answers but also coherent and verifiable reasoning processes. Multi-agent systems (MASs) have shown growing potential in this domain by leveraging collaboration and verification among multiple LLM agents. However, existing evaluations primarily focus on the correctness of final answers in text-only settings, offering limited insight into multimodal reasoning capabilities, the reliability of intermediate reasoning steps, and robustness against reasoning errors. To address these limitations, we introduce MAMath-Bench, a process-aware benchmark designed to evaluate the multimodal mathematical reasoning capabilities of MASs. MAMath-Bench comprises 1,003 problems spanning 11 mathematical categories, multiple difficulty levels, and several modalities, including text, images, and audio. It features fine-grained milestone annotations to evaluate systems across three dimensions: problem-solving correctness, reasoning-process reliability, and robustness to adversarial reasoning-chain perturbations. Our evaluation yields three key findings. First, multi-agent collaboration does not consistently improve final-answer accuracy over strong single LLMs. Second, collaboration can substantially enhance the coverage of intermediate reasoning, but such gains may come at the cost of increased reasoning redundancy. Third, current systems remain vulnerable to adversarial reasoning errors, particularly those involving subtle structural perturbations. The dataset is available at https://github.com/mira-ai-lab/AgentMath.

</details>


### [25] [When Planning Problems Become Hard for LLMs: Separating Structural Complexity from Semantic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26964)
*Mayukh Debnath,Adeyinka Adedigba,Rammohan Mallipeddi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 10 pages, 1 figure, 17 supplementary pages

Selection reason: 分离规划的结构复杂度与语义可解释性，并研究 LLM 与符号规划器的混合路由，对 agent 规划难度建模与路由策略有启发。

TL;DR: 在七个STRIPS领域中，零训练删除松弛启发式h_add对结构难度的预测优于学习模型；谓词重命名显著降低LLM规划成功率；混合路由更依赖对LLM行为的校准，而非结构分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分经典规划中的结构难度与符号词汇解释难度，并评估结构预测器及谓词重命名对LLM规划的影响，以改进LLM与符号规划器的混合路由。

Method: 在七个BFS最优STRIPS领域比较h_add与岭回归、对象计数、ARC等结构预测器；逐步重命名谓词并保持状态、转移和最优规划不变；测量LLM规划成功率、拒答率及上下文难度预测；分析混合路由的影响因素。

Result: h_add在留出领域预测结构难度最优（平均Spearman |ρ|=0.931），优于岭回归（0.707）、对象计数（0.657）和ARC（0.400）；ARC也未胜过同特征线性模型。谓词重命名从0%到100%使有效LLM规划成功率从67.5%降至40.0%，拒答率保持0%。提供ARC条件信息的LLM上下文难度预测优于训练ARC（平均|ρ|=0.826）。混合路由更依赖对目标LLM结果的校准。

Conclusion: 已有符号启发式已能很好地估计结构难度；改进混合规划应建模LLM对谓词语义敏感的行为，而非继续开发更多结构预测器。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLMs）在经典规划中表现困难，但现有基准很少区分结构难度与解释符号词汇的难度。我们考察了七个具有BFS最优规划长度的STRIPS领域。为在不求解具体实例的情况下预测结构难度，零训练的删除松弛启发式 h_add 在留出领域上优于学习式替代方法（平均 Spearman |ρ|=0.931），包括岭回归（0.707）、对象计数（0.657）以及情景检索架构ARC（0.400）。ARC也未能胜过使用相同特征的线性模型。为隔离语义效应，我们在保持状态、转移和最优规划不变的同时逐步重命名谓词。有效的LLM规划成功率在0%到100%重命名之间从67.5%降至40.0%，而拒答率保持为0%。一个被提供ARC条件信息的LLM在上下文中预测难度的表现优于训练过的ARC（平均 |ρ|=0.826）。LLM与符号规划器之间的混合路由更多取决于对目标LLM结果的校准，而非所选择的结构分数。因此，已有的符号启发式已经能很好地估计结构难度；改进混合规划需要建模对谓词语义敏感的LLM行为，而不是开发更多结构预测器。

Original abstract: Large language models (LLMs) struggle with classical planning, but benchmarks rarely distinguish structural difficulty from the difficulty of interpreting symbolic vocabulary. We examine seven STRIPS domains with BFS-optimal plan lengths. For predicting structural difficulty without solving an instance, the zero-training delete-relaxation heuristic $h_{\mathrm{add}}$ outperforms learned alternatives on held-out domains (mean Spearman $|ρ|=0.931$), including ridge regression ($0.707$), object count ($0.657$), and the episodic retrieval architecture ARC ($0.400$). ARC also fails to outperform a linear model using the same features. To isolate semantic effects, we progressively rename predicates while preserving states, transitions, and optimal plans. Valid LLM planning success drops from $67.5\%$ to $40.0\%$ between $0\%$ and $100\%$ renaming, while refusals remain at $0\%$. An LLM supplied with ARC's conditioning information predicts difficulty in context better than trained ARC (mean $|ρ|=0.826$). Hybrid routing between an LLM and a symbolic planner depends more on calibration to the target LLM's outcomes than on the structural score selected. Thus, established symbolic heuristics already estimate structural difficulty well; improving hybrid planning requires modeling LLM behavior sensitive to predicate semantics rather than developing more structural predictors.

</details>


### [26] [SPEM: Structure-Aware Procedural Memory for Efficient Skill Use in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26995)
*Ran Zhou,Zehui Kong,Fanyu Lin,Jiaheng Liu,Zhen Zhang,Yuxiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 结构感知的程序性记忆（原子技能+Skill-DAG 复用与写回），直接对应 agent 技能/经验复用与效率优化，可迁移到代码优化技能库。

TL;DR: SPEM是一种轻量级结构感知过程记忆框架，通过原子技能记忆与技能-DAG记忆、Skill-GNN选择及成功执行写回，提升LLM智能体技能使用的效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能库扩展后，完整加载技能token与运行时开销高，而扁平语义检索忽略技能间依赖、执行顺序、状态转移和数据流约束，难以兼顾高效与准确。

Method: 提出SPEM，维护单个技能的原子技能记忆和成功执行衍生的可复用技能-DAG记忆；新任务优先复用高置信度技能-DAG模板，否则检索原子技能并用Skill-GNN进行状态与结构感知选择，成功执行写回为新技能-DAG。

Result: 在ALFWorld和SkillsBench上取得最佳整体有效性-效率权衡，ALFWorld成功率87.1%、SkillsBench奖励38.38%，并较强基线降低token和运行时成本。

Conclusion: 结构感知的过程记忆与技能组合复用能有效支持LLM智能体的大规模技能使用，并在效果与效率之间取得更优平衡。

中文摘要：基于LLM的智能体日益依赖可复用技能。然而，技能库规模扩大使得高效且准确的技能使用变得困难。完整加载技能会带来较高的token和运行时开销。相比之下，扁平语义检索将技能视为独立文本条目，忽略了依赖关系、执行顺序、状态转移和数据流约束。我们提出SPEM，一种轻量级、结构感知的过程记忆框架，用于基于LLM的智能体中高效使用技能。SPEM维护原子技能记忆（用于单个技能）和技能-DAG记忆（用于由成功执行衍生的可复用组合）。对于新任务，SPEM在可用时复用高置信度技能-DAG模板；否则，它检索原子技能并调用Skill-GNN进行状态与结构感知的技能选择。成功执行会被写回为新的技能-DAG，使过程记忆随时间累积。在ALFWorld和SkillsBench上的实验表明，SPEM实现了最佳的整体有效性-效率权衡，达到87.1%的ALFWorld成功率和38.38%的SkillsBench奖励，同时相较强基线降低了token和运行时成本。

Original abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly rely on reusable skills. However, scaling skill libraries makes efficient and accurate skill use difficult. Full skill loading incurs high token and runtime overhead. In contrast, flat semantic retrieval treats skills as independent text entries, ignoring dependencies, execution order, state transitions, and data flow constraints. We propose SPEM, a lightweight structure-aware procedural memory framework for efficient skill use in LLM-based agents. SPEM maintains Atomic Skill Memory for individual skills and Skill-DAG Memory for reusable compositions derived from successful executions. For a new task, SPEM reuses high-confidence Skill-DAG templates when available. Otherwise, it retrieves atomic skills and invokes Skill-GNN for state- and structure-aware skill selection. Successful executions are written back as new Skill-DAGs, allowing procedural memory to accumulate over time. Experiments on ALFWorld and SkillsBench show that SPEM achieves the best overall effectiveness--efficiency trade-off, reaching 87.1% ALFWorld success and 38.38% SkillsBench reward while reducing token and runtime costs against strong baselines.

</details>


### [27] [Does Multi-Agent Collaboration Reduce Hallucination in Clinical Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27027)
*Won Seok Jang,Harry Bhaskar Joshi,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 系统比较多种多 agent 拓扑对推理级幻觉的影响，得出协作只重分配已有能力而非提供新能力的实证结论，对 agent 架构设计有普遍参考价值。

TL;DR: 多智能体协作可提升临床任务表现，但不必然降低推理中的幻觉；幻觉水平及变化方向主要由模型而非拓扑或智能体数量决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多只检查最终答案，但临床医生会依据显式推理采取行动，因此需要直接在推理依据中衡量协作是否降低幻觉，并识别决定该效应的因素。

Method: 评估四种系统（单LLM基线与三种多智能体拓扑）在四个模型和三个临床任务上的表现；依据事实性与忠实性分类体系，对生成推理依据中的幻觉评分，并与任务指标并列报告；另开展自我纠正实验。

Result: 协作通常提升任务表现，但不一定降低幻觉，且无拓扑结构可靠占优；$36$个模型—系统—任务比较中$12$个可分辨，其中四个下降、八个上升，无模型同时出现在两侧；模型决定幻觉水平与变化方向。自我纠正实验中，除Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct外各模型均减少幻觉；协作降幻觉的模型获益最大，而Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct完全不降低幻觉。

Conclusion: 协作只是重新分配模型已具备的能力，并不能提供模型本身不具备的能力。

中文摘要：多智能体大语言模型系统能提升临床任务表现，而这种提升常被解读为系统更可信的证据。然而，这两个主张并不相同。临床医生依据其陈述的推理而非仅依据结论行事，因此协作是否减少幻觉、以及什么决定其何时减少，也必须在推理中测量。然而大多数评估只对最终答案评分。我们评估四种系统（一个单LLM基线和三种多智能体拓扑结构），覆盖四个模型和三个临床任务，在事实性与忠实性分类体系下，对每个系统生成的推理依据内部的幻觉进行评分，并与任务指标并列报告。我们报告：协作通常会换来任务表现提升，但并非必然降低幻觉，且没有一种拓扑结构可靠地优于另一种。效应因模型而异：在$36$个模型—系统—任务比较中，$12$个大到足以分辨——四个下降、八个上升，且没有模型同时出现在两侧。决定幻觉水平以及任何变化方向的是模型，而不是拓扑结构或智能体数量。一个自我纠正实验与此一致：在向模型展示其自身被标记的错误后，除Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct外，每个模型都减少了幻觉；协作能降低其幻觉的那个模型获益最大，而Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct——其幻觉被协作放大得最多的模型——完全没有降低其幻觉。我们发现，协作重新分配了模型已经具备的能力；它并不能提供模型不具备的能力。

Original abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems improve performance on clinical tasks, and that improvement is often read as evidence of a more trustworthy system. However, the two are not the same claim. A clinician acts on the stated reasoning and not only on the conclusion, so whether collaboration reduces hallucination, and what decides when it does, has to be measured in the reasoning too. Yet most evaluations score only the final answer. We evaluate four systems (a single-LLM baseline and three multi-agent topologies) across four models and three clinical tasks, scoring hallucination, under a factuality and faithfulness taxonomy, inside the rationale each system generates and reporting it alongside the task metric. We report that collaboration buys task performance but not lower hallucination as a rule, and no topology is reliably better than another. The effect differs by model: of the $36$ model--system--task comparisons, $12$ are large enough to resolve --- four decreases and eight increases, with no model on both sides. What sets the level, and the direction of any change, is the model rather than the topology or the number of agents. A self-correction experiment is consistent with this: shown its own flagged errors, every model except Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct reduces hallucination; the one model for which collaboration lowers hallucination gains the most, and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, the model whose hallucination it amplifies most, does not reduce its hallucination at all. We find that collaboration redistributes what its models can already do; it does not supply what they cannot.

</details>


### [28] [M-Plan: Comprehensive Planning For LLM Agents with Memory Arrangement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27032)
*Ziyang Liu,Jiakang Yuan,Jiayuan Fan,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 闭环多 agent 框架结合长短期记忆与全局-局部引导，在长程任务上验证，规划+记忆设计可迁移到 agent 长程执行。

TL;DR: M-Plan提出一种融合长期与短期记忆的闭环多智能体框架，通过全局-局部与过去-当前指导提升LLM智能体在多轮长时程任务中的准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体在单轮任务中表现突出，但在长时程任务和无先验训练环境中仍受限于长上下文处理、连续决策与推理能力不足；现有带长期记忆的MAS多依赖直接检索或摘要，检索记忆含历史噪声且缺乏任务适应，而任务特定指导型MAS又缺少相应长期记忆。

Method: 提出M-Plan闭环MAS框架，集成长期与短期记忆，并结合全局-局部和过去-当前指导；执行中提供任务特定里程碑与洞见，仅在困惑时给出局部提示，指导的生成与利用构成短期记忆；任务完成后优化或重组相关信息并存入长期记忆以供未来检索，保持即插即用设计。

Result: 在ALFWorld、Webshop和PDDL等多轮任务上跨多种模型开展大量实验，M-Plan在准确性和效率上均优于多个基线方法。

Conclusion: M-Plan通过融合长期与短期记忆及全局-局部、过去-当前指导，构建了区别于单独长期或短期记忆工作的综合框架，有效提升智能体在多轮任务中的表现。
