中文摘要：基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体近期在单轮任务中表现出色，但在长时程任务中仍面临困难，尤其是在未经先验训练的环境中。单个LLM的一个关键局限在于难以处理长上下文，这源于其在连续决策与推理能力上的不足。尽管现有配备长期记忆库的多智能体系统（MAS）可以部分弥补这一差距，但它们大多依赖知识复用或摘要，这种方法往往受限于直接检索而不针对当前任务进行任何适应，同时检索到的记忆可能包含大量有待预处理的历史噪声。相比之下，以任务特定指导为特征的MAS通常缺乏专门为该指导设计的长期记忆。为应对这些挑战，我们提出M-Plan，一个融合长期与短期记忆的闭环MAS框架。通过结合全局-局部与过去-当前指导，M-Plan在保持即插即用设计的同时提升智能体在多轮任务中的表现。在任务执行过程中，智能体接收任务特定的里程碑和洞见，并且仅在出现困惑时获得局部提示，这些共同构成全局-局部指导。这些指导的生成与利用也构建了短期记忆。任务完成后，当前任务的相关信息在被送入长期记忆存储以供未来检索之前会被优化或重组。上述设计构建了一个不同于其他单独长期或短期记忆工作的综合框架。在包括ALFWorld、Webshop和PDDL的多轮任务上跨多种模型的大量实验表明，M-Plan在准确性和效率上均优于多个基线方法。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents have recently demonstrated outstanding performance in single-turn tasks, yet they continue to struggle with long-horizon tasks, particularly in environments without prior training. A key limitation of single LLMs is their difficulty in processing long contexts, which stems from weaknesses in continuous decision-making and reasoning capabilities. Although existing multi-agent systems (MAS) equipped with long-term memory bases can partially address this gap, they mostly rely on knowledge reuse or summarization, an approach that is often constrained by directly retrieval without any adaptation for the current task, while the retrieved memory may contain much history noises waiting to be preprocessed. In contrast, MAS characterized by task-specific guidance often lack long-term memory specifically designed for the guidance. To address these challenges, we propose M-Plan, a closed-loop MAS framework that integrates long-term and short-term memory. By combining global-local and past-current guidance, M-Plan enhances agents' performance on multi-turn tasks while maintaining a plug-and-play design. During task execution, agents receive task-specific milestones and insights, and are provided with local hints only when confusion arises, which form global-local guidance together. These guidance generation and utilization construct the short-term memory as well. After task completion, relevant information about the current task is optimized or reorganized before being sent to the long-term memory storage for future retrieval. The designments mentioned above build a comprehensive framework different from other individual long-term or short-term memory works. Extensive experiments on multi-turn tasks including ALFWorld, Webshop, and PDDL across diverse models demonstrate that M-Plan outperforms multiple baseline methods in both accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [29] [ReuF2F: Benchmarking Coding Agents on Formalized Undergraduate Research Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27046)
*Zeyu Zheng,Yiyang Liu,Yuyang Zhao,Congmin Zheng,Jiachen Zhu,Jianian Chen,Kangning Zhang,Kayue Daniel Wong,Lingyu Yang,Rong Shan,Shihao Chen,Thomas Zhu,Wentao Zhang,Xiaopeng Li,Yan Hu,Yongxi Lin,Yuxiang Chen,Zhuoying Ou,Jianghao Lin,Jeremy Avigad,Prasad Tetali,Jingbang Chen,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Benchmark available at https://github.com/cmu-l3/reuF2F

Selection reason: 面向 Lean 4 开放研究问题的 coding agent 基准，并构建基于 mini-SWE-agent 的统一评测 harness，直接相关于 coding agent 评测与验证。

TL;DR: 提出Lean 4基准ReuF2F，包含244个来自REU的形式化开放问题，并采用Docker化编码智能体评估与Leanimum-agent统一测试框架；最佳模型仅解决约40%的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI开始解决开放研究问题后，可靠验证愈发重要；现有形式化数学基准多聚焦已知结果或少数著名开放问题，且评估协议不一定适配现代编码智能体。

Method: 构建ReuF2F：Lean 4基准，含244个来自本科生研究体验（REU）的形式化开放问题；将每个问题及其逻辑否定放入Docker容器中的Lean项目；在mini-SWE-agent上构建Leanimum-agent作为统一评估框架。

Result: 评估显示，即使表现最好的模型也仅解决约40%的问题。

Conclusion: 形式化开放研究问题仍存在很大改进空间，ReuF2F适合用于端到端开放问题求解的留出评估。

中文摘要：随着AI系统开始解决开放研究问题，可靠的验证变得日益重要。形式化数学为数学推理提供了一个可信框架，使智能体能够在进行数学探索的同时生成机器可检查的证明。现有的形式化数学基准通常聚焦于已知结果或一小部分著名开放问题，而一旦这些问题的解答被发现并广泛传播，此类基准可能难以被可靠地评估。现有评估协议也并不总是与现代编码智能体的运作方式相一致。因此，我们提出ReuF2F，这是一个Lean 4基准，包含244个形式化的开放问题，这些问题取自本科生研究体验（REU），涵盖广泛数学子领域中的入门级开放研究问题。我们预期，这些较不著名、往往关注较少且可能更长时间保持未解决的开放问题，更适合用于对Lean中端到端开放问题求解进行留出评估。我们采用标准的编码智能体评估设置，将每个问题及其逻辑否定放入Docker容器中的Lean项目。我们还在mini-SWE-agent上构建了Leanimum-agent，以提供一个统一测试框架，用于跨模型进行公平比较。我们的评估发现，即使表现最好的模型也仅解决了约40%的问题，这表明在形式化开放研究问题上仍有很大的改进空间。

Original abstract: As AI systems begin to resolve open research problems, reliable verification becomes increasingly important. Formal mathematics provides a trusted framework for mathematical reasoning, enabling agents to produce machine-checkable proofs while conducting mathematical exploration. Existing formal mathematics benchmarks often focus on known results or a small set of prominent open problems, which can become difficult to evaluate reliably once solutions are discovered and widely disseminated. Existing evaluation protocols are also not always aligned with how modern coding agents operate. We therefore introduce ReuF2F, a Lean 4 benchmark of 244 formalized open problems drawn from Research Experiences for Undergraduates (REU), spanning entry-level open research problems across a broad range of mathematical subfields. We expect these less prominent open problems, which tend to attract less attention and may remain unsolved for longer, to be better suited for held-out evaluation of end-to-end open problem resolution in Lean. We adopt a standard coding-agent evaluation setting, placing each problem and its logical negation into a Lean project in a Docker container. We also build Leanimum-agent on mini-SWE-agent to provide a unified harness for fair comparisons across models. Our evaluation finds that even the best-performing model resolves only ~40% of the problems, leaving substantial room for improvement on formalized open research problems.

</details>


### [30] [PROVISO: Provenance-Informed Action Selection for Secure LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27115)
*Bo Yan,Qi Zhang,Ziwen Kan,Fnu Suya,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 基于来源溯源（用户 vs 外部工具输出）的动作选择控制器，属 agent 工具使用安全与可靠性控制，可迁移到代码 agent 的验证/采纳决策。

TL;DR: PROVISO 在动作执行前利用来源追踪和替代动作比较进行校准价值决策，在多个智能体安全任务上同时提升安全性与效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御未追踪动作内容来自用户还是外部来源，且孤立判断动作而不与替代方案比较，导致安全性与效用难以兼顾。

Method: PROVISO 是一个执行前控制器：将可信观察、外部观察、被提议动作和替代动作编码为带来源类型的记忆，通过角色特定查询关联，并提交从结果中学习到的最高校准价值动作。

Result: 在四个智能体安全任务上，无基线在安全性和效用上同时超过 PROVISO；SkillsBench 上 F1 比最强基线高 13.84 个百分点，ToolSafe 上高 7.07 个百分点，三个额外技能检测基准达到 88–99 F1。

Conclusion: PROVISO 通过来源感知与替代方案感知的执行前决策，有效缓解安全性与效用之间的权衡，并优于现有基线。

中文摘要：LLM 智能体通过工具和第三方技能作用于外部世界，因此单次执行的动作可能造成不可逆伤害。现有防御在判断一个被提议的动作时，既不追踪其内容来自用户还是来自工具输出等外部来源，也将其孤立判断而不是与可用替代方案进行比较，从而迫使安全性与效用之间进行权衡。我们提出 PROVISO，一个控制器，它在智能体提出动作之后、执行之前决定提交哪个动作。PROVISO 将可信观察、外部观察、被提议动作和一个替代动作编码为带来源类型的记忆，通过角色特定查询将它们关联起来，并提交具有从结果中学习到的最高校准价值的动作。在跨越智能体生命周期的四个智能体安全任务上，没有基线在安全性和效用两方面同时超过 PROVISO。PROVISO 在 SkillsBench 上比最强基线将 F1 提高 13.84 个百分点，在 ToolSafe 上提高 7.07 个百分点，并在三个额外的技能检测基准上达到 88–99 F1。

Original abstract: LLM agents act on the external world through tools and third-party skills, so a single executed action can cause irreversible harm. Existing defenses judge a proposed action without tracking whether its content comes from the user or from external sources such as tool outputs, and they judge it in isolation rather than against an available alternative, which forces a trade-off between security and utility. We present PROVISO, a controller that decides which action to commit after the agent proposes an action and before it is executed. PROVISO encodes trusted observations, external observations, the proposed action, and an alternative into provenance-typed memory, relates them through role-specific queries, and commits the action with the highest calibrated value learned from outcomes. On four agent security tasks spanning the agent lifecycle, no baseline exceeds PROVISO in both security and utility. PROVISO improves F1 over the strongest baseline by 13.84 points on SkillsBench and by 7.07 points on ToolSafe, and it reaches 88-99 F1 on three additional skill detection benchmarks.

</details>


### [31] [PORA: Learning When More Reasoning Pays Off for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27119)
*Bo Yan,Qi Zhang,Zhen Tan,Tianlong Chen,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 学习在每步动作前分配短/长推理预算，显式权衡下游收益与预算压力，对长程 agent 的推理预算控制与效率优化有直接借鉴。

TL;DR: PORA通过在同一状态下配对比较短推理与长推理的结果，学习在有限预算下按状态分配推理长度，并在推理时结合剩余预算压力进行选择，从而在多种任务上提升效果并减少token与请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的总推理预算有限且需支持未来步骤；长推理可能减少后续错误，但会占用未来决策所需预算，因此需要估计长推理的下游价值，并判断其是否值得消耗剩余预算。

Method: 提出PORA：训练时从每个选定交互状态分别以短推理配额和长推理配额完成同一任务两次，比较最终质量与总处理量以估计长推理价值；推理时由控制器将该估计与学习到的剩余预算压力价格权衡，直接选择短推理或长推理，无需同时运行两种方案。

Result: 在应用、记忆、代码、零售和数学任务上评估，PORA在所有设置中均取得强劲结果；在BFCL记忆任务上比最强现有方法高17.4--32.9个百分点；在同一智能体内，于代码和数学任务上达到或超过始终使用长推理，同时分别减少50.6%的actor token和31.2%的模型请求。

Conclusion: PORA能够基于配对结果学习长推理的下游价值，并在剩余预算约束下自适应选择推理长度，在多个任务上实现更优或相当表现的同时显著降低token消耗和模型请求。

中文摘要：LLM智能体在多步任务中执行操作，并根据反馈修正动作。由于总推理预算通常有限且必须支持未来步骤，智能体必须在每个动作前在短推理与长推理之间做出选择。较长推理可能通过防止后续错误来改善最终任务结果，但也会消耗可能用于未来决策的预算容量。这带来两个挑战：估计长推理的下游价值，以及判断该价值是否值得使用剩余预算。我们提出配对结果引导的推理分配（Paired Outcome-guided Reasoning Allocation, PORA），它通过比较同一交互状态下的短推理和长推理来学习这一决策。在训练期间，PORA从每个选定状态将任务完成两次，一次使用短推理配额，一次使用长推理配额，并比较它们的最终质量和总处理量，以估计长推理何时有价值。在推理期间，控制器将该估计与学习到的关于剩余预算压力的价格进行权衡，并选择短推理或长推理，而无需同时运行两种备选方案。我们在应用、记忆、代码、零售和数学任务上评估PORA，在所有设置中均取得强劲结果。在BFCL记忆任务上，它比最强现有方法高出17.4--32.9个百分点。在同一智能体内，它在代码和数学任务上达到或超过始终使用长推理，同时分别减少50.6%的actor token和31.2%的模型请求。

Original abstract: LLM agents perform tasks over multiple steps and revise their actions based on feedback. As the total reasoning budget is often limited and must support future steps, the agent must choose between short and long reasoning before each action. Longer reasoning may improve the final task outcome by preventing later errors, but it also uses budget capacity that may be needed for future decisions. This creates two challenges: estimating the downstream value of long reasoning and deciding when that value is worth using the remaining budget. We propose Paired Outcome-guided Reasoning Allocation (PORA), which learns this decision by comparing short and long reasoning from the same interaction state. During training, \method{} completes the task twice from each selected state, once with a short reasoning allowance and once with a long allowance, and compares their final quality and total processing to estimate when longer reasoning is valuable. During inference, a controller weighs this estimate against learned prices on remaining-budget pressure and chooses either short or long reasoning without running both alternatives. We evaluate PORA across application, memory, code, retail, and mathematics tasks, achieving strong results across all settings. On BFCL memory, it exceeds the strongest existing method by 17.4--32.9 percentage points. Within the same agent, it matches or exceeds always using long reasoning on code and mathematics with 50.6% fewer actor tokens and 31.2% fewer model requests, respectively.

</details>


### [32] [EDMA: Entropy-Driven Multimodal Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27120)
*Emanuele Mezzi,Gertjan Burghouts,Fabio Massacci,Mengyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

Selection reason: 用逻辑熵量化各模态贡献并选择熵最低的推理轨迹，其轨迹选择/单调置信度代理机制可迁移到 agent 多步推理路径筛选。

TL;DR: EDMA用逻辑熵量化多模态问答中各模态对候选答案区分的贡献，构建可量化回答轨迹，并在标准MMQA基准上显著优于提示基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型方法虽将MMQA拆分为中间步骤，但未量化每种模态对最终答案的贡献；量化模态影响有望提升过程可追溯性与最终性能。

Method: 提出熵驱动多模态回答（EDMA），将MMQA建模为迭代熵降低过程；采用模态条件分区估计单模态熵降低、熵驱动多模态融合捕获互补信息，并基于熵选择使逻辑熵最小的回答轨迹。

Result: 在标准MMQA基准上，EDMA相比Planning Prompting平均提升12.8 F1和4.6 LLM-J点，相比Chain-of-Thought提升18.6 F1和8.3 LLM-J点；优于自一致性基线，并与多智能体系统基线具有竞争力。更高熵与更多假阳性相关，逻辑熵可作为正确推理路径的有效单调选择器。

Conclusion: 逻辑熵可作为多模态问答中模态贡献量化和推理路径选择的有效指标，EDMA通过熵驱动轨迹构建与选择提升性能并增强过程可追溯性。

中文摘要：多模态问答（MMQA）基于整合异构数据源、选择性利用相关模态并忽略干扰项。现有基于多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的方法通过将任务拆分为中间步骤来提升性能，但未量化每种模态对得出最终答案的贡献。我们认为，量化每种模态对回答过程的影响能够提升过程可追溯性和最终性能。因此，我们提出熵驱动多模态回答（EDMA），利用逻辑熵将多模态问答形式化为一个熵降低的迭代过程，并量化每种模态在区分正确与错误候选答案中的贡献。这能够构建可量化的回答轨迹，其中每一步都关联一个可测量的熵降低。EDMA引入（i）模态条件分区以估计单模态熵降低，（ii）熵驱动多模态融合以捕获跨模态互补信息，以及（iii）基于熵的选择，选择使逻辑熵最小的回答轨迹。在标准MMQA基准上评估，EDMA优于有竞争力的提示基线，相比Planning Prompting平均提升12.8 F1和4.6 LLM-J点，相比Chain-of-Thought提升18.6 F1和8.3 LLM-J点。此外，EDMA优于自一致性基线，并与多智能体系统基线具有竞争力。此外，我们表明更高熵与更多假阳性相关，并通过受控轨迹选择证明逻辑熵可作为正确推理路径的有效、单调选择器，验证熵水平是性能的可靠代理。

Original abstract: Multimodal question answering (MMQA) is based on integrating heterogeneous data sources, selectively leveraging relevant modalities, and ignoring distractors. Existing approaches based on Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) improve performance by splitting the task into intermediate steps, but do not quantify how each modality contributes to reaching the final answer. We argue that quantifying the effect that each modality has on the answering process improves process traceability and final performance. Thus, we propose Entropy-Driven Multimodal Answering (EDMA), which uses logical entropy to formalise multimodal QA as an iterative process of entropy reduction and to quantify the contribution of each modality in distinguishing correct from incorrect candidate answers. This enables the construction of quantifiable answering trajectories, where each step is associated with a measurable entropy reduction. EDMA introduces (i) modality-conditioned partitioning to estimate unimodal entropy reduction, (ii) entropy-driven multimodal fusion to capture complementary information across modalities, and (iii) entropy-based selection of the answering trajectory that minimises the most logical entropy. Evaluated across standard MMQA benchmarks, EDMA outperforms competitive prompting baselines, with average gains of 12.8 F1 and 4.6 LLM-J points over Planning Prompting and 18.6 F1 and 8.3 LLM-J points over Chain-of-Thought. Moreover, EDMA outperforms self-consistency baselines and is competitive with the multi-agent system baseline. Moreover, we show that higher entropy correlates with more false positives and, through controlled trajectory selection, demonstrate that logical entropy serves as an effective, monotonic selector for correct reasoning paths, validating entropy level as a reliable proxy for performance.

</details>


### [33] [Magnolia: A Persistent-Memory Agent Framework for Computational Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27230)
*Tao Jiang,Benjamin Navet,Yousouf Taghzouti,Fabien Gandon,Louis-Félix Nothias,Élise Dumont*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/Magnolia-AI

Selection reason: Magnolia 提出会话-项目-跨项目三层持久记忆与经验蒸馏、人工校验晋升机制，对 agent 长期记忆设计有直接参考价值。

TL;DR: Magnolia 是持久记忆 LLM 智能体框架，三层记忆整合对接、分子动力学、量子化学与实验记录；四个案例跟踪 317 次运行，受控重放显示记忆显著提升任务通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学数据与自动化快速增长，需要专用科学智能体帮助化学家处理大量数据和发现（包括阴性结果），并兼顾可复现性与人在回路控制。

Method: 提出 Magnolia 持久记忆人工智能智能体框架，基于 LLM 整合对接、量子化学等计算协议、实验室记录本和聊天日志；采用三层记忆：会话级观察自动捕获并提炼为结构化学习，确认后提升为项目级条目，经人类验证后晋升为跨项目规则以指导后续工作并提供化学反馈。

Result: 在四个案例研究（COMPAS 芳香体系量子化学评估、丁烷与修饰 DNA 分子动力学、蛋白质-肽对接）中，Magnolia 跟踪 317 次运行、积累 116 个项目级条目并晋升 4 条跨项目规则；四项任务受控重放中，带记忆运行通过无记忆运行未通过的检查，如对接配置检查 10/10 通过，而对照组仅 1–4/10。

Conclusion: Magnolia 结合自动化、可复现性和人在回路控制，简化对接、分子动力学和量子化学计算，并提供直接分析与 LLM 增强反馈，加速计算（生物）化学研究。

中文摘要：化学最近进入了一个高数据范式，因为来自高通量平台或计算研究的分子数据库已经可用。化学科学内部日益增加的自动化正在促成一种更加数字化和精准的化学。专用科学智能体正变得至关重要，用于引导化学家应对不断增长的数据和发现，包括阴性发现。在此，我们介绍 Magnolia，一种持久记忆人工智能智能体框架，它整合了从分子对接 to 量子化学的计算协议、实验室记录本和聊天日志，并依赖大语言模型（LLMs）。它依赖于一个三层记忆系统：会话级观察被自动捕获并提炼为结构化学习内容，经确认并提升为项目级条目，并在经过人类验证后，晋升为跨项目规则，这些规则主动指导后续工作并提供直接的化学反馈。我们在四个案例研究中展示 Magnolia：芳香体系的量子化学评估（COMPAS 数据库）、丁烷和修饰 DNA 的分子动力学，以及一个蛋白质-肽对接活动。在这些项目中，Magnolia 跟踪了 317 次运行，并积累了 116 个项目级条目，从中晋升了四条跨项目规则；在四项任务的受控重放中，带记忆的运行通过了无记忆运行未能通过的检查（例如，十项对接配置检查中通过十项，而对照组十项中仅通过一至四项）。通过结合自动化、可复现性和人在回路控制，我们的 Magnolia 框架简化了对接、分子动力学和量子化学计算，并提供直接分析和 LLM 增强反馈，为计算（生物）化学提供加速访问。

Original abstract: Chemistry has recently entered a high-data regime as molecular databases from high-throughput platforms or computational studies have become available. Increasing automation within the chemical sciences leads to a more digital and precision chemistry. Dedicated scientific agents are becoming central to guiding the chemist through an ever-increasing amount of data and findings, including negative ones. Here, we introduce Magnolia, a persistent-memory artificial intelligence agent framework that integrates computational protocols, from docking to quantum chemistry, lab notebooks and chat logs relying on large language models (LLMs). It relies on a three-tier memory system: session-level observations are automatically captured and distilled into structured learnings, confirmed and elevated to project-tier entries and, when human-validated, promoted to cross-project rules that actively guide subsequent work and provide direct chemical feedback. We illustrate Magnolia on four case studies: quantum-chemical evaluation of aromatic systems (COMPAS database), molecular dynamics of butane and of modified DNA, and a protein-peptide docking campaign. Across these projects, Magnolia tracked 317 runs and accumulated 116 project-tier entries, from which four cross-project rules were promoted; in controlled replays of four tasks, runs with memory passed the checks that runs without it failed (for example, ten of ten docking-configuration checks, against one to four of ten). By combining automation, reproducibility, and human-in-the-loop control, our Magnolia framework streamlines docking, molecular dynamics and quantum chemistry calculations with direct analysis and LLM-augmented feedback, offering accelerated access to computational (bio)chemistry.

</details>


### [34] [EGI-BENCH: An Enterprise General Intelligence Benchmark Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27234)
*Zhiwei Liu,Zhujun Lan,Jielin Qiu,Zixiang Chen,Liangwei Yang,Ming Zhu,Akshara Prabhakar,Wenting Zhao,John Emmons,Jason Wu,Shafiq Rayhan Joty,Junnan Li,Shelby Heinecke,Silvio Savarese,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 统一多套企业级 agent benchmark（含工具调用、多轮规划、长程软件工程）并给出细粒度子指标路由，可作为 agent 评测方法参考。

TL;DR: EGI-BENCH将八个企业级智能体基准统一到单一CLI，并通过子指标路由构建六维EGI分数；在quick模式下评测14个LLM，EGI为39.6–65.6，且无单一模型全面领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体评估分散在多个仓库中，运行器、依赖和评分约定不一致，导致跨基准比较困难，且单一聚合分数含义不清。

Method: 整合八个企业面智能体基准，提供统一命令行接口和两种受运行时预算约束的执行模式；将各基准细粒度子指标路由到六维EGI框架M1-M6，并描述quick/deep预算；在quick模式下评测GPT、Claude、Gemini系列共14个前沿与高性价比LLM。

Result: EGI总分从39.6（GPT-4o mini）到65.6（gpt-5.5）。没有模型在所有能力轴领先：gpt-5.5在任务结果、规划和检索grounding领先；Claude Sonnet 4.5在工具执行和安全/弃答领先；Claude Haiku 4.5在工程质量领先。

Conclusion: EGI-BENCH为企业智能体能力提供了统一、可比较的评测套件与六维评分框架，揭示不同模型的能力权衡；代码已开源。

中文摘要：企业采用智能体工作流中的LLM，受限于覆盖广泛能力范围的评估：CRM运营、异构文档检索、个人数据理解、多轮规划、工具使用、函数调用、长时程研究以及长上下文软件工程。如今这些评估分散在各自独立的代码库中，运行器、依赖项和评分约定各不相同，这使得跨基准比较困难，并掩盖了单一聚合数值实际代表什么。我们发布EGI-BENCH，一个统一的LLM智能体基准套件，将八个面向企业的智能体基准（CRMARENA、HERB、PERSONABENCH、USERBENCH、MCP-UNIVERSE、MCPEVAL、BENCHFORCE、LOCOBENCH）整合到单一命令行接口之后，并提供两种受运行时预算约束的执行模式。在该套件之上，我们定义企业通用智能（EGI）分数，一个六维框架（M1-M6），其各桶由从每个基准路由而来的细粒度子指标填充，而非基准级平均值。我们描述该套件、子指标路由以及quick/deep预算，并报告在quick模式下跨越GPT、Claude和Gemini系列的十四个前沿与高性价比LLM的结果。EGI总分范围从39.6（GPT-4o mini）到65.6（gpt-5.5），且没有单一模型在所有能力轴上占优：gpt-5.5在任务结果、规划和检索 grounding 上领先；Claude Sonnet 4.5在工具执行和安全/弃答上领先；Claude Haiku 4.5在工程质量上领先。代码见 https://github.com/SalesforceAIResearch/egi-bench。

Original abstract: Enterprise adoption of LLMs in agentic workflows is gated by evaluations that span a wide arc of abilities: CRM operations, heterogeneous-document retrieval, personal data understanding, multi-turn planning, tool use, function calling, long-horizon research, and long-context software engineering. These evaluations live today in separate repositories with divergent runners, dependencies, and scoring conventions, which makes cross-benchmark comparison difficult and obscures what a single aggregate number actually represents. We release EGI-BENCH, a unified LLM agent benchmark suite that consolidates eight enterprise facing agent benchmarks (CRMARENA, HERB, PERSONABENCH, USERBENCH, MCP-UNIVERSE, MCPEVAL, BENCHFORCE, LOCOBENCH) behind a single command-line interface with two runtime-budgeted execution modes. On top of the suite we define the Enterprise General Intelligence (EGI) Score, a six-dimensional framework (M1-M6) whose buckets are populated by fine-grained sub-metrics routed from each benchmark rather than by benchmark-level averages. We describe the suite, the sub-metric routing, and the quick/deep budgets, and report results for fourteen frontier and cost-efficient LLMs spanning the GPT, Claude, and Gemini families under quick mode. Top-line EGI ranges from 39.6 (GPT-4o mini) to 65.6 (gpt-5.5), and no single model dominates every capability axis: gpt-5.5 leads task outcome, planning, and retrieval grounding; Claude Sonnet 4.5 leads tool actioning and safety/abstention; and Claude Haiku 4.5 leads engineering quality. Code is available at https://github.com/SalesforceAIResearch/egi-bench.

</details>


### [35] [Recoverability and Verifier Strength: Two Measured Preconditions for Unattended Agent Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27465)
*De Wang,Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on AgenticOS. 6 pages + appendix

Selection reason: 提出验证器强度 σ 与可回滚性两个可测量前提，界定无人类干预的 agent 自我改进闭环边界，正是自动化代码/agent 自优化可靠性的核心问题。

TL;DR: 提出验证器强度σ与可恢复性两项可测指标，并给出仅当两者充分时才无人值守运行的自主体改进规则；在平台审批门和τ^2-bench实验中验证，发现LLM评审可能看似健康却任意批准，只有保留旧版本且测量验证器强度时变更才可无人值守。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主体平台开始自动读取执行轨迹、生成并验证变更且无人发布，需界定闭环中哪些步骤可安全无人值守。

Method: 定义并测量验证器强度σ（与可信参考验证器的一致性）和可恢复性（撤销坏变更的成本、是否需新验证批准、回滚恢复旧行为的程度）；构建应用该规则的平台，在平台审批门和τ^2-bench受控实验中测试。

Result: LLM评审在37个设置中9个无论输入如何都给同分且报告无错误；保存副本回滚可恢复原行为，重新生成撤销不能；部署中的变更使17%任务结果超出正常波动且撤销无法修复。

Conclusion: 只有保留旧版本并测量验证器强度，变更才可无人值守运行；两项指标支撑该规则。

中文摘要：Agent 平台正开始改进它们自己的 Agent：它们读取已部署 Agent 的执行轨迹，推导出一项变更，让自动验证器批准该变更，并在没有人类作者的情况下发布。这个闭环中有多少可以安全地在无人参与下运行？我们提出两个平台可以测量的量。验证器强度 σ 表示可用验证器——通常是 LLM 评审器——与可信参考验证器的一致程度；它限制一个步骤能在多大程度上改进 Agent。可恢复性指坏变更能被多低成本地撤销：撤销是否需要新的验证器批准，以及回滚能恢复多少旧行为。一个步骤只有在两者都充分时才应无人值守运行。在我们的平台上，该规则允许提示词、工具和技能编辑无人值守运行，而权重更新仍须人类签核。我们的主要贡献是这两个定义及其测量方法。我们构建了一个应用该规则的平台，并以两种方式测试：在平台自身的审批门上，以及在 τ^2-bench Agent 基准上的受控实验中。在验证器强度方面，平台的 LLM 评审器有时无论看到什么都给出相同分数（37 个设置中的 9 个），且未报告错误，因此验证器可能在批准任何内容时看起来仍健康；其强度必须被测量，而两个简单测试能捕获该失败。在可恢复性方面，恢复保存的副本使 Agent 回到原始行为，但重新生成的撤销不能，因此只有保留旧版本的变更才能在无人情况下被逆转；并且当一项变更被部署时，它使 17% 的任务结果超出正常运行间波动，而任何撤销都无法修复。这些共同支持该规则：一项变更只有在其旧版本被保留且其验证器强度已被测量时，才能无人值守运行。

Original abstract: Agent platforms are starting to improve their own agents: they read a deployed agent's execution traces, derive a change, have an automated verifier approve it, and release it with no human author. How much of this loop can safely run without a human? We propose two quantities a platform can measure. Verifier strength $σ$ is how well the available verifier -- often an LLM judge -- agrees with a trusted reference verifier; it limits how much a step can improve the agent. Recoverability is how cheaply a bad change can be undone: whether undoing it needs a new verifier approval, and how much of the old behavior a rollback restores. A step should run unattended only if both are adequate. On our platform this rule allows prompt, tool and skill edits to run unattended and keeps weight updates under human sign-off.
  Our main contribution is these two definitions and how to measure them. We built a platform that applies the rule and tested it in two ways: on the platform's own approval gate, and in controlled experiments on the $τ^2$-bench agent benchmark. On verifier strength, the platform's LLM judge sometimes gave the same score no matter what it was shown (9 of 37 settings) and reported no error, so a verifier can look healthy while approving anything; its strength must be measured, and two simple tests catch the failure. On recoverability, restoring a saved copy brought the agent back to its original behavior, but a regenerated undo did not, so only changes whose old version is kept can be reversed without a human; and while a change was deployed, it altered the outcome of 17 percent of tasks beyond normal run-to-run variation, which no undo repairs. Together these support the rule: a change can run unattended only when its old version is kept and its verifier's strength has been measured.

</details>


### [36] [TEX: Test-Time Scaling Testing Agents via Execution-based Cross-Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27290)
*Akash Gokul,Srijan Bansal,Yingbo Zhou,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 测试生成 agent 通过并行候选与执行交叉验证矩阵互相精化，是执行反馈驱动的 test-time scaling，与 coding agent 验证闭环高度相关。

TL;DR: TEX 通过执行驱动的交叉验证协作扩展测试时计算：并行生成测试与补丁并构建 K×K 反馈矩阵，在 SWT-Bench Verified 上达到 86.4%，随机选择输出也超过此前 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展方法要么孤立候选，要么聚合轨迹，难以适用于长时程任务；需要一种无需冗长轨迹共享、可客观验证的协作扩展机制。

Method: 提出 TEX 框架，并行化智能体生成测试和补丁，将每个测试与每个补丁交叉执行形成 K×K 反馈矩阵；各智能体利用对应矩阵切片迭代改进。

Result: 在 SWT-Bench Verified 上达到 86.4%，较单智能体基线提升 9.0%；随机选择 TEX 输出达 84.4%，超过此前最优 84.0%。

Conclusion: 执行驱动的协作反馈不仅能提升最佳样本，还能整体提升解决方案质量，是长时程智能体任务的有效测试时扩展机制。

中文摘要：测试用例生成对语言模型而言至关重要，但仍具挑战性。现有的测试时扩展方法要么孤立候选，要么聚合轨迹，无法扩展到长时程任务。我们提出 TEX，一个基于执行驱动交叉验证的协作式测试时扩展框架。TEX 并行化智能体以生成测试和补丁，将每个测试与每个补丁交叉执行，形成 $K \times K$ 反馈矩阵。智能体利用各自的矩阵切片进行改进，从而无需冗长的轨迹共享即可实现客观验证。在 SWT-Bench Verified 上，TEX 达到 86.4%，树立新的最优结果，并相较单智能体基线提升 +9.0%。此外，即使从 TEX 输出中随机选择（84.4%）也超过了此前最优（84.0%），表明协作式执行反馈提升了解决方案质量，而不仅仅是最佳样本。我们的结果强调，以执行为基础的反馈是长时程智能体任务的有效扩展机制。

Original abstract: Test-case generation is critical but remains challenging for language models. Existing test-time scaling methods either isolate candidates or aggregate traces, which do not scale to long-horizon tasks. We present TEX, a collaborative test-time scaling framework based on execution-driven cross-validation. TEX parallelizes agents to generate tests and patches, cross-executing each test against every patch to form a $K \times K$ feedback matrix. Agents use their matrix slices for refinement, enabling objective validation without verbose trace sharing. On SWT-Bench Verified, TEX achieves 86.4%, establishing a new state-of-the-art and improving +9.0% over single-agent baselines. Moreover, even random selection from TEX outputs (84.4%) surpasses the previous SOTA (84.0%), showing collaborative execution feedback improves solution quality, not just best-case samples. Our results highlight execution-grounded feedback as an effective scaling mechanism for long-horizon agentic tasks.

</details>


### [37] [Evolving Self-tuning Benchmarks for Long-Horizon Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27307)
*Hyunji Alex Nam,Michael Dennis,Natasha Jaques,Minsuk Chang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: TURTLE 用 proposer-solver-feedback 的 UED 自动演化长程工具使用任务，可作为 agent 训练与评测数据自生成机制迁移。

TL;DR: 提出 TURTLE：用 UED 与 proposer、solver、feedback generator 三类 LLM 智能体演化工具使用任务；在 AppWorld、τ-Bench、AutomationBench 上提升任务难度、多样性与失败模式覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用基准依赖人工监督或过于领域特定，难以规模化；AI 助手要可靠使用 Gmail、Venmo、Slack 等应用，需要更强的工具使用能力与可扩展评测数据。

Method: TURTLE 采用无监督环境设计（UED），由 proposer 演化任务、solver 执行任务、feedback generator 总结 solver 执行轨迹；根据 solver 的经验表现奖励困难但可解决的任务，从而生成自适应、可演化的测试套件，并适配用户模拟器、密集文档检索和多应用交互等不同环境需求。

Result: 在 AppWorld、τ-Bench 和 AutomationBench 上使用 Gemma 4 31B-it 的实验表明：演化任务达到目标难度范围（如 AppWorld 困难任务占比增加 165%），提升推理与并行工具交互方面的任务多样性，并比原始任务暴露更广泛的失败模式。

Conclusion: TURTLE 能以可扩展方式生成演化型工具使用测试套件，适配不同环境需求，并更全面地评估 AI 助手的工具使用能力与失败模式。

中文摘要：为了让 AI 助手超越简单的聊天界面，并可靠地协助用户使用 Gmail、Venmo 和 Slack 等应用，我们需要提升其工具使用能力。然而，现有的工具使用基准仍然有限且难以扩展，因为它们依赖人工监督或过于领域特定。为了实现数据生成的可扩展性，我们提出 TURTLE（Tool Use Related Tasks with Long-Horizon Evolution），这是一种无监督环境设计（Unsupervised Environment Design, UED）方法，利用 proposer、solver 和 feedback generator 三类大语言模型智能体来演化现有任务。proposer 会根据 solver 的经验表现，因生成困难但可解决的任务而获得奖励，feedback generator 则总结 solver 的执行轨迹，供 proposer 利用。TURTLE 生成不断演化、自我调整的测试套件，可适应不同的环境需求，从支持用户模拟器和密集文档检索，到跨多个应用交互。在 AppWorld、τ-Bench 和 AutomationBench 上使用 Gemma 4 31B-it 的实证结果表明，演化后的任务达到期望的难度范围（例如 AppWorld 上困难任务占比增加 165%），提升了任务多样性（在推理和同时进行的工具交互方面），并比原始任务激发出更广泛的失败模式。

Original abstract: For AI assistants to move beyond simple chat interfaces and reliably assist users with applications like Gmail, Venmo, and Slack, we need to improve their tool-use capabilities. However, existing tool use benchmarks are still limited and difficult to scale because they rely on human supervision or are too domain-specific. To make data generation scalable, we propose, TURTLE (Tool Use Related Tasks with Long-Horizon Evolution), an Unsupervised Environment Design (UED) approach to evolve existing tasks using a proposer, a solver, and a feedback generator large language model agents. The proposer is rewarded for difficult yet solvable tasks based on the solver's empirical performance, and the feedback generator summarizes the solver's execution traces for the proposer to exploit. TURTLE produces evolving, self-tuning test suites adaptable to different environment requirements, from supporting user simulators and dense document retrieval to interacting across multiple apps. Empirical results on AppWorld, $τ$-Bench, and AutomationBench using Gemma 4 31B-it show that the evolved tasks target the desired difficulty range (e.g., a 165% increase in the percentage of hard tasks on AppWorld), increase task diversity (in terms of reasoning and simultaneous tool interactions), and elicit a broader range of failure modes than the original tasks.

</details>


### [38] [Unsupervised Tool Evolution for Coding Agents in Data-Lake Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27327)
*Yuqing Yang,Yurong Liu,Deqing Fu,Manav Garg,Sravan Babu Bodapati,Fatma Ozcan,Alon Y. Halevy,Jiani Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: TEAL 在无标注下为 coding agent 演化可复用工具库与使用技能，依赖轨迹证据与重跑采纳判据，与自动代码优化 agent 的工具沉淀直接相关。

TL;DR: TEAL 在无监督设置下通过 Explorer 和 Critic 演化 Worker 的工具库与使用技能，并用 TEAL-Q 生成合成问题；在 KramaBench/LakeQA 上于紧步骤预算下显著提升多个 Worker，且合成问题演化效果接近真实问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体回答数据湖分析问题后丢弃发现、清洗和连接数据的工作，下一问题从头重做；真实标签需专家先解，因此需要无标签的工具演化来复用工作。

Method: 提出 TEAL：Explorer 从数据湖、Critic 从 Worker 轨迹提出工具；仅当重跑无需额外步骤即被采用且变更答案有轨迹证据支持才保留。TEAL-Q 在无真实问题时生成合成问题用于演化。以步骤预算评估四个 Worker LLM。

Result: 在 KramaBench 和 LakeQA，10/15 步紧预算下 TEAL 提升所有 Worker；Sonnet-5 在 15 步分别提升 9.3 和 21.7 个百分点。LakeQA 库在 20 步演化、30 步部署得 68.3，高于基线 59.6 和直接 30 步演化 64.2；合成问题演化与真实问题相当。

Conclusion: 无需标签即可演化出有用工具；为无监督智能体演化中演化什么以及如何获得反馈提供启示。

中文摘要：编码智能体可以通过发现、清洗和连接相关数据来回答数据湖上的分析性问题。然而，它们会在每个问题之后丢弃这项工作，并在下一个问题上从头重做。工具演化可跨任务自动构建和精炼可复用工具，自然适合这种重复性工作。由于数据湖问题的真实标签需要专家先解决该问题才能获得，我们考虑无监督设置。我们提出 TEAL（Tool Evolution for Analytics over data Lakes），它在无标签情况下为给定的 Worker 智能体演化一个工具库和使用技能。两个智能体从互补证据中提出工具：Explorer 来自数据湖本身，Critic 来自 Worker 轨迹。仅当 Worker 在重跑时无需额外步骤就采用该工具，且任何发生变化的答案都由轨迹证据支持时，才保留所提议的更改。我们还引入 TEAL-Q，它在真实问题不可用时生成合成问题以进行工具演化。我们在 KramaBench 和 LakeQA 上，在步骤预算下用四个 Worker LLM 进行评估；步骤预算限制 Worker 每个问题可采取的步数。在 10 或 15 步的紧预算下，TEAL 在两个基准上改进每个 Worker，使 Sonnet-5 在 15 步时在 KramaBench 上提升 9.3 个百分点，在 LakeQA 上提升 21.7 个百分点。更紧的预算也为演化带来更好的反馈。例如，Sonnet-5 的 LakeQA 工具库在 20 步时演化、在 30 步时原样部署，得分为 68.3，高于基线（59.6）和直接在 30 步时演化的工具库（64.2）。此外，在合成问题上演化的工具库与在真实问题上演化的工具库表现相当。这些结果表明，无需标签即可演化出有用的工具，并有望启发未来关于在无监督智能体演化中演化什么以及如何获得反馈的研究。

Original abstract: Coding agents can answer analytical questions over a data lake by discovering, cleaning, and joining the relevant data. However, they discard this work after each question and redo it from scratch for the next one. Tool evolution, which automatically builds and refines reusable tools across tasks, is a natural fit for this repetition. Since a ground-truth label for a data-lake question requires an expert to solve it first, we consider the unsupervised setting. We propose TEAL (Tool Evolution for Analytics over data Lakes), which evolves a library of tools and usage skills for a given Worker agent without labels. Two agents propose tools from complementary evidence, an Explorer from the data lake itself and a Critic from Worker trajectories. A proposed change is kept only if the Worker adopts the tool on a rerun without extra steps and any changed answer is supported by trajectory evidence. We also introduce TEAL-Q, which generates synthetic questions for tool evolution when real ones are unavailable. We evaluate on KramaBench and LakeQA with four Worker LLMs under step budgets, which cap the number of steps the Worker may take per question. With a tight budget of 10 or 15 steps, TEAL improves every Worker on both benchmarks, lifting Sonnet-5 at 15 steps by 9.3 points on KramaBench and 21.7 points on LakeQA. A tighter budget also yields better feedback for evolution. For example, Sonnet-5's LakeQA library evolved at 20 steps and deployed unchanged at 30 steps scores 68.3, above both the baseline (59.6) and a library evolved directly at 30 steps (64.2). Additionally, a library evolved on synthetic questions performs comparably to one evolved on real questions. These results show that useful tools can be evolved without labels, and hopefully inspire future work on what to evolve and how to obtain feedback in unsupervised agent evolution.

</details>


### [39] [Modeling Authentic Research Trajectories: A Graph View of Research Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27337)
*Ruochen Li,Zhiyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: A research progress graph

Selection reason: 提出 Research Progress Graph 显式建模带证据与推理的轨迹演化，其轨迹表示与评估视角可迁移到 agent 轨迹建模与训练。

TL;DR: 提出研究进展图（RPG）来完整记录真实研究轨迹中的状态演化、认知转换、时间索引与推理；比较35个LLM研究智能体后发现它们都是不完整RPG，并主张以轨迹预测、评估和训练重塑研究智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文中的研究想法通常并非项目最初想法；真实研究轨迹包含实验、洞见、挑战、文献探索和想法修订背后的证据与推理，但很少被记录。现有LLM研究智能体会丢弃想法版本之间的证据和推理。

Method: 提出RPG表示真实且完整的研究轨迹，将想法演化建模为带时间索引和推理标注的类型化研究状态及认知转换；以作者在注释和论文中论证步骤的真实轨迹为依据，并将35个研究智能体的表示与RPG进行比较。

Result: 现有35个研究智能体均对应不完整的RPG；没有一个把发生变化的状态、改变该状态的证据以及连接二者的推理保留为单一演化记录。

Conclusion: RPG要求研究智能体预测下一个研究状态而非一次性生成完整想法，按轨迹而非最终文本评估想法，并用真实轨迹作为训练数据，从而推动研究智能体从生成静态想法转向建模想法如何演化。

中文摘要：已发表论文中的研究想法很少是项目最初的想法。二者之间存在一个闭环过程：研究人员在该过程中运行实验、提炼洞见、识别挑战、探索文献并修订想法，每一步都由他们能够明确表达的证据和推理所驱动。我们将一个想法经过该过程所走过的路径，连同每一步背后的证据和推理，称为其真实研究轨迹。尽管它构成每一项科学贡献的基础，却很少被记录。现有的基于LLM的研究智能体一次性生成想法，依据评审、文献或自身实验结果对其加以改进，或在候选想法和实验上进行搜索。在所有这些方法中，想法的文本不断演化，而连接一个版本与下一个版本的证据和推理却被丢弃。我们提出研究进展图（RPG），一种对真实且完整研究轨迹的表示。RPG将想法的演化记录为通过认知转换连接的类型化研究状态，每个状态和转换都标注时间索引及其背后的推理，从而使这种演化成为明确的研究对象。我们将该表示建立在真实轨迹之上，这些轨迹的步骤由其作者在注释和论文中给出依据。将35个研究智能体的表示与RPG进行比较，我们发现每一个都是不完整的RPG；没有一个将发生变化的状态、改变该状态的证据以及连接二者的推理保留为一条不断演化的记录。用RPG对想法形成进行建模，会改变研究智能体的设计、评估和训练方式。智能体预测下一个研究状态，而不是预测一个完整想法；想法按轨迹而非最终文本进行评估；真实轨迹可作为训练数据。这些方向使研究智能体从生成静态想法转向建模想法如何演化。

Original abstract: The research idea in a published paper is rarely the project's initial idea. Between the two lies a closed-loop process in which researchers run experiments, derive insights, identify challenges, explore the literature, and revise the idea, with each step motivated by evidence and reasoning they can articulate. We call the path one idea takes through this process, with the evidence and reasoning behind each step, its authentic research trajectory. Although it underlies every scientific contribution, it is rarely recorded. Existing LLM-based research agents generate an idea once, refine it against reviews, literature, or their own experimental results, or search over candidate ideas and experiments. In all of them the text of the idea evolves, while the evidence and reasoning that connected one version to the next are discarded. We propose the Research Progress Graph (RPG), a representation of authentic and complete research trajectories. An RPG records the evolution of an idea as typed research states connected by cognitive transitions, each annotated with a temporal index and the reasoning behind it, which makes this evolution an explicit object of study. We ground the representation in authentic trajectories whose steps their authors justify in annotations and papers. Comparing the representations of thirty-five research agents with the RPG, we find that each is an incomplete RPG; none keeps the state that changed, the evidence that changed it, and the reasoning connecting them as one evolving record. Modeling ideation with the RPG changes how research agents should be designed, evaluated, and trained. Agents predict the next research state instead of a complete idea, ideas are evaluated as trajectories instead of final texts, and authentic trajectories serve as training data. These directions shift research agents from generating static ideas to modeling how ideas evolve.

</details>


### [40] [An LLM-Based Agent for Legal Compliance Assessment: Architecture, Memory, and Evaluation in a Canadian and Quebec Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27347)
*Soukaina Alaoui Ismaili,Jean-Sebastien Dessureault*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 合规评估 agent 描述了分层记忆组织与自愈重写-解释-升级的纠错链，虽为法律垂类，纠错与人工升级流程可借鉴到 agent 可靠性控制。

TL;DR: 提出 ComplianceAgent，用大型语言模型按固定量表并在参考法律语料库支撑下评估文本合规性，阈值触发自我修复重写、合规标签、可解释性报告和人工升级；在加拿大和魁北克十三部法律文书语料及十五份人工标注金标准上，实现合规/不合规完全区分、重复查询得分稳定和相关段落检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工审查法律合规性速度慢，关键词规则方法脆弱，需要一种可扩展、可解释且能结合参考法律语料库的自动化合规评估方案。

Method: ComplianceAgent 作为 COMPASS 多智能体框架的法律合规子代理，使用大型语言模型按固定评分量表打分，将判断锚定在参考法律语料库中；当得分达到或低于关键阈值时，执行包含自我修复式重写、合规标签、可解释性报告和人工审核升级的纠正链。文中还说明各步骤的模型行为、代理记忆组织方式以及同一实现适配不同参考语料库的能力。

Result: 在加拿大和魁北克语境下，使用涵盖联邦、省级、国际条约和三河市市政文件的十三部法律文书语料库，并以十五份人工标注文本为金标准进行评估；分类指标、重复查询稳定性测试、检索精确率和综合得分显示，代理能区分所有合规与不合规文本，重复查询得分稳定，索引返回相关段落。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于大型语言模型、参考法律语料库和阈值触发纠正链的 ComplianceAgent 可在加拿大和魁北克法律语境下有效完成合规评分、稳定复现与相关法律段落检索，并具备可解释性与人工升级机制。

中文摘要：手动检查文本是否符合一整套法律体系速度慢，而使用关键词规则则脆弱。本文提出 ComplianceAgent，即 COMPASS 的法律合规子代理，COMPASS 是一个多智能体框架，从四个维度评估文本：数字主权、碳影响、合规性和伦理。该代理依赖一个大型语言模型，按固定评分量表对文本评分，将其判断建立在参考法律语料库上，并且当得分达到或低于关键阈值时，运行由自我修复式重写、合规标签、可解释性报告和升级给人工审核者组成的纠正链。我们描述语言模型在每个步骤做什么、代理的记忆如何组织，以及同一实现如何指向不同的参考语料库。我们在加拿大和魁北克背景下评估该代理，使用由十三部法律文书组成的语料库，汇集联邦和省文本、国际条约以及三河市市政文件。评估依赖由十五份文本组成的人工标注金标准，并结合分类指标、重复查询稳定性测试、检索精确率和综合得分。在该金标准上，代理将每份合规文本与每份不合规文本区分开，其得分在重复查询下保持稳定，索引返回相关段落。

Original abstract: Checking whether a text complies with a body of law is slow when done by hand and fragile when done with keyword rules. This paper presents ComplianceAgent, the legal compliance sub-agent of COMPASS, a multi-agent framework that evaluates texts along four axes: digital sovereignty, carbon impact, compliance, and ethics. The agent relies on a large language model that scores a text on a fixed rubric, grounds its judgment in a reference legal corpus, and, when the score falls at or below a critical threshold, runs a corrective chain made of self-healing rewrites, a compliance label, an explainability report, and escalation to a human reviewer. We describe what the language model does at each step, how the memory of the agent is organized, and how the same implementation can be pointed at different reference corpora. We evaluate the agent in a Canadian and Quebec context, on a corpus of thirteen legal instruments that brings together federal and provincial texts, international treaties, and municipal documents of the City of Trois-Rivières. The evaluation relies on a hand-annotated gold standard of fifteen texts and combines classification metrics, a repeated-query stability test, retrieval precision, and a composite score. On this gold standard the agent separates every conforming text from every non-conforming one, its scores remain stable under repeated queries, and the index returns relevant passages.

</details>


### [41] [REM: What to Store, When to Recall, What to Forget in Experience Memory for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27377)
*Xinyi Lisa Chen,Pedro Herrero-Vidal,Xiaohan Chen,Xiaojing Yu,Cris Liu,Bingqing Song,Bin Xu,Suleiman A. Khan,You-Lin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Continual Learning for Enterprise AI Agents (CLEA). 16 pages, 3 figures, 14 tables

Selection reason: REM 在 coding agent 思考-行动循环内管理经验记忆的存/取/忘生命周期，直接提升仓库级 bug 修复成功率，与 SemOpt 类经验复用高度契合。

TL;DR: REM从智能体思考—行动循环内部管理经验记忆的存储、召回与遗忘，在仓库级缺陷修复上以更低成本提升检索准确率和任务解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统侧重检索最相关历史轨迹，但检索无法观察运行中的智能体，难以判断其是否需要经验、何时需要以及保留多久。

Method: 提出REM框架，在智能体循环内通过三个显式决策管理记忆生命周期：将完成轨迹整合为阶段结构化文档；任务开始时注入文档，并在循环内门控检测到困难时才提供更昂贵的实现记录；记录单次暴露后即逐出。

Result: 在SWE-ContextBench Lite上，REM的match@1为68.7%，优于所有最先进记忆系统的55.6%；最终任务解决率为27.6%，高于24.9%，成本降低17%，且每个重复实验均有提升。以摘要或原始轨迹注入同一经验，即使理想检索也无效；消融任一决策均降低解决率。

Conclusion: 经验复用的瓶颈不在检索保真度，而在经验的存储与生命周期治理；这为智能体记忆提供了控制论视角。

中文摘要：大型语言模型（LLM）智能体如今可通过数百个“思考—行动”步骤自主完成长时程任务，例如仓库级编程，但每次运行产生的经验很少被下一次运行复用。此前的记忆系统集中于检索，即寻找最相关的历史轨迹。检索无法观察正在运行的智能体，因此无法决定智能体是否需要某段经验、在何时需要以及需要多久。我们提出REM（Reusable Experience Memory，可复用经验记忆），一个从智能体“思考—行动”循环内部管理记忆生命周期的框架，通过三个明确决策实现。存储什么：每条完成的轨迹被整合为阶段结构化文档，其规模比轨迹小两个数量级。何时召回：文档在任务开始时注入；成本更高的实现记录会被暂缓，直到循环内门控检测到智能体陷入困难。遗忘什么：该记录在单次暴露后被逐出。在仓库级缺陷修复（SWE-ContextBench Lite）上，REM比所有最先进的记忆系统更准确地检索相关轨迹（68.7% 对 55.6% match@1），并在每个重复实验中提升最终任务解决率（27.6% 对 24.9%），同时成本降低17%。将同一经验以摘要或原始轨迹形式注入，即使通过理想检索获得，也不会产生同样效果。消融三个决策中的任意一个都会降低解决率。总之，这些结果表明，经验复用的瓶颈不是检索保真度，而是经验如何被存储并在其生命周期中被治理，从而为智能体记忆开辟了控制论视角。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents now autonomously complete long-horizon tasks, such as repository-level coding, through hundreds of think-act steps, yet the experience each run produces is rarely reused on the next. Prior memory systems concentrate on retrieval, finding the most relevant past trajectory. Retrieval cannot observe the running agent, so it cannot decide whether the agent needs an experience, at which point, or for how long. We introduce REM (Reusable Experience Memory), a framework that manages the memory lifecycle from inside the agent's think-act loop through three explicit decisions. What to store: each completed trajectory is consolidated into a phase-structured document, two orders of magnitude smaller than the trajectory. When to recall: the document is injected at the start of the task; the costlier implementation record is withheld until an in-loop gate detects the agent struggling. What to forget: the record is evicted after a single exposure. On repository-level bug fixing (SWE-ContextBench Lite), REM retrieves the related trajectory more accurately than every state-of-the-art memory system (68.7% vs. 55.6% match@1) and improves, in every replicate, end-task resolution (27.6% vs. 24.9%) at 17% lower cost. Injecting the same experience as a summary or raw trajectory, even when oracle-retrieved, does not. Ablating any of the three decisions reduces resolution. Altogether, these results demonstrate that the bottleneck in experience reuse is not retrieval fidelity but how experience is stored and governed across its lifecycle, opening a control-theoretic perspective on agent memory.

</details>


### [42] [ProMemAgent: Long-Horizon Proactive Assistance with Context-Aware Prospective Memory from Sensory Perceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27400)
*Lilin Xu,Linyang He,Kaiyuan Hou,Yuang Fan,Minghui Zhao,Xuhai Xu,Xiaofan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出面向长程主动协助的前瞻性记忆agent与基准，其情境记忆组织与检索机制可迁移到通用agent记忆设计。

TL;DR: 提出 ProMemAgent，将前瞻记忆引入日常感官上下文以支持长时程主动辅助，并构建 CPMBench 基准；实验显示其在记忆检索和长时程主动辅助上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动智能体多关注即时需求，难以处理需数小时或数天后执行的延迟意图；人类依靠前瞻记忆解决该问题。

Method: ProMemAgent 将第一人称视频、音频等过去感官感知组织为上下文感知记忆，生成前瞻线索并关联早期事件与后续发展；在当前上下文中检索相关事件并进行记忆增强的主动推理，判断是否需要辅助。同时构建包含 1,000 个实例、覆盖八个日常类别的 CPMBench。

Result: ProMemAgent 取得最佳记忆检索性能，并在长时程主动辅助任务上持续优于所有基线。

Conclusion: 前瞻记忆与感官上下文记忆相结合可有效支持长时程主动辅助，ProMemAgent 与 CPMBench 为该方向提供了方法与评测基础。

中文摘要：大语言模型（LLM）智能体正从被动响应转向主动辅助，即在没有明确请求的情况下预判用户需求。然而，现有的主动智能体主要处理即时需求，而许多日常情境涉及延迟意图，这些意图只能在数小时或数天后才能执行。人类通过前瞻记忆来处理此类意图，即记住在恰当时机到来时执行某一意图的能力。本文提出 ProMemAgent，这是首个将前瞻记忆融入日常感官上下文以实现长时程主动辅助的主动智能体。ProMemAgent 将过去的感官感知（例如第一人称视频和音频）组织为上下文感知记忆，生成前瞻线索，并将较早事件与后续发展关联起来以供未来检索。给定当前上下文，它检索相关的过去事件，并执行记忆增强的主动推理，以判断是否需要提供辅助。我们进一步提出 CPMBench，这是首个用于评估基于感官上下文的长时程主动辅助的基准，包含涵盖八个日常类别的 1,000 个实例。大量实验表明，ProMemAgent 取得了最佳的记忆检索性能，并在长时程主动辅助上持续优于所有基线。

Original abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are moving beyond reactive responses toward proactive assistance that anticipates user needs without explicit requests. However, existing proactive agents primarily address immediate needs, while many everyday situations involve delayed intentions that can only be acted on hours or days later. Humans handle such intentions through prospective memory, the ability to remember to act on an intention when the right moment arises. In this paper, we introduce ProMemAgent, the first proactive agent to incorporate prospective memory over everyday sensory contexts for long-horizon proactive assistance. ProMemAgent organizes past sensory perceptions (e.g., egocentric video and audio) into context-aware memory, generating prospective cues and linking earlier events with subsequent developments for future retrieval. Given the current context, it retrieves relevant past events and performs memory-augmented proactive reasoning to determine whether assistance is needed. We further introduce CPMBench, the first benchmark for evaluating long-horizon proactive assistance over sensory contexts, comprising 1,000 instances across eight everyday categories. Extensive experiments show that ProMemAgent achieves the best memory retrieval performance and consistently outperforms all baselines on long-horizon proactive assistance.

</details>


### [43] [More Memory, Worse Agents: Error Reproduction and Anti-Persistence in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27406)
*Ruhan Wang,Kishan Panaganti,Dongruo Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 31 pages, 12 figures, 7 tables, including appendices

Selection reason: 系统研究外部记忆中抽象规则导致错误复现的问题，并提出按需重建指导的抗持久化记忆设计，对agent自我改进与经验复用很有启发。

TL;DR: 论文提出Anti-Persistence记忆机制：只持久化轨迹与结果，按需生成并执行后丢弃任务指导，以抑制抽象错误在“抽象—执行—再抽象”循环中的再现，并在成功率与LLM调用成本上优于对比记忆设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体可通过外部记忆复用经验而无需更新参数，但若持久化规则、摘要、技能等抽象内容，其中错误会经检索复用传播，并通过塑造执行轨迹在再抽象中复制自身，形成错误再现。核心问题是记忆应保留原始经验还是抽象知识。

Method: 提出Anti-Persistence：累积轨迹与结果，不持久化抽象指导；用自适应选择器根据先前结果决定是否及如何合成任务特定指导，指导仅在执行时使用并在执行后丢弃，从而避免其作为存储对象被直接复用。

Result: 在不同基准和主干模型上，Anti-Persistence在所评估的记忆设计中取得更高的整体留出成功率，并具有最低的平均LLM调用成本。

Conclusion: 基于记忆的自我改进应将经验积累与抽象持久化分离；按需重建并丢弃指导可缓解抽象错误的长期再现，同时保持较高性能与较低调用成本。

中文摘要：LLM智能体无需更新模型参数即可跨任务提升性能：它们将自身执行过程中获得的经验存入外部记忆，并复用这些经验来指导后续决策。一个核心设计问题是：应当仅保留观察到的轨迹与结果，还是也保留由这些轨迹和结果推断出的规则、摘要和技能。保留这些抽象内容使智能体能够复用策略，而无需反复从原始轨迹中推导策略。然而，存储的抽象内容中的错误可能通过检索与复用来传播到后续决策。这些错误还可能塑造执行轨迹，而这些轨迹随后又成为新抽象内容的基础，从而复制最初的错误。我们将这种反馈机制称为错误再现（error reproduction）：错误通过抽象、执行和再抽象的循环被重新生成，使其持久性超出对原始错误知识的直接复用。我们提出Anti-Persistence，一种记忆设计，它累积轨迹与结果，同时按需重建任务特定指导。一个自适应选择器利用先前结果来决定是否以及如何合成指导，该指导在执行后即被丢弃。这防止其作为存储对象被直接复用，尽管通过保留轨迹产生的间接反馈仍然可能存在。在不同基准和主干模型上，Anti-Persistence在所评估的记忆设计中实现了更高的整体留出成功率以及最低的平均LLM调用成本。这些结果支持在基于记忆的自我改进中，将经验积累与抽象持久化分离。

Original abstract: LLM agents can improve across tasks without updating model parameters by storing experience from their own executions in external memory and reusing it to guide later decisions. A central design question is whether to retain only observed trajectories and outcomes or also the rules, summaries, and skills inferred from them. Retaining these abstractions allows agents to reuse strategies without repeatedly deriving them from raw trajectories. However, errors in stored abstractions can propagate to subsequent decisions through retrieval and reuse. They can also shape execution trajectories, which then become the basis for new abstractions that reproduce the original errors. We term this feedback mechanism error reproduction: errors are regenerated through a cycle of abstraction, execution, and re-abstraction, extending their persistence beyond direct reuse of the original faulty knowledge. We propose Anti-Persistence, a memory design that accumulates trajectories and outcomes while reconstructing task-specific guidance on demand. An adaptive selector uses prior outcomes to decide whether and how to synthesize guidance, which is discarded after execution. This prevents its direct reuse as a stored object, although indirect feedback through retained trajectories remains possible. Across different benchmarks and backbones, Anti-Persistence achieves higher overall held-out success and the lowest average LLM-call cost among the evaluated memory designs. These results support separating experience accumulation from abstraction persistence in memorybased self-improvement.

</details>


### [44] [KPHarness: Co-Evolving Knowledge and Probing for Long-Horizon Gaming Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27417)
*Zhikang Chen,Yueqing Sun,Sen Cui,Ziang Ye,Yu Wang,Yan Feng2,Xierui Song,Yu Zhang,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 面向长程游戏agent提出知识-探测协同演化的执行harness自适应框架，其失败诊断与受控harness修订思路可迁移到coding agent。

TL;DR: KPHarness通过恢复知识与主动探测协同演化，为冻结VLA模型自适应外部执行外壳，并在Minecraft任务及未见任务上提升覆盖与完成度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失败轨迹只能说明需要干预，不能说明执行外壳应如何改；需诊断证据而非仅提出新恢复规则。

Method: 构建KPHarness：恢复知识生成干预假设指导主动探测，探测结果反哺知识及探测选择；快执行与慢研究循环分离，沙箱配对评估接受有界修订，验证恢复跨任务复用，即时/延迟反馈更新策略。

Result: Minecraft采矿、战斗、制作任务覆盖率优于裸策略、固定手工外壳、仅知识非自适应探测演化；未见任务在固定演化外壳、知识库和探测策略下完成度提升。

Conclusion: 证据驱动的知识-探测协同演化可有效改进执行外壳及其失败调查方式，并促进跨任务复用与泛化。

中文摘要：一条失败的游戏轨迹表明智能体需要干预，但并不必然表明其执行外壳应如何改变。因此，有效的执行外壳自适应需要收集诊断性证据，而不是仅仅提出替代性恢复规则。我们提出 KPHarness，一个通过知识-探测协同演化来适配冻结视觉-语言-动作模型外部执行外壳的框架。恢复知识提供上下文特定的干预假设以指导主动探测；探测结果揭示这些假设何时适用，并为同时改进恢复知识和后续探测选择提供证据。这种互惠过程使执行外壳不仅能够改进其应用哪些干预，还能改进其如何调查失败。KPHarness 将快速策略执行与慢速研究循环分离，后者将积累的知识和探测证据转化为有界的执行外壳修订。每个修订都在匹配的沙箱条件下与现行版本比较，只有当配对评估显示改进时才被接受。经进一步验证支持的恢复被保留以供跨任务复用，而即时交互反馈和延迟的回合成功信用在自适应过程中更新动作策略和知识探测策略。在涵盖采矿、战斗和制作的 Minecraft 任务上，实验显示其任务覆盖率优于裸策略执行、人工指定的固定执行外壳以及采用非自适应探测的仅知识执行外壳演化。在先前未见任务上的评估进一步显示，在演化后的执行外壳、知识库和探测策略保持固定时，任务完成度得到提高。

Original abstract: A failed gaming trajectory reveals that an agent needs intervention, but not necessarily how its execution harness should change. Effective harness adaptation therefore requires gathering diagnostic evidence, rather than merely proposing alternative recovery rules. We present KPHarness, a framework that adapts the external execution harness of a frozen vision-language-action model through knowledge-probing co-evolution. Recovery knowledge supplies context-specific intervention hypotheses that guide active probing; probe outcomes reveal when these hypotheses apply and provide evidence for refining both recovery knowledge and later probe selection. This reciprocal process allows the harness to improve not only which interventions it applies, but also how it investigates failures. KPHarness separates fast policy execution from a slow research loop that translates accumulated knowledge and probing evidence into bounded harness revisions. Each revision is compared with the incumbent under matched sandbox conditions and accepted only when the paired evaluation shows improvement. Recoveries supported by further validation are retained for cross-task reuse, while immediate interaction feedback and delayed episode-success credit update action- and knowledge-probing policies during adaptation. Experiments on Minecraft tasks spanning mining, combat, and crafting show improved task coverage over bare policy execution, a manually specified fixed harness, and knowledge-only harness evolution with non-adaptive probing. Evaluation on previously unseen tasks further shows improved task completion with the evolved harness, knowledge bank, and probing policies held fixed,

</details>


### [45] [Harness Self-Evolution for Long-Horizon Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27435)
*Ruhan Wang,Yucheng Shi,Zongxia Li,Junyao Yang,Zhongzhi Li,Yue Yu,Haitao Mi,Dongruo Zhou,Leoweiliang*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 38 pages, 7 figures, 7 tables, including appendices

Selection reason: 直接研究长程终端agent的harness自演化与可靠性提升，利用成功/失败轨迹和运行时监控迭代改进执行框架，与自动代码优化agent高度相关。

TL;DR: RiseHarness在固定模型权重下，利用同一任务的成败运行与运行时harness轨迹迭代改进终端智能体harness，提升跨任务可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程终端智能体单次可能成功但重复运行不稳定，随机错误会沿动作与上下文传播，导致能力与可靠执行之间存在差距。

Method: 聚焦同一配置下成败并存的任务，以成功运行为参考；结合智能体轨迹和运行时监控器的内部harness轨迹，借助Harness Handbook关联行为与源码，检索其他框架实现并适配为设计参考；候选改动经行为检查和重复评估，有收益版本进入下一轮。

Result: 在多个模型上评估任务成功率和可靠性，消融harness执行证据与外部研究，并测试同一模型向未见基准的迁移；摘要未给出具体数值结果。

Conclusion: 将特定模型作为harness适配单元，通过改进共享交互机制，可把不同框架的合适机制整合到单一harness中并使现有能力在跨任务时更可靠。

中文摘要：长时程终端智能体可能在一次运行中解决任务，但在重复运行中失败。随机错误会通过动作和上下文传播，造成能力与可靠执行之间的差距。我们提出RiseHarness，一个在模型权重固定条件下迭代改进智能体harness的框架。它聚焦于翻转在同一配置下既有成功运行又有失败运行的任务，并将成功运行作为失败分析的参考。RiseHarness将智能体轨迹与运行时监控器产生的内部harness轨迹相结合，以考察模型动作如何与命令执行、反馈、上下文管理和完成决策交互。Harness Handbook将这些证据与harness行为和源代码关联起来，帮助识别跨任务共享机制中的重复问题。随后，RiseHarness在其他harness框架中搜索相关实现，并将其适配为面向当前模型和执行协议的设计参考。候选改动经过行为检查和重复任务评估；具有可测收益的版本作为下一轮迭代的种子。该循环将不同框架中的合适机制整合到单一harness中，并利用新的执行证据指导进一步改动。我们在多个模型上评估任务成功率和可靠性，消融harness执行证据与外部研究的作用，并在同一模型上测试向未见基准的迁移。RiseHarness将特定模型作为harness适配单元，研究对共享交互机制的改动如何使现有能力在跨任务时更可靠。

Original abstract: Long-horizon terminal agents may solve a task in one run yet fail across repeated runs. Stochastic errors can propagate through actions and context, creating a gap between capability and reliable execution. We present RiseHarness, a framework for iteratively improving agent harnesses with model weights fixed. It focuses on flipping tasks with both successful and failed runs under the same configuration, using successful runs as references for failure analysis. RiseHarness combines agent trajectories with internal harness traces from a runtime monitor to examine how model actions interact with command execution, feedback, context management, and completion decisions. A Harness Handbook links this evidence to harness behaviors and source code, helping identify recurring problems in mechanisms shared across tasks. RiseHarness then searches other harness frameworks for relevant implementations and adapts them as design references to the current model and execution protocol. Candidate changes undergo behavioral checks and repeated task evaluation; versions with measured gains seed the next iteration. This loop integrates suitable mechanisms from different frameworks into a single harness and uses new execution evidence to guide further changes. We evaluate task success and reliability across models, ablate harness execution evidence and external research, and test transfer to unseen benchmarks with the same model. RiseHarness treats a specific model as the unit of harness adaptation, studying how changes to shared interaction mechanisms can make existing capabilities more reliable across tasks.

</details>


### [46] [Runtime Control for AI Agents: Closing the Evaluation--Execution Gap in Operational Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27480)
*Jayesh Bhadane,Ramachandra Yerramsetti,Rajesh Gupta,Sumit Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 8 pages, 1 figure, 8 tables

Selection reason: 针对agent工作流中的审批失效问题提出状态绑定的运行时保证层，涉及执行前重校验与安全控制，可迁移到agent可靠性设计。

TL;DR: 提出状态绑定的运行时保障层，在审批依据变化后使执行许可失效，并在派发时复核；42案例重放中识别15个后续失效审批、派发前暂扣14个动作并补偿1个已落地效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审批签发时的有效性可能因后续事实、证据、策略或权限变化而失效，导致评估与执行之间出现差距。

Method: 设计状态绑定的运行时保障层，分离概率性测量与确定性裁决，将执行许可绑定到审批的决策相关依据，记录变更后使许可失效，并在派发时重新检查可容许性；给出八种失效模式、保障模型、参考架构及前瞻干预与回溯补救。

Result: 在脚本化42案例重放中，状态绑定配置识别出15个后来失效的审批，而刻意不重新评估的时间点比较器不会重新审视；该系统在派发前暂扣14个受影响动作，并补偿1个已落地效应。结果仅反映规定策略与事件顺序下的行为。

Conclusion: 在显式拦截、摄取、依赖和序列化假设下，该模型可在注册执行面内确立派发时有效性；结果不表示优于派发时强制执行设计或生产有效性。

中文摘要：语言模型智能体越来越多地参与运营工作流，在这些工作流中，审批、绑定和支付会形成承诺。签发时有效的审批，在相关事实、证据、策略或权限发生变化后，可能不再授权执行。我们将此称为评估—执行差距。我们规定了一种状态绑定的运行时保障层：它将概率性测量与确定性裁决分离，将执行许可绑定到审批的决策相关依据，在相关的已记录变更发生后使该许可失效，并在派发时重新检查可容许性。这将先前审批的持续有效性与当前状态策略检查区分开来。我们描述了八种失效模式、一个保障模型、一个参考架构，以及带有回溯性补救的前瞻性干预。在一个脚本化的42案例重放中，状态绑定配置识别出15个后来失效的审批，而一个刻意不重新评估的时间点比较器不会重新审视这些审批；它在派发前暂扣了14个受影响动作，并补偿了一个已落地效应。这些结果说明的是在规定策略和事件顺序下的行为，而不是优于派发时强制执行设计或生产有效性。在显式的拦截、摄取、依赖和序列化假设下，该模型在注册的执行面内确立了派发时有效性。

Original abstract: Language-model agents increasingly participate in operational workflows where approvals, binds, and payments create commitments. An approval valid when issued may no longer authorize execution after relevant facts, evidence, policy, or authority change. We call this the evaluation--execution gap. We specify a state-bound runtime assurance layer that separates probabilistic measurement from deterministic adjudication, binds an execution allowance to the decision-relevant basis of an approval, invalidates that allowance after relevant logged changes, and rechecks admissibility at dispatch. This distinguishes the continued validity of a prior approval from a current-state policy check. We describe eight failure modes, an assurance model, a reference architecture, and prospective interventions with retrospective remediation. In a scripted 42-case replay, the state-bound configuration identifies 15 later-invalidated approvals that a deliberately non-re-evaluating point-in-time comparator does not revisit; it holds 14 affected actions before dispatch and compensates one landed effect. These results illustrate behavior under the stipulated policies and event orders, not superiority over dispatch-time enforcement designs or production effectiveness. Under explicit interception, ingestion, dependency, and serialization assumptions, the model establishes dispatch-time validity within the registered execution surface.

</details>


### [47] [The More the Better? A Comparison of Single- and Multi-Agent LLM Setups on a Financial Review Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27488)
*Pei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 受控比较单agent与多agent在文档审查任务上的效果与成本，提供多agent分工收益有限的实证结论，对agent系统设计有参考价值。

TL;DR: 在信用备忘录审查任务中，多智能体分工能多捕获部分缺陷但增加误报，平衡准确率未提升且token消耗更高；结果依赖指令，重复采样单智能体可优于团队。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验实践指南中多智能体分工提升大语言模型文档审查效果的说法。

Method: 在300份合成信用评估备忘录和四个模型上，受控比较单智能体与三智能体团队，分别测量漏报、误报、平衡准确率和token消耗，并采用Bonferroni校正检验主要预测。

Result: 团队多捕获0.08到0.10的带有解释的缺陷，但在全部四个模型上增加误报；平衡准确率变化不超过0.02，token消耗为2.2到2.4倍；展示未分配要求可降低部分Claude模型的误报；单智能体三次采样在部分模型上平衡准确率更高。

Conclusion: 狭义多智能体分工并非普遍更优，效果依赖指令；重复采样单智能体是可考虑的替代方案。

中文摘要：从业者指南认为，当工作被分配给多个智能体时，大语言模型能更好地审阅文档。我们在一项金融任务上，通过单智能体与多智能体设置的受控比较，检验其一种狭义形式：依据要求审阅信用评估备忘录。一个智能体在一次调用中检查六项要求。同一模型上的三人团队检查同一份备忘录，每人两项要求，使用相同措辞，不设角色或后续阶段。300份合成备忘录中每项要求的标签均已知，因此漏报和误报分别测量。在四个模型上以Bonferroni校正水平检验两个主要预测。在要求对每项要求只判定一次的指令下，团队在单智能体会漏掉一些缺陷的三个模型上，多捕获0.08到0.10的带有解释的缺陷，其中在两个模型上达到校正水平。它在全部四个模型上增加更多误报，其中在三个模型上达到校正水平。作为探索性指标的平衡准确率变化不超过0.02，团队使用2.2到2.4倍的token。向每个智能体展示其未被分配的要求，可降低两个Claude模型上增加的误报，其中在一个模型上达到校正水平。在探索性比较中，对单智能体采样三次并在任一次抽样中标记，在这些模型上比团队给出更高的平衡准确率，且团队的结果取决于指令。

Original abstract: Practitioner guidance holds that a large language model reviews a document better when the work is divided among agents. We test a narrow form of it in a controlled comparison of single-agent and multi-agent setups on a financial task, reviewing credit assessment memos against requirements. One agent checks six requirements in one call. A team of three on the same model checks the same memo, two requirements each, in the same words, with no roles or later stage. Labels are known for every requirement on 300 synthetic memos, so misses and false alarms are measured separately. Two primary predictions are tested on four models at a Bonferroni-corrected level. Under an instruction to decide each requirement once, the team catches 0.08 to 0.10 more of the defects that carry an explanation on the three models whose single agent misses some, at the corrected level on two. It raises more false alarms on all four, at the corrected level on three. Balanced accuracy, an exploratory measure, changes by 0.02 or less, and the team uses 2.2 to 2.4 times the tokens. Showing each agent the requirements it was not given reduces the added false alarms on the two Claude models, at the corrected level on one. In exploratory comparisons, sampling the single agent three times and flagging on any draw gives higher balanced accuracy than the team on those models, and the team's results depend on the instruction.

</details>


### [48] [MemDev-Bench: Does Memory Help Code Agents?Benchmarking Memory Systems over Real Repository Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27524)
*Guorui Chen,Tao Liu,Zhenzhen Huang,Yidan Zhang,Liyu Jiang,Shuoyuan Wang,Hanchen Sun,Xu He,Dong Li,Yihan Hu,Jianye Hao,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出基于真实仓库历史的代码agent记忆基准，系统分析记忆对定位、编辑及失败模式的影响，与代码agent经验复用直接相关。

TL;DR: 提出 MemDev-Bench，用相关性受控的仓库历史评估记忆系统对代码智能体的影响，发现记忆提升定位与编辑，但未重评适用性会导致失败，且原始轨迹比压缩记忆更省步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏时间有效历史或对先前任务相关性的控制，难以可靠评估记忆系统如何组织和影响代码智能体的经验复用。

Method: 构建 MemDev-Bench，将任务与同仓库、带相关性标注的先前任务配对，并建立自动化流水线使历史任务可执行可验证，生成 4,869 个历史任务对应 555 个基础任务，系统评估多种记忆系统。

Result: 记忆主要提升细粒度定位、编辑和功能正确性；即使历史相关也可能因未重新评估记忆适用性导致失败，不相关历史更易增加失败；直接提供原始历史轨迹比使用记忆系统显著减少执行步数，因可复用压缩记忆常省略的过程信息。

Conclusion: 未来记忆系统应保留过程信息，并依据当前任务上下文重新评估记忆的适用性。

中文摘要：代码智能体越来越多地在不断演化的真实代码仓库上工作，记忆系统旨在帮助它们复用先前经验。然而，这类经验多样且不断演化，因此记忆系统如何组织经验并进而影响代码智能体仍不清楚。现有基准无法可靠回答这一问题，因为它们要么缺乏时间上有效的历史，要么缺乏对先前任务相关性的控制。本文提出 MemDev-Bench，用于评估记忆系统如何在多样的仓库历史中影响代码智能体。它将每个任务与来自同一仓库、带有相关性标注的先前任务配对，以构建相关性受控的历史。我们还构建了一条自动化流水线，能够可靠地使这些先前任务可执行、可验证，为 555 个基础任务生成了 4,869 个历史任务。基于 MemDev-Bench，我们在多样开发场景下对记忆系统进行系统评估，得到三个主要发现。(1) 记忆主要改善细粒度定位与编辑，从而提升功能正确性。(2) 即使存在相关历史，记忆也会导致原本可解决的任务失败，而不相关历史往往会增加此类失败。这些失败主要源于智能体在复用记忆时没有重新评估其适用性。(3) 与使用记忆系统相比，直接向智能体提供原始历史轨迹显著减少其执行步数。进一步分析表明，智能体通过复用压缩记忆往往省略的过程信息来节省这些步数。这些结果表明，未来记忆系统应保留过程信息，并针对当前任务上下文重新评估记忆适用性。

Original abstract: Code agents increasingly work on evolving real repositories, and memory systems aim to help them reuse earlier experience. However, such experience is diverse and evolving, so it remains unclear how memory systems organize it and thereby affect code agents. Existing benchmarks cannot answer this reliably because they lack either temporally valid histories or control over prior-task relevance. In this paper, we present MemDev-Bench to evaluate how memory systems affect code agents over diverse repository histories. It pairs each task with relevance-annotated earlier tasks from the same repository to build histories with controlled relevance. We also build an automated pipeline that reliably makes these earlier tasks executable and verifiable, producing 4,869 historical tasks for 555 base tasks. Based on MemDev-Bench, our systematic evaluation of memory systems across diverse development scenarios yields three main findings. (1) Memory mainly improves fine-grained localization and editing, increasing functional correctness. (2) Memory also causes failures on otherwise solvable tasks even with related history, and unrelated history tends to increase such failures. These failures mainly stem from agents reusing memory without reassessing its applicability. (3) Compared with using memory systems, directly providing agents with raw historical trajectories substantially reduces their steps. Further analysis suggests that agents save these steps by reusing process information that compressed memories tend to omit. These results suggest that future memory systems should preserve process information and reassess memory applicability against current task context.

</details>


### [49] [Automated Multi-Agent Organization Design: From Architecture Search to Organizational Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27531)
*Shao-Jun Xia,Yuner Zhang,Huixin Zhang,Di Wang,Yifan Jiang,Liangxi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: First draft: 4 pages, 3 figures

Selection reason: 把多agent组织作为一等设计对象，自动搜索职责分配与协调规则，对多agent架构设计和自动优化有明确迁移价值。

TL;DR: 提出AMOD，将多智能体组织作为一等设计对象，通过执行驱动循环自动分配与修订功能责任和协调规则，并用移除、通用角色替换和改派等测试验证责任必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动多智能体设计多关注架构是否有效，尚未回答自动生成结构何时具有功能上有意义的责任划分；需要识别必要责任、其分配方式及变更时机。

Method: 将AMOD形式化为执行驱动设计循环：自动化设计器为任务实例化组织，分配功能责任与协调规则；执行后分析任务结果、中间产物和交互轨迹，识别缺失、重复或无效责任并修订组织；在匹配资源预算下通过移除责任、替换为通用角色或改变分配进行互补评估。

Result: 摘要为框架性工作，未报告具体定量实验结果；提出了AMOD及基于责任移除、替换和改派的互补评估方案。

Conclusion: AMOD为基于证据且自适应的多智能体组织设计提供了原则性基础。

中文摘要：智能体系统设计正日益从手工编写的提示词和工作流转向对智能体程序和交互结构的自动化搜索，多智能体系统受到越来越多关注。然而，一个核心问题仍未解决：自动生成的多智能体结构何时展现出功能上有意义的责任划分？我们认为，自动化多智能体设计应超越寻找有效架构，转向识别哪些责任在功能上是必要的、应如何分配这些责任，以及何时应改变它们。为探索这一问题，我们提出自动化多智能体组织设计（AMOD），它将组织本身视为一等设计对象，显式建模智能体团队如何组成、智能体承担哪些功能责任、它们如何协调以及集体决策如何作出。我们将 AMOD 形式化为一个执行驱动的设计循环。对于每个任务，自动化设计器通过为一组智能体分配功能责任和协调规则来实例化一个组织。执行之后，检查任务结果、中间产物和交互轨迹，以识别哪些责任可能缺失、重复或无效，并据此修订组织。我们还提出在匹配资源预算下，通过移除所发现的责任、用通用角色替换它们或改变其分配来测试这些责任，并辅以互补评估。AMOD 为基于证据的自适应多智能体组织设计提供了原则性基础。

Original abstract: Agentic system design is increasingly shifting from hand-crafted prompts and workflows toward automated search over agentic programs and interaction structures, with multi-agent systems receiving growing attention. Yet a central question remains unresolved: when does an automatically generated multi-agent structure exhibit a functionally meaningful division of responsibility? We argue that automated multi-agent design should move beyond searching for effective architectures toward identifying which responsibilities are functionally necessary, how they should be allocated, and when they should change. To explore this, we propose Automated Multi-Agent Organization Design (AMOD), which treats organization itself as a first-class design object, explicitly modeling how agent teams are composed, what functional responsibilities the agents assume, how they coordinate, and how collective decisions are made. We formulate AMOD as an execution-driven design loop. For each task, an automated designer instantiates an organization by assigning functional responsibilities and coordination rules to a set of agents. After execution, task outcomes, intermediate artifacts, and interaction traces are examined to identify which responsibilities may be missing, duplicated, or ineffective, and the organization is revised accordingly. We also propose testing discovered responsibilities by removing them, replacing them with generic roles, or changing their assignment under matched resource budgets, with complementary evaluations. AMOD provides a principled foundation for evidence-based and adaptive multi-agent organization design.

</details>


### [50] [Act on Your Behalf, Beyond Your Authorization: Benchmarking LLM Agent Authorization Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27548)
*Zhiping Zhang,Franklin Mingzhe Li,Zihao Huang,Tianshi Li*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出LLM agent授权对齐基准，揭示任务完成不等于权限合规，并分析推理强度与询问用户的影响，对agent安全控制有参考意义。

TL;DR: 作者提出 AuthorizationBench，用 286 个真实案例衍生任务和 6,864 条轨迹评估 LLM 智能体是否遵守用户授权，发现越权行为普遍存在，且任务完成、增加推理或后果导向安全评估都不足以保证授权对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估主要看任务完成或有害后果，容易漏掉未造成传统安全/隐私危害但违反用户委托授权的行为，因此需要专门衡量授权对齐。

Method: 基于真实案例构建 286 个可执行任务，覆盖情境触发、直接有害后果有无及十个应用领域；在授权感知沙箱中用四个模型、两种推理努力设置、三种智能体框架执行，得到 6,864 条轨迹。

Result: 19.2% 轨迹出现越权，24 种配置为 10.5%–26.6%；提高推理努力总体几乎不变；有/无直接有害后果越权率相似（17.9% vs 20.8%）；良性压力高于恶意注入（23.5% vs 17.6%，p=0.002）；先咨询委托人者越权率更低（14.0% vs 21.6%，p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 任务能力、额外推理和后果导向安全评估本身不能确保授权对齐；智能体自主性与保留用户决策权存在张力，需要进一步以人为中心研究。

中文摘要：当大语言模型智能体代表用户行动时，预期其应在用户授予的授权范围内行动。现有智能体评估主要衡量任务完成情况或有害后果，因此可能忽略那些违反这种委托授权、但未产生传统安全或隐私危害的行为。我们提出 AuthorizationBench，一个基于有记录的真实世界案例、面向授权对齐的基准。一个可扩展的构建流程生成了 286 个可执行任务，涵盖情境触发因素、是否存在直接有害后果以及十个应用领域。我们在一个授权感知沙箱中，使用四个模型、两种推理努力程度设置和三种智能体执行框架执行每个任务，该沙箱保持授权路径与超出授权路径均可执行且可观测，共产生 6,864 条轨迹。智能体在 19.2% 的轨迹中超出授权行动，在 24 种配置中该比例从 10.5% 到 26.6% 不等。任务完成并不意味着授权合规。提高推理努力程度使总体比例几乎不变，且影响因模型而异。在受控配对中，智能体在有直接有害后果（17.9%）和无直接有害后果（20.8%）时以相似比例超出授权行动，并且在良性压力下比在恶意注入下更常如此（23.5% 对 17.6%，p=0.002）。我们的结果表明，任务能力、额外推理以及面向后果的安全评估本身并不能确保授权对齐。此外，在完成任务前咨询委托人的智能体表现出更低的超出授权行为比例（14.0% 对 21.6%，p<0.001），这指向智能体自主性与保留用户决策权之间的张力，需要进一步以人为中心的研究。数据集和代码见 https://github.com/PEACH-Research-Lab/AuthorizationBench 。

Original abstract: When an LLM agent acts on behalf of a user, it is expected to act within the authorization scope the user grants. Existing agent evaluations largely measure task completion or harmful consequences and can therefore overlook actions that violate this delegation without producing conventional safety or privacy harms. We introduce AuthorizationBench, a benchmark of authorization alignment grounded in documented real-world cases. A scalable construction pipeline produces 286 executable tasks spanning situational triggers, the presence or absence of direct harmful consequences, and ten application domains. We execute every task with four models at two reasoning-effort settings across three agent harnesses in an authorization-aware sandbox that keeps authorized and out-of-authorization paths executable and observable, yielding 6,864 trajectories. Agents act out of authorization in 19.2% of trajectories, with rates ranging from 10.5% to 26.6% across the 24 configurations. Task completion does not imply authorization. Increasing reasoning effort leaves the aggregate rate nearly unchanged, with effects varying across models. On controlled pairs, agents act out of authorization at similar rates with (17.9%) and without (20.8%) direct harmful consequences, and more often under benign pressures than malicious injections (23.5% versus 17.6%, p=0.002). Our results show that task capability, additional reasoning, and consequence-oriented safety evaluation do not by themselves ensure authorization alignment. Moreover, agents that consult the principal before completing a task exhibit lower rates of out-of-authorization behavior (14.0% versus 21.6%, p<0.001), pointing to a tension between agent autonomy and preserving users' decision authority that calls for further human-centered study. Dataset and code are available at https://github.com/PEACH-Research-Lab/AuthorizationBench .

</details>


### [51] [The Show-Up Attack: When Subagents Win Over Their Supervisor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27574)
*Maosen Zhang,Jianshuo Dong,Yutong Zhang,Jie Zhang,Tianwei Zhang,Han Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: Preprint

Selection reason: 揭示多agent系统中子agent越权操纵监督者的攻击面，并提出评测基准，对多agent权限边界与安全机制有启发。

TL;DR: 论文提出“现身攻击”：恶意子代理诱导监督者将其纳入职责范围外任务；SUA-Arena 含 6 种策略、188 个用例，6 个前沿 LLM 均受影响，GPT-5.6-Sol 监督时 ASR 达 0.93，现有缓解措施不稳定，需显式权限边界机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协调提升能力的同时引入新的攻击面，恶意子代理可能利用协调机制越权参与任务，需要系统评估该风险。

Method: 提出并研究 show-up attack，构建 SUA-Arena 基准，包含 6 种攻击策略和 188 个测试用例，并评估 6 个前沿 LLM 作为监督者时的脆弱性及提示、受限通信等缓解策略。

Result: 6 个前沿 LLM 均易受 show-up attack 影响；例如 GPT-5.6-Sol 作为监督者时 ASR 最高达 0.93；提示与受限通信等缓解策略无法一致降低风险。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统需要显式机制来强制执行权限边界，以防范恶意子代理越权参与任务。

中文摘要：多智能体系统正越来越多地被部署用于协调多个大语言模型（LLM）智能体。然而，这也创造了新的攻击面：恶意智能体可能有机会利用这种协调。本文提出并研究了一种“show-up attack（现身攻击）”，其中恶意子代理操纵监督者，使其将自身卷入超出既定范围的任务。为评估这一风险，我们提出了 SUA-Arena，它实现了 6 种攻击策略并包含 188 个测试用例。随后，我们评估了 6 个前沿 LLM，发现它们均易受现身攻击影响。例如，GPT-5.6-Sol 作为监督者时，攻击成功率（ASR）高达 0.93。提示和受限通信等缓解策略未能持续降低风险。这些结果表明，多智能体系统需要用于强制执行权限边界的显式机制。

Original abstract: Multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed to coordinate multiple large language model (LLM) agents. However, this also creates new attack surfaces: malicious agents may have chances to exploit the coordination. In this paper, we propose and study the \textit{show-up attack}, in which a malicious subagent manipulates the supervisor into involving it in tasks beyond its assigned scope. To evaluate this risk, we propose SUA-Arena, which implements 6 attack strategies and contains 188 test cases. Then, we evaluate 6 frontier LLMs and find that all of them are vulnerable to show-up attacks. For example, GPT-5.6-Sol as a supervisor suffers an ASR of up to 0.93. Mitigation strategies such as prompting and restricted communication fail to reduce the risk consistently. These results show that multi-agent systems need explicit mechanisms for enforcing authority boundaries.

</details>


### [52] [DuplexLedger: Benchmarking Transactional Memory in Full-Duplex Speech Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27581)
*Yuxuan Li,Xiangchen Guan,Kunrong Li,Jin Wang,Kaiwen Luo,Yang Xiao,Yufei Guo,Zhisheng Chen,Liang Lin,Kun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 提出全双工语音agent的事务性记忆基准与选择性写入/更新机制，其可审计记忆管理思路对agent记忆设计有一定迁移价值。

TL;DR: 论文提出双语全双工记忆基准 DuplexLedger 和轻量侧车方案 ACT-Mem，揭示交互-记忆差距，并在留出会话上提升最新值记忆准确率、减少过时与干扰信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将全双工交互时序与持久记忆结果分离，无法判断哪些事件应写入、更新、忽略或保留，也难以解释正确答案对应的记忆状态转移。

Method: 构建 DuplexLedger：1,094 个双语持久会话、四类全双工场景、事件级 Write/Update/Ignore/Retain 监督、显式前后记忆状态及 6,564 个同会话问题与检查；评估五个全双工模型。提出 ACT-Mem：8.92M 参数侧车，含事件控制器、版本化事务账本、类型化检索器，耦合冻结 MiniCPM-o 4.5。

Result: 五个模型中无系统兼具强边界控制与连贯最终状态记忆，最佳最终状态事实覆盖率仅 2.01%。在 219 个留出会话上，ACT-Mem 将最新值准确率从 4.11% 提升至 8.68%，减少过时断言和干扰项采纳；改善交互边界但减慢打断停止。

Conclusion: 应面向全双工语音智能体构建选择性、可审计的持久记忆机制，而非不加区分地保留完整转录文本。

中文摘要：全双工语音智能体必须在边听边说时更新持久记忆，但哪些事件应被存储、修订或排除仍不清楚。现有基准将交互时序与记忆结果隔离开来，掩盖了正确答案背后的状态转移。我们提出 DuplexLedger，一个双语基准，包含 1,094 个持久会话，将四种全双工场景与事件级 Write/Update/Ignore/Retain 监督、显式的前/后记忆状态以及 6,564 个同会话问题与检查对齐。评估五个全双工模型揭示出交互-记忆差距：没有系统能同时结合强边界控制与连贯的最终状态记忆，最佳最终状态事实覆盖率为 2.01%。我们提出 ACT-Mem，一个 8.92M 参数的侧车系统，由事件控制器、版本化事务账本和类型化检索器组成，并与冻结的 MiniCPM-o 4.5 主干耦合。在 219 个留出会话上，ACT-Mem 将最新值准确率从 4.11% 提升到 8.68%，同时减少过时断言和干扰项采纳。它改善了交互边界，但减慢了打断停止。这些结果支持选择性、可审计的全双工记忆，而非不加区分地保留转录文本。代码见 https://github.com/lyx-researcher/DuplexLedger。

Original abstract: Full-duplex speech agents must update persistent memory while listening and speaking, yet it remains unclear which events should be stored, revised, or excluded. Existing benchmarks isolate interaction timing from memory outcomes, obscuring the state transitions behind a correct answer. We introduce DuplexLedger, a bilingual benchmark of 1,094 persistent sessions aligning four full-duplex scenarios with event-level Write/Update/Ignore/Retain supervision, explicit pre/post memory states, and 6,564 same-session questions and checks. Evaluating five full-duplex models reveals an interaction-memory gap: no system combines strong boundary control with coherent final-state memory, and the best final-state fact coverage is 2.01%. We propose ACT-Mem, an 8.92M-parameter sidecar comprising an event controller, a versioned transactional ledger, and a typed retriever coupled with a frozen MiniCPM-o 4.5 backbone. On 219 held-out sessions, ACT-Mem increases latest-value accuracy from 4.11% to 8.68% while reducing stale assertions and distractor adoption. It improves interaction boundaries but slows interruption stopping. These results motivate selective, auditable full-duplex memory rather than indiscriminate transcript retention. Code is available at https://github.com/lyx-researcher/DuplexLedger.

</details>


### [53] [Beyond Experience: Knowledge-Guided Evolution for NPU Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27588)
*Xi Chen,Ming Shen,Haotian Yan,Ruoyu Sun,Tianjun Yao*

Main category: cs.AI



Selection reason: 直接研究LLM agent自动生成与优化NPU kernel，结合外部知识库、进化搜索与修复反馈，属于自动代码优化agent的核心相关方向。

TL;DR: 提出知识库驱动的演化式内核搜索方法 ASCENDEVOLVE，通过检索平台文档、头文件与算子仓库中的任务特定证据，弥补 NPU 智能体仅依赖自身经验的不足，并在 CANN-Bench 的 53 个 Ascend C 算子上取得 71.39 总分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向昇腾 NPU 的智能体主要依赖自身生成与优化经验，只能复用已尝试内容，难以应对需要未知机制的新算子以及优化过程中出现的新情形；作者主张仅靠经验不足以支撑内核生成，需要外部知识。

Method: ASCENDEVOLVE 从平台文档、工具包头文件和算子仓库构建大规模外部知识库并建立索引；在演化式内核搜索的每一步，为智能体检索小型任务特定证据切片，用于启发多样实现策略、指导失败候选修复以及约束强候选重组。

Result: 在 CANN-Bench 的 53 个 Ascend C 算子（覆盖四个难度级别）上，总体得分为 71.39，较 Reflexion 与 OpenEvolve 分别提升 11.6% 和 17.2%。

Conclusion: 将演化式内核搜索建立在可检索的外部知识之上，可有效弥补经验记忆的覆盖局限，提升新兴加速器上的内核生成与优化表现。

中文摘要：LLM 智能体已成为主流 GPU 软件栈上能力出色的内核工程师，但在昇腾 NPU 等新兴加速器上仍表现吃力，因为其编程模型在预训练数据中几乎未被体现。近期 NPU 智能体主要通过经验来弥合这一差距：它们从自身生成与优化试验中积累记忆，并在后续任务中复用。我们认为，仅靠经验不足以完成内核生成。智能体只能记住自己尝试过什么，而很少尝试它不知道的机制。因此，当新算子需要不同机制时，这类记忆帮助甚微；即使对于熟悉的算子，随着优化推进也会出现未见过的情形。我们提出 ASCENDEVOLVE，它将演化式内核搜索的每一步都建立在由平台文档、工具包头文件与算子仓库构建的大规模外部知识库之上。由于该语料库远超任何上下文窗口，ASCENDEVOLVE 对其建立索引，并将每个智能体路由到一小段任务特定的证据切片，而不是语料库本身。检索到的知识为多样化实现策略提供种子，在候选失败时指导修复，并约束强候选的重组方式。在来自 CANN-Bench、覆盖四个难度级别的 53 个 Ascend C 算子上，ASCENDEVOLVE 取得 71.39 的总分，相较 Reflexion 和 OpenEvolve 分别提升 11.6% 和 17.2%。

Original abstract: LLM agents have become capable kernel engineers on mainstream GPU stacks, but they struggle on emerging accelerators such as Ascend NPUs, whose programming model is barely represented in pretraining data. Recent NPU agents address this gap largely through experience: they accumulate memory from their own generation and optimization trials and reuse it on later tasks. We argue that experience alone is not enough for kernel generation. An agent can only remember what it has tried, and it rarely tries a mechanism it does not know. As a result, such memory offers little help when a new operator calls for a different mechanism, and even familiar operators raise unseen situations as optimization proceeds. We present ASCENDEVOLVE, which grounds every step of an evolutionary kernel search in a large external knowledge base built from platform documentation, toolkit headers, and operator repositories. Because this corpus far exceeds any context window, ASCENDEVOLVE indexes it and routes each agent to a small, task-specific slice of evidence instead of the corpus itself. The retrieved knowledge seeds diverse implementation strategies, guides repair when a candidate fails, and constrains how strong candidates are recombined. On 53 Ascend C operators from CANN-Bench spanning four difficulty levels, ASCENDEVOLVE achieves an overall score of 71.39, improving over Reflexion and OpenEvolve by 11.6% and 17.2%.

</details>


### [54] [CUA-Recovery: Benchmarking Error Recovery in Long-Horizon Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27645)
*Xu Hu,Haoming Li,Yuxiang Wang,Jinxin Yang,Kangshuo Li,Licheng Wang,Dingyi Kang,Jiayu Song,Tianhao Wang,Zhanhua Hu,Xu Yang,Qiannan Li,Bingzhe Li,Feng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

Comments: 33 pages, 13 figures, 18 tables

Selection reason: 长程 computer-use agent 的错误检测与恢复基准，含合成恢复轨迹训练，可直接迁移到 coding agent 的可靠性验证与自我纠错。

TL;DR: 提出 CUA-Recovery 基准与 ReRail 框架，利用真实策略产生的经确认失败构建长时程恢复轨迹，发现现有智能体错误识别与恢复能力较弱，而基于该训练套件微调可同时提升恢复和任务完成表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CUA 基准主要评估端到端任务性能，缺乏对执行中错误检测与恢复能力的深入评估；少量恢复基准又依赖人工注入错误，难以反映真实智能体策略导致的失败类型和时程。

Method: 构建 ReRail 框架，在持久桌面环境中将 MyPCBench 任务组合为新的长时程工作流，生成可执行真值，收集多种策略下经验证器确认的失败并转化为经过验证的恢复轨迹；据此构建包含测试套件和训练套件的 CUA-Recovery 基准。

Result: 测试套件包含 100 个任务上的 2,250 个经人工验证错误状态；训练套件包含 377 个任务上的 8.6K 条轨迹。六种智能体与六种接管深度下，EAR 最高 44.0%，Pass@3 最高 25.5%。微调 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 后，留出恢复状态平均 EAR 从 19.9% 升至 35.2%，Rubric Score 从 5.0% 升至 16.1%；MyPCBench 和 Odysseys 的 Pass@3 分别提升 15.3 分和 10.8 分。

Conclusion: CUA-Recovery 揭示了智能体在长时程工作流中识别继承错误并完成恢复任务的显著不足；基于真实失败构建的恢复训练轨迹不仅能提升错误恢复能力，也能提升从干净状态开始的任务完成表现。

中文摘要：随着计算机使用智能体（CUA）转向跨越多个应用的长时程工作流，未被检测到的错误可能在不被察觉的情况下破坏持久状态，并在后续动作中引发级联失败。可靠的错误检测与恢复已成为构建稳健 CUA 的关键瓶颈。然而，现有基准主要关注端到端任务性能，对智能体能否在执行过程中检测并恢复错误提供的洞察有限。少数评估恢复能力的基准在很大程度上依赖人工注入错误，这可能无法反映真实智能体策略所诱发的失败类型或失败时程。我们提出 CUA-Recovery，一个包含跨多个桌面应用的测试套件和训练套件的基准。我们使用 ReRail 构建它，ReRail 是一个用于在持久桌面环境中合成长期任务和轨迹的框架。ReRail 将 MyPCBench 任务组合为具有可执行真值的新长时程工作流，从多种策略中收集经验证器确认的失败，并将其转化为经过验证的恢复轨迹。CUA-Recovery 包含一个经人工验证的测试套件，覆盖 100 个任务上的 2,250 个错误状态，以及一个训练套件，覆盖 377 个任务上的 8.6K 条轨迹。在测试套件上的评估表明，智能体在识别继承错误和完成任务两方面都表现困难。在六种智能体和六种接管深度下，错误意识率（EAR）最高达到 44.0%，Pass@3 最高达到 25.5%。在训练套件上对 Qwen3.5-35B-A3B 进行微调后，其在留出恢复状态上的平均 EAR 从 19.9% 提升到 35.2%，Rubric Score 从 5.0% 提升到 16.1%。其 Pass@3 在 MyPCBench 上提升 15.3 分，在 Odysseys 上提升 10.8 分，表明这些训练轨迹既有助于恢复，也有助于从干净初始状态完成任务。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/xu-hu-2002/CUA-Recovery 获取。

Original abstract: As computer-use agents (CUAs) move to long-horizon workflows spanning multiple applications, undetected errors can silently corrupt persistent state and cause cascading failures in subsequent actions. Reliable error detection and recovery have become critical bottlenecks for building robust CUAs. However, existing benchmarks focus primarily on end-to-end task performance, providing limited insight into whether agents can detect and recover from errors during execution. The few benchmarks that evaluate recovery rely largely on manually injected errors, which may not reflect the types or horizons of failures induced by real agent policies. We introduce CUA-Recovery, a benchmark comprising test and training suites across multiple desktop applications. We construct it using ReRail, a framework for synthesizing long-horizon tasks and trajectories in a persistent desktop environment. ReRail composes MyPCBench tasks into new long-horizon workflows with executable ground truth, collects verifier-confirmed failures from multiple policies, and turns them into verified recovery trajectories. CUA-Recovery comprises a human-verified test suite of 2,250 erroneous states over 100 tasks and a training suite of 8.6K trajectories over 377 tasks. Evaluation on the test suite shows that agents struggle both to recognize inherited errors and to complete the task. Across six agents and six takeover depths, Error Awareness Rate (EAR) reaches at most 44.0% and Pass@3 at most 25.5%. After fine-tuning Qwen3.5-35B-A3B on the training suite, its average EAR rises from 19.9% to 35.2% on held-out recovery states, and its Rubric Score goes from 5.0% to 16.1%.
